class_name EnemyDefinition
extends Resource

## Định nghĩa thông số & cấu hình sinh vật (Enemy Definition) cho NOCTELYRA (Milestone M7)
## Hỗ trợ 3 kiểu tấn công (MELEE, LUNGE, RANGED), phạm vi đi tuần, leash distance,
## thời gian báo trước (telegraph) và thời gian nghỉ hồi phục (recovery).

enum AttackType {
	MELEE,
	LUNGE,
	RANGED
}

@export var enemy_type_id: String = "corrupted_wolf"
@export var display_name: String = "Quái Tha Hóa"
@export var max_health: float = 80.0
@export var move_speed: float = 120.0
@export var detection_range: float = 380.0
@export var attack_range: float = 55.0
@export var attack_damage: float = 15.0
@export var attack_cooldown: float = 1.2
@export var attack_type: AttackType = AttackType.MELEE

# Thông số hành vi & di chuyển mở rộng
@export var patrol_radius: float = 150.0 # Bán kính đi tuần quanh điểm sinh ban đầu
@export var leash_distance: float = 500.0 # Khoảng cách tối đa so với điểm sinh trước khi từ bỏ đuổi
@export var telegraph_duration: float = 0.4 # Thời gian báo trước khi ra đòn
@export var attack_duration: float = 0.25 # Thời gian hiệu lực đòn đánh
@export var recovery_duration: float = 0.6 # Thời gian nghỉ hồi phục sau đòn đánh
@export var lunge_speed: float = 450.0 # Vận tốc lao đòn lunge (đối với LUNGE)
@export var projectile_speed: float = 320.0 # Vận tốc đạn (đối với RANGED)
@export var projectile_damage: float = 12.0
@export var projectile_color: Color = Color(0.6, 0.4, 1.0, 1.0)
@export var is_flying: bool = false # Sinh vật bay/lơ lửng, không chịu trọng lực rơi
@export var drop_item_id: String = "" # Vật phẩm đánh rơi

static func get_definition(type_id: String) -> EnemyDefinition:
	return create_default(type_id)

static func create_default(type_id: String) -> EnemyDefinition:
	var def := EnemyDefinition.new()
	def.enemy_type_id = type_id

	match type_id:
		"corrupted_mooncap":
			# Nấm Mộng Tha Hóa (Thánh Lâm Elyndra)
			def.display_name = "Nấm Mộng Tha Hóa"
			def.max_health = 60.0
			def.move_speed = 80.0 # Tốc độ chậm
			def.detection_range = 300.0
			def.attack_range = 50.0
			def.attack_damage = 12.0
			def.attack_cooldown = 1.4
			def.attack_type = AttackType.MELEE
			def.patrol_radius = 120.0
			def.leash_distance = 420.0
			def.telegraph_duration = 0.45
			def.recovery_duration = 0.6
			def.is_flying = false

		"corrupted_mana_wisp":
			# Linh Hồn Tinh Mạch (Thánh Lâm Elyndra)
			def.display_name = "Linh Hồn Tinh Mạch"
			def.max_health = 35.0 # Ít HP
			def.move_speed = 60.0 # Di chuyển lơ lửng chậm
			def.detection_range = 380.0
			def.attack_range = 280.0 # Tấn công tầm xa
			def.attack_damage = 10.0
			def.attack_cooldown = 1.8
			def.attack_type = AttackType.RANGED
			def.patrol_radius = 100.0
			def.leash_distance = 450.0
			def.telegraph_duration = 0.7 # Có thời gian niệm chú
			def.recovery_duration = 0.8
			def.projectile_speed = 320.0
			def.projectile_damage = 12.0
			def.projectile_color = Color(0.6, 0.4, 1.0, 1.0) # Tím ma thuật
			def.is_flying = true # Lơ lửng, không rơi xuống đất

		"ashfang_wolf":
			# Sói Tro (Tiền Đồn Vharok)

			def.max_health = 80.0

class_name EnemyProjectile
extends Area2D

## Đạn ma pháp của quái vật (Enemy Projectile) cho NOCTELYRA (Milestone M7)
## Hỗ trợ đạn ma pháp của Linh Hồn Tinh Mạch và cầu lửa của Hỏa Tích Hắc Nham.

@export var speed: float = 300.0
@export var damage: float = 12.0
@export var lifetime: float = 3.0

var direction: Vector2 = Vector2.RIGHT
var source_enemy: Node = null
var _time_alive: float = 0.0

@onready var visual_core: ColorRect = $VisualCore
@onready var visual_glow: ColorRect = $VisualGlow

func _ready() -> void:
	add_to_group("enemy_projectiles")
	collision_layer = 0
	collision_mask = 2 # Player Hurtbox nằm ở Layer 2
	area_entered.connect(_on_area_entered)

func setup(dir: Vector2, dmg: float, spd: float, col: Color, src: Node = null) -> void:
	direction = dir.normalized() if dir != Vector2.ZERO else Vector2.RIGHT
	damage = dmg
	speed = spd
	source_enemy = src

	if visual_core:
		visual_core.color = col.lightened(0.3)
	if visual_glow:
		visual_glow.color = Color(col.r, col.g, col.b, 0.45)

func _physics_process(delta: float) -> void:
	global_position += direction * speed * delta
	_time_alive += delta
	if _time_alive >= lifetime:
		queue_free()

func _on_area_entered(area: Area2D) -> void:
	if area is Hurtbox and is_instance_valid(area):
		var dinfo := DamageInfo.new(damage, source_enemy)
		dinfo.knockback_dir = direction
		dinfo.knockback_force = 120.0
		area.apply_damage(dinfo)
		queue_free()

class_name EnemyVisual
extends Node2D

## Bộ điều khiển hiển thị đồ họa cho sinh vật (Enemy Visual Controller) cho NOCTELYRA (Milestone M7)
## Hỗ trợ 5 trạng thái hoạt ảnh: 'idle', 'move', 'attack', 'hit', 'death'.
## Dựng hình placeholder đặc trưng cho 4 chủng loại quái mới,
## sẵn sàng để thay thế bằng AnimatedSprite2D / SpriteFrames trong tương lai.

@export var enemy_type_id: String = "corrupted_wolf"

@onready var main_body: ColorRect = $MainBody
@onready var detail_node: Node2D = $DetailNode
@onready var eye_rect: ColorRect = $MainBody/EyeRect

var current_state: String = "idle"
var _base_color: Color = Color(0.8, 0.2, 0.2)
var _anim_time: float = 0.0

func _ready() -> void:
	apply_type_style(enemy_type_id)

func apply_type_style(type_id: String) -> void:
	enemy_type_id = type_id

	# Xóa các chi tiết cũ nếu có
	if detail_node:
		for c in detail_node.get_children():
			c.queue_free()

	match type_id:
		"corrupted_mooncap":
			# Nấm Mộng Tha Hóa: Thân nấm màu tím xanh, mũ nấm có đốm sáng
			_base_color = Color(0.55, 0.25, 0.85) # Tím ma thuật
			if main_body:
				main_body.size = Vector2(28, 26)
				main_body.position = Vector2(-14, -26)
				main_body.color = _base_color

			if eye_rect:
				eye_rect.color = Color(0.3, 0.9, 1.0) # Mắt xanh lam dễ thương
				eye_rect.position = Vector2(4, 8)
				eye_rect.size = Vector2(6, 6)

			# Thêm mũ nấm và các đốm sáng
			if detail_node:
				var cap := ColorRect.new()
				cap.name = "MushroomCap"
				cap.size = Vector2(38, 16)
				cap.position = Vector2(-19, -38)
				cap.color = Color(0.4, 0.2, 0.75)
				detail_node.add_child(cap)

				var spot1 := ColorRect.new()
				spot1.size = Vector2(6, 6)
				spot1.position = Vector2(-12, -34)
				spot1.color = Color(0.6, 0.9, 1.0, 0.9) # Đốm sáng xanh lam
				detail_node.add_child(spot1)

				var spot2 := ColorRect.new()
				spot2.size = Vector2(6, 6)
				spot2.position = Vector2(6, -34)
				spot2.color = Color(0.8, 0.6, 1.0, 0.9) # Đốm sáng tím nhạt
				detail_node.add_child(spot2)

		"corrupted_mana_wisp":
			# Linh Hồn Tinh Mạch: Khối sáng lơ lửng hình cầu ma pháp
			_base_color = Color(0.4, 0.6, 1.0, 0.85) # Lam tinh thạch
			if main_body:
				main_body.size = Vector2(24, 24)
				main_body.position = Vector2(-12, -28)
				main_body.color = _base_color

			if eye_rect:
				eye_rect.color = Color(0.9, 0.95, 1.0) # Lõi năng lượng phát sáng
				eye_rect.position = Vector2(6, 6)
				eye_rect.size = Vector2(12, 12)

			if detail_node:
				var halo := ColorRect.new()
				halo.name = "ManaHalo"
				halo.size = Vector2(34, 34)
				halo.position = Vector2(-17, -33)
				halo.color = Color(0.6, 0.3, 0.95, 0.35) # Hào quang tím bao quanh
				detail_node.add_child(halo)

		"ashfang_wolf":
			# Sói Tro: Thân đen xám, vệt ma lực cam đỏ
			_base_color = Color(0.22, 0.22, 0.26) # Lông tro đen xám
			if main_body:
				main_body.size = Vector2(40, 26)
				main_body.position = Vector2(-20, -26)
				main_body.color = _base_color

			if eye_rect:
				eye_rect.color = Color(1.0, 0.2, 0.1) # Mắt đỏ rực
				eye_rect.position = Vector2(6, 4)
				eye_rect.size = Vector2(8, 6)

			if detail_node:
				var streak := ColorRect.new()
				streak.name = "FireStreak"
				streak.size = Vector2(28, 6)
				streak.position = Vector2(-14, -18)
				streak.color = Color(1.0, 0.45, 0.1, 0.85) # Vệt ma lực đỏ cam
				detail_node.add_child(streak)

		"ember_lizard":
			# Hỏa Tích Hắc Nham: Thân thấp, đá đen nham thạch với vảy lửa
			_base_color = Color(0.18, 0.16, 0.2) # Nham thạch đen
			if main_body:
				main_body.size = Vector2(36, 18)
				main_body.position = Vector2(-18, -18)
				main_body.color = _base_color

			if eye_rect:
				eye_rect.color = Color(1.0, 0.7, 0.1) # Mắt vàng cam
				eye_rect.position = Vector2(6, 3)
				eye_rect.size = Vector2(6, 5)

			if detail_node:
				var crest := ColorRect.new()
				crest.name = "FireCrest"
				crest.size = Vector2(22, 6)
				crest.position = Vector2(-11, -24)
				crest.color = Color(1.0, 0.35, 0.05, 0.9) # Gai lưng hỏa thạch
				detail_node.add_child(crest)

		_:
			# Mặc định: Sói tha hóa Rừng Noctis
			_base_color = Color(0.8, 0.2, 0.2)
			if main_body:
				main_body.size = Vector2(32, 32)
				main_body.position = Vector2(-16, -32)
				main_body.color = _base_color

func _process(delta: float) -> void:
	_anim_time += delta
	# Hiệu ứng lơ lửng tự nhiên cho sinh vật bay (Wisp)
	if enemy_type_id == "corrupted_mana_wisp" and current_state != "death":
		position.y = sin(_anim_time * 3.5) * 6.0

func set_visual_state(state_name: String) -> void:
	current_state = state_name

	# Nếu có AnimatedSprite2D trong tương lai, gọi:
	var anim_sprite = get_node_or_null("AnimatedSprite2D")
	if anim_sprite and anim_sprite.has_method("play") and anim_sprite.sprite_frames and anim_sprite.sprite_frames.has_animation(state_name):
		anim_sprite.play(state_name)
		return

	# Xử lý hiệu ứng màu / hình dạng placeholder
	if main_body == null:
		return

	match state_name:
		"idle":
			main_body.color = _base_color
			scale = Vector2.ONE
		"move":
			main_body.color = _base_color.lightened(0.1)
		"telegraph":
			# Báo trước đòn đánh: nhấp nháy màu vàng / đỏ cảnh báo
			main_body.color = Color(1.0, 0.85, 0.2)
		"attack":
			main_body.color = Color(1.0, 0.3, 0.1)
		"hit":
			main_body.color = Color(1.0, 1.0, 1.0) # Flash trắng khi trúng đòn
		"death":
			main_body.color = Color(0.3, 0.3, 0.3, 0.6)

FILE: src\actors\npc\npc.gd

class_name NPC
extends CharacterBody2D

signal dialogue_requested(npc: NPC, dialogue_lines: Array[String], quest_action: String)

@export var npc_name: String = "Elyra"
@export var title: String = "Người Hướng Dẫn Làng Ánh Nguyệt"

@onready var interaction_component: InteractionComponent = $InteractionComponent
@onready var prompt_label: Label = $PromptLabel

func _ready() -> void:
	add_to_group("npcs")
	if prompt_label:
		prompt_label.visible = false
	if interaction_component:
		interaction_component.interaction_available.connect(_on_interaction_available)
		interaction_component.interaction_unavailable.connect(_on_interaction_unavailable)

func is_interactable() -> bool:
	return interaction_component != null and interaction_component.is_player_nearby

func _unhandled_input(event: InputEvent) -> void:
	# Nếu có InteractionCoordinator điều phối trung tâm thì để coordinator xử lý
	if get_tree() and get_tree().get_nodes_in_group("interaction_coordinator").size() > 0:
		return
	if is_interactable() and event.is_action_pressed("interact"):
		trigger_interaction()
		get_viewport().set_input_as_handled()

func trigger_interaction() -> void:
	# Không cho phép kích hoạt nếu hội thoại đang mở hoặc đang trong grace cooldown
	var cur_ui: GameUI = _get_game_ui()
	if cur_ui and (cur_ui.is_dialogue_open() or cur_ui.is_dialogue_cooling_down()):
		return

	var quest_mgr: QuestManager = _get_quest_manager()
	var player: Player = _get_player()
	var lines: Array[String] = []
	var action: String = "none"

	if npc_name == "Kaelis":
		var p_journal = player.quest_journal_component if player else null
		var q_state = QuestDefinition.QuestState.NOT_STARTED
		if p_journal:
			q_state = p_journal.get_quest_state("quest_elyndra_cleansing")
		match q_state:
			QuestDefinition.QuestState.NOT_STARTED:
				lines = [
					"Chào lữ khách từ Rừng Hắc Nguyệt. Ta là Kaelis, Người Canh Giữ Tinh Mạch của Thánh Lâm Elyndra.",
					"Ma khí bóng tối đang len lỏi vào các bụi nấm, biến chúng thành Nấm Ma Hóa làm ô nhiễm dòng chảy Tinh Mạch.",
					"Ngươi có thể giúp ta tiêu diệt 3 Nấm Ma Hóa (Corrupted Mooncap) để thanh tẩy vùng này không?"
				]
				action = "start_quest:quest_elyndra_cleansing"
			QuestDefinition.QuestState.ACTIVE:
				var prog: int = p_journal.get_quest_progress("quest_elyndra_cleansing") if p_journal else 0
				lines = [
					"Hãy cẩn trọng khi bước qua những cụm nấm phát sáng ma thuật.",
					"Tiến độ hiện tại: %d/3 Nấm Ma Hóa. Cố gắng lên nhé lữ khách!" % prog
				]
				action = "none"
			QuestDefinition.QuestState.READY_TO_TURN_IN:
				var inv := player.inventory_component if player else null
				if p_journal and not p_journal.can_turn_in("quest_elyndra_cleansing", inv):
					lines = [
						"Ngươi đã thanh tẩy Thánh Lâm, nhưng túi đồ của ngươi hiện đã đầy!",
						"Hãy dọn chỗ trống trong túi đồ trước khi nhận thưởng nhé."
					]
					action = "none"
				else:
					lines = [
						"Thật phi thường! Dòng chảy Tinh Mạch đã bắt đầu lưu thông thanh khiết trở lại.",
						"Xin hãy nhận 80 Vàng và 2 Bình Máu như lời cảm tạ chân thành từ Thánh Lâm Elyndra!"
					]
					action = "complete_quest:quest_elyndra_cleansing"
			QuestDefinition.QuestState.COMPLETED:
				lines = [
					"Cảm ơn ngươi đã bảo vệ Thánh Lâm. Nơi này từng là điểm giao thoa hòa bình giữa Tinh Linh và Quỷ tộc bán thú từ thời cổ đại.",
					"Quỷ tộc ở Tiền Đồn Vharok tuy thô ráp nhưng rất coi trọng danh dự và hiệp ước. Hãy ghé thăm họ nếu ngươi cần thêm thử thách."
				]
				action = "none"
	elif npc_name == "Rhaek":
		var p_journal = player.quest_journal_component if player else null
		var q_state = QuestDefinition.QuestState.NOT_STARTED
		if p_journal:
			var inv = player.inventory_component if player else null
			p_journal.update_item_collection(inv)
			q_state = p_journal.get_quest_state("quest_vharok_embers")
		match q_state:
			QuestDefinition.QuestState.NOT_STARTED:
				lines = [
					"Đứng lại, lữ khách! Ta là Rhaek, Chiến Binh Tiền Đồn của Gia tộc Hắc Nham Quỷ tộc.",
					"Lò rèn của Tiền Đồn Vharok đang cạn kiệt năng lượng. Chúng ta cần Lõi Tàn Hỏa (Ember Core) từ đá núi lửa để duy trì ngọn lửa rèn.",
					"Hãy thu thập giúp ta 3 Lõi Tàn Hỏa, ta sẽ thù lao xứng đáng cho ngươi!"
				]
				action = "start_quest:quest_vharok_embers"
			QuestDefinition.QuestState.ACTIVE:
				var prog: int = p_journal.get_quest_progress("quest_vharok_embers") if p_journal else 0
				lines = [
					"Ngươi có thể tìm Lõi Tàn Hỏa quanh các mỏm đá vách núi hoặc tiêu diệt Lũ Thằn Lằn Tro Tàn (Ember Lizard).",
					"Ngươi hiện có %d/3 Lõi Tàn Hỏa. Hãy mau tìm đủ mang về đây!" % prog
				]
				action = "none"
			QuestDefinition.QuestState.READY_TO_TURN_IN:
				var inv := player.inventory_component if player else null
				if p_journal and not p_journal.can_turn_in("quest_vharok_embers", inv):
					lines = [
						"Ngươi đã thu thập đủ Lõi Tàn Hỏa, nhưng túi đồ của ngươi chật cứng rồi!",
						"Hãy dọn chỗ trống trong túi đồ rồi quay lại đây nhận thưởng. Quỷ tộc không để bạn hữu chịu thiệt!"
					]
					action = "none"
				else:
					lines = [
						"Hahaha! Tốt lắm! Đúng là Lõi Tàn Hỏa rực lửa nguyên chất!",
						"Quỷ tộc bán thú luôn sòng phẳng: đây là 100 Vàng và 3 Mảnh Chân Ma dành cho lòng quả cảm của ngươi!"
					]
					action = "complete_quest:quest_vharok_embers"
			QuestDefinition.QuestState.COMPLETED:
				lines = [
					"Lò rèn của tiền đồn đã rực cháy trở lại! Ngươi đã chứng minh được dũng khí của một chiến binh thực thụ.",
					"Vùng đất cằn cỗi này còn nhiều mối đe dọa rình rập. Hãy giữ vững tinh thần cảnh giác, người bạn của Quỷ tộc!"
				]
				action = "none"
	elif quest_mgr and quest_mgr.current_quest and npc_name == "Elyra":
		var q_state = quest_mgr.current_quest.current_state
		match q_state:
			QuestData.State.NOT_STARTED:
				lines = [
					"Chào dũng sĩ trẻ! Ta là Elyra, người trông coi Làng Ánh Nguyệt.",
					"Ma lực sâu trong Rừng Hắc Nguyệt đang hỗn loạn bất thường, khiến các sinh vật hoang dã bị tha hóa.",
					"Ngươi có thể tiến vào rừng tiêu diệt 3 sinh vật bị ma hóa để bảo vệ ranh giới làng được không?"
				]
				action = "start_quest"
			QuestData.State.ACTIVE:
				lines = [
					"Hãy cẩn trọng khi bước qua ranh giới Rừng Hắc Nguyệt.",
					"Ngươi đã diệt được %d/%d sinh vật ma hóa. Mau hoàn thành giúp dân làng nhé!" % [quest_mgr.current_quest.current_amount, quest_mgr.current_quest.target_amount]
				]
				action = "none"
			QuestData.State.READY_TO_TURN_IN:
				lines = [
					"Ngươi đã trở về an toàn! Ma khí quanh bìa rừng đã lắng dịu đáng kể.",
					"Xin hãy nhận 50 Vàng và ta sẽ dùng quang ma pháp thanh tẩy, hồi phục trọn vẹn sinh lực cho ngươi!"
				]
				action = "complete_quest"
			QuestData.State.COMPLETED:
				lines = [
					"Cảm ơn ngươi đã bảo vệ Làng Ánh Nguyệt.",
					"Hãy tiếp tục rèn luyện kỹ năng kiếm thuật (J) và ma pháp (K) để sẵn sàng cho những thử thách lớn hơn phía trước!"
				]
				action = "none"
	else:
		lines = ["Xin chào dũng sĩ! Chúc ngươi một ngày bình an dưới ánh sáng mặt trăng."]

	emit_signal("dialogue_requested", self, lines, action)
	if cur_ui and not dialogue_requested.get_connections():
		cur_ui.show_dialogue(npc_name, lines, action)

func _get_quest_manager() -> QuestManager:
	var cur_scene = get_tree().current_scene if get_tree() else null
	if cur_scene:
		var mgr = cur_scene.get_node_or_null("QuestManager") as QuestManager
		if mgr:
			return mgr
	var p = get_parent()
	if p:
		var mgr = p.get_node_or_null("QuestManager") as QuestManager
		if mgr:
			return mgr
	return null

func _get_player() -> Player:
	var cur_scene = get_tree().current_scene if get_tree() else null
	if cur_scene:
		var p = cur_scene.get_node_or_null("Player") as Player
		if p:
			return p
	var par = get_parent()
	if par:
		var p = par.get_node_or_null("Player") as Player
		if p:
			return p
	if get_tree():
		var players = get_tree().get_nodes_in_group("player")
		if players.size() > 0:
			return players[0] as Player
	return null

func _get_game_ui() -> GameUI:
	var cur_scene = get_tree().current_scene if get_tree() else null
	if cur_scene:
		var ui = cur_scene.get_node_or_null("GameUI") as GameUI
		if ui:
			return ui
	var p = get_parent()
	if p:
		var ui = p.get_node_or_null("GameUI") as GameUI
		if ui:
			return ui
	return null

func _on_interaction_available(_node: Node) -> void:
	if prompt_label:
		prompt_label.visible = true

func _on_interaction_unavailable(_node: Node) -> void:
	if prompt_label:
		prompt_label.visible = false


===================================================

===================================================
FILE: src\actors\player\player.gd
===================================================

class_name Player
extends CharacterBody2D

signal state_changed(new_state_name: String)
signal player_died()
signal player_respawned()

enum State {
	IDLE,
	RUN,
	JUMP,
	FALL,
	DASH,
	DEAD
}

@export var movement_data: PlayerMovementData

@onready var sprite_root: Node2D = $SpriteRoot
@onready var anim_player: AnimationPlayer = $AnimationPlayer
@onready var collision_shape: CollisionShape2D = $CollisionShape2D
@onready var health_component: HealthComponent = $HealthComponent
@onready var combat_component: CombatComponent = $CombatComponent
@onready var hurtbox: Hurtbox = $Hurtbox
@onready var melee_hitbox: Hitbox = $MeleeHitbox
@onready var inventory_component: InventoryComponent = $InventoryComponent
@onready var equipment_component: EquipmentComponent = $EquipmentComponent
@onready var spellbook_component: SpellbookComponent = $SpellbookComponent
@onready var quest_journal_component: QuestJournalComponent = $QuestJournalComponent
@onready var progression_component: PlayerProgressionComponent = $PlayerProgressionComponent
@onready var player_stats_component: PlayerStatsComponent = $PlayerStatsComponent

var current_state: State = State.IDLE
var facing_direction: int = 1
var jumps_left: int = 2
var dash_timer: float = 0.0
var dash_cooldown_timer: float = 0.0
var dash_direction: int = 1
var spawn_position: Vector2 = Vector2.ZERO

var race_id: String:
	get:
		if player_stats_component:
			return player_stats_component.race_id
		return "human"
	set(val):
		_ensure_m8_components()
		if player_stats_component:
			player_stats_component.set_race(val)
		apply_race_visual(val)

var hurtbox_collision: CollisionShape2D:
	get:
		if hurtbox:
			return hurtbox.get_node_or_null("CollisionShape2D") as CollisionShape2D
		return null

var _movement_data_isolated: bool = false

func _enter_tree() -> void:
	_ensure_isolated_movement_data()

func _ensure_isolated_movement_data() -> void:
	if _movement_data_isolated:
		return
	if movement_data != null:
		movement_data = movement_data.duplicate()
	else:
		movement_data = PlayerMovementData.new()
	_movement_data_isolated = true

func get_race_base_modulate() -> Color:
	match race_id:
		"elf":
			return Color(0.88, 1.05, 0.95, 1.0)
		"demon_beast":
			return Color(1.05, 0.88, 0.95, 1.0)
		_:
			return Color(1.0, 1.0, 1.0, 1.0)

func _ready() -> void:
	_ensure_isolated_movement_data()
	spawn_position = global_position
	_ensure_m8_components()
	_change_state(State.IDLE)

	if health_component:
		health_component.invulnerability_started.connect(_on_invuln_started)
		health_component.invulnerability_ended.connect(_on_invuln_ended)
		health_component.died.connect(_on_player_died)

func _ensure_m8_components() -> void:
	if progression_component == null:
		progression_component = get_node_or_null("PlayerProgressionComponent") as PlayerProgressionComponent
		if progression_component == null:
			progression_component = PlayerProgressionComponent.new()
			progression_component.name = "PlayerProgressionComponent"
			add_child(progression_component)

	if player_stats_component == null:
		player_stats_component = get_node_or_null("PlayerStatsComponent") as PlayerStatsComponent
		if player_stats_component == null:
			player_stats_component = PlayerStatsComponent.new()
			player_stats_component.name = "PlayerStatsComponent"
			player_stats_component.player = self
			add_child(player_stats_component)

func apply_race_visual(r_id: String) -> void:
	if sprite_root == null:
		return
	var visual_badge := sprite_root.get_node_or_null("RaceVisualBadge") as Node2D
	if visual_badge == null:
		visual_badge = Node2D.new()
		visual_badge.name = "RaceVisualBadge"
		sprite_root.add_child(visual_badge)

	for c in visual_badge.get_children():
		c.queue_free()

	match r_id:
		"elf":
			sprite_root.modulate = Color(0.88, 1.05, 0.95, 1.0)
			var ear_left := Polygon2D.new()
			ear_left.polygon = PackedVector2Array([Vector2(-6, -98), Vector2(-15, -105), Vector2(-6, -101)])
			ear_left.color = Color(0.4, 0.95, 0.7, 0.9)
			visual_badge.add_child(ear_left)

			var ear_right := Polygon2D.new()
			ear_right.polygon = PackedVector2Array([Vector2(6, -98), Vector2(15, -105), Vector2(6, -101)])
			ear_right.color = Color(0.4, 0.95, 0.7, 0.9)
			visual_badge.add_child(ear_right)

			var aura := Line2D.new()
			aura.width = 2.0
			aura.default_color = Color(0.3, 1.0, 0.7, 0.5)
			var pts := PackedVector2Array()
			for i in range(13):
				var ang: float = float(i) * TAU / 12.0
				pts.append(Vector2(cos(ang) * 22.0, sin(ang) * 8.0 - 5.0))
			aura.points = pts
			visual_badge.add_child(aura)

		"demon_beast":
			sprite_root.modulate = Color(1.05, 0.88, 0.95, 1.0)
			var horn_left := Polygon2D.new()
			horn_left.polygon = PackedVector2Array([Vector2(-6, -106), Vector2(-12, -118), Vector2(-3, -110)])
			horn_left.color = Color(0.85, 0.2, 0.35, 0.95)
			visual_badge.add_child(horn_left)

			var horn_right := Polygon2D.new()
			horn_right.polygon = PackedVector2Array([Vector2(6, -106), Vector2(12, -118), Vector2(3, -110)])
			horn_right.color = Color(0.85, 0.2, 0.35, 0.95)
			visual_badge.add_child(horn_right)

			var tail := Line2D.new()
			tail.width = 3.0
			tail.default_color = Color(0.8, 0.25, 0.4, 0.9)
			tail.points = PackedVector2Array([Vector2(-8, -52), Vector2(-18, -56), Vector2(-24, -46), Vector2(-20, -38)])
			visual_badge.add_child(tail)

		_: # "human"
			sprite_root.modulate = Color(1.0, 1.0, 1.0, 1.0)
			var scarf := Line2D.new()
			scarf.width = 3.0
			scarf.default_color = Color(0.3, 0.6, 1.0, 0.8)
			scarf.points = PackedVector2Array([Vector2(-8, -86), Vector2(0, -84), Vector2(8, -86), Vector2(10, -74)])
			visual_badge.add_child(scarf)

func _physics_process(delta: float) -> void:
	# Khi chết: Vô hiệu hóa toàn bộ di chuyển, rơi và input
	if current_state == State.DEAD:
		velocity.x = move_toward(velocity.x, 0.0, 800.0 * delta)
		if not is_on_floor():
			velocity.y += movement_data.gravity * delta
		move_and_slide()
		return

	# Luôn giảm thời gian hồi chiêu Dash độc lập
	if dash_cooldown_timer > 0.0:
		dash_cooldown_timer -= delta

	# Kiểm tra nếu đang mở hội thoại NPC, vừa đóng, đang mở túi đồ, sổ nhiệm vụ hoặc thông tin nhân vật thì tạm ngừng nhận input
	var ui: GameUI = _get_game_ui()
	if ui and (ui.is_dialogue_open() or ui.is_dialogue_cooling_down() or (ui.has_method("is_ui_cooling_down") and ui.is_ui_cooling_down()) or ui.is_inventory_open() or ui.is_quest_journal_open() or (ui.has_method("is_character_sheet_open") and ui.is_character_sheet_open())):
		velocity.x = move_toward(velocity.x, 0.0, movement_data.friction * delta)
		if not is_on_floor():
			velocity.y += movement_data.gravity * delta
		move_and_slide()
		return

	# Xử lý input tấn công & ma pháp (J & K)
	_handle_combat_inputs()

	match current_state:
		State.DASH:
			# Trong lúc DASH: Giữ nguyên vận tốc lướt và không bị ghi đè bởi state khác
			dash_timer -= delta
			velocity.x = dash_direction * movement_data.dash_speed
			velocity.y = 0.0
			if dash_timer <= 0.0:
				if is_on_floor():
					var dir := _get_input_direction()
					if dir != 0.0:
						_change_state(State.RUN)
					else:
						_change_state(State.IDLE)
				else:
					_change_state(State.FALL)

		State.JUMP:
			# Ưu tiên Dash trên không hoặc Nhảy đôi
			if _check_dash():
				pass
			elif _check_double_jump():
				pass
			else:
				_apply_gravity(delta)
				_process_air_movement(delta)
				if velocity.y >= 0.0:
					_change_state(State.FALL)
				elif is_on_floor():
					_restore_jumps()
					if abs(velocity.x) > 10.0:
						_change_state(State.RUN)
					else:
						_change_state(State.IDLE)

		State.FALL:
			# Có thể Dash hoặc Nhảy đôi giữa lúc rơi
			if _check_dash():
				pass
			elif _check_double_jump():
				pass
			else:
				_apply_gravity(delta)
				_process_air_movement(delta)
				if is_on_floor():
					_restore_jumps()
					var dir := _get_input_direction()
					if dir != 0.0 or abs(velocity.x) > 10.0:
						_change_state(State.RUN)
					else:
						_change_state(State.IDLE)

		State.IDLE:
			_restore_jumps()
			if _check_dash():
				pass
			elif _check_jump():
				pass
			elif not is_on_floor():
				_change_state(State.FALL)
			else:
				_process_ground_movement(delta)
				if abs(velocity.x) > 10.0:
					_change_state(State.RUN)

		State.RUN:
			_restore_jumps()
			if _check_dash():
				pass
			elif _check_jump():
				pass
			elif not is_on_floor():
				_change_state(State.FALL)
			else:
				_process_ground_movement(delta)
				if abs(velocity.x) <= 10.0 and _get_input_direction() == 0.0:
					_change_state(State.IDLE)

	move_and_slide()

	# Khôi phục số lần nhảy khi đang chạm đất
	if is_on_floor() and current_state != State.JUMP and current_state != State.DEAD:
		_restore_jumps()

func _handle_combat_inputs() -> void:
	if combat_component == null:
		return

	# Phím 1, 2, 3: Chọn phép thuật, phím U: Nâng cấp phép
	var sb := spellbook_component
	if sb == null:
		sb = get_node_or_null("SpellbookComponent") as SpellbookComponent
	if sb:
		if Input.is_action_just_pressed("spell_1"):
			sb.select_spell_by_index(0)
		elif Input.is_action_just_pressed("spell_2"):
			sb.select_spell_by_index(1)
		elif Input.is_action_just_pressed("spell_3"):
			sb.select_spell_by_index(2)
		elif Input.is_action_just_pressed("spell_upgrade"):
			_upgrade_current_spell()

	if Input.is_action_just_pressed("attack"):
		combat_component.perform_attack(facing_direction)
	elif Input.is_action_just_pressed("cast_spell"):
		combat_component.cast_spell(self, facing_direction, health_component)

func _upgrade_current_spell() -> void:
	var sb := spellbook_component
	if sb == null:
		sb = get_node_or_null("SpellbookComponent") as SpellbookComponent
	var inv := inventory_component
	if inv == null:
		inv = get_node_or_null("InventoryComponent") as InventoryComponent
	if sb == null or inv == null:
		return
	if current_state == State.DEAD:
		return
	if get_tree().paused:
		return
	var ui := _get_game_ui()
	if ui and (ui.is_dialogue_open() or ui.is_dialogue_cooling_down() or ui.is_inventory_open() or ui.is_quest_journal_open() or (ui.has_method("is_character_sheet_open") and ui.is_character_sheet_open())):
		return
	var world := get_tree().current_scene
	var res := sb.upgrade_spell(sb.selected_spell_id, inv, world)
	if ui and res.has("message"):
		ui.show_notification(res["message"])

func _on_invuln_started() -> void:
	if sprite_root and current_state != State.DEAD:
		sprite_root.modulate = Color(1.0, 0.4, 0.4, 0.7)

func _on_invuln_ended() -> void:
	if sprite_root and current_state != State.DEAD:
		sprite_root.modulate = get_race_base_modulate()

func _on_player_died() -> void:
	_change_state(State.DEAD)
	velocity = Vector2.ZERO
	# Tắt hitbox tấn công
	if melee_hitbox:
		melee_hitbox.monitoring = false
		melee_hitbox.visible = false
	if combat_component:
		combat_component.end_attack()
	# Tắt nhận đòn
	if hurtbox:
		hurtbox.set_deferred("monitoring", false)
		hurtbox.set_deferred("monitorable", false)
	# Hiệu ứng mờ dần
	if sprite_root:
		sprite_root.modulate = Color(0.6, 0.2, 0.2, 0.6)

	# Dọn dẹp các projectile của Player đang bay để không gây tác động sau khi chết
	if get_tree():
		for p in get_tree().get_nodes_in_group("player_projectiles"):
			if is_instance_valid(p) and not p.is_queued_for_deletion():
				p.queue_free()

	emit_signal("player_died")

	var ui := _get_game_ui()
	if ui:
		ui.show_game_over()

func respawn() -> void:
	global_position = spawn_position
	velocity = Vector2.ZERO
	if health_component:
		health_component.current_health = health_component.max_health
		health_component.current_mana = health_component.max_mana
		health_component.is_invulnerable = false
		health_component.emit_signal("health_changed", health_component.current_health, health_component.max_health)
		health_component.emit_signal("mana_changed", health_component.current_mana, health_component.max_mana)
	if hurtbox:
		hurtbox.set_deferred("monitoring", true)
		hurtbox.set_deferred("monitorable", true)
	if sprite_root:
		sprite_root.modulate = get_race_base_modulate()

	if get_tree():
		for p in get_tree().get_nodes_in_group("player_projectiles"):
			if is_instance_valid(p) and not p.is_queued_for_deletion():
				p.queue_free()

	_restore_jumps()
	dash_timer = 0.0
	dash_cooldown_timer = 0.0
	_change_state(State.IDLE)
	emit_signal("player_respawned")

func _restore_jumps() -> void:
	jumps_left = movement_data.max_jumps

func _change_state(new_state: State) -> void:
	current_state = new_state
	var state_name := _get_state_name(current_state)
	emit_signal("state_changed", state_name)

	var has_player := (anim_player != null)

	match current_state:
		State.IDLE:
			if has_player and anim_player.has_animation("idle"):
				anim_player.play("idle")
		State.RUN:
			if has_player and anim_player.has_animation("run"):
				anim_player.play("run")
		State.JUMP:
			if has_player and anim_player.has_animation("jump"):
				anim_player.play("jump")
			else:
				_play_fallback_air_visual()
		State.FALL:
			if has_player and anim_player.has_animation("fall"):
				anim_player.play("fall")
			else:
				_play_fallback_air_visual()
		State.DASH:
			if has_player and anim_player.has_animation("dash"):
				anim_player.play("dash")
			else:
				_play_fallback_dash_visual()
			dash_timer = movement_data.dash_duration
			dash_cooldown_timer = movement_data.dash_cooldown
			
			var move_dir := int(sign(_get_input_direction()))
			if move_dir != 0:
				dash_direction = move_dir
				_set_facing(dash_direction)
			else:
				dash_direction = facing_direction
		State.DEAD:
			if anim_player:
				anim_player.pause()

func _play_fallback_air_visual() -> void:
	if anim_player and anim_player.has_animation("run"):
		anim_player.play("run")
		anim_player.seek(0.12, true)
		anim_player.pause()

func _play_fallback_dash_visual() -> void:
	if anim_player and anim_player.has_animation("run"):
		anim_player.play("run")
		anim_player.seek(0.48, true)
		anim_player.pause()

func _get_input_direction() -> float:
	return Input.get_axis("move_left", "move_right")

func _process_ground_movement(delta: float) -> void:
	var direction := _get_input_direction()
	if direction != 0.0:
		_set_facing(int(sign(direction)))
		velocity.x = move_toward(velocity.x, direction * movement_data.move_speed, movement_data.acceleration * delta)
	else:
		velocity.x = move_toward(velocity.x, 0.0, movement_data.friction * delta)

func _process_air_movement(delta: float) -> void:
	var direction := _get_input_direction()
	if direction != 0.0:
		_set_facing(int(sign(direction)))
		velocity.x = move_toward(velocity.x, direction * movement_data.move_speed, movement_data.air_acceleration * delta)
	else:
		velocity.x = move_toward(velocity.x, 0.0, movement_data.air_friction * delta)

func _apply_gravity(delta: float) -> void:
	velocity.y += movement_data.gravity * delta

func _check_jump() -> bool:
	if Input.is_action_just_pressed("jump"):
		if is_on_floor() or jumps_left == movement_data.max_jumps:
			velocity.y = movement_data.jump_velocity
			jumps_left = movement_data.max_jumps - 1
			_change_state(State.JUMP)
			return true
	return false

func _check_double_jump() -> bool:
	if Input.is_action_just_pressed("jump") and jumps_left > 0:
		velocity.y = movement_data.double_jump_velocity
		jumps_left -= 1
		_change_state(State.JUMP)
		return true
	return false

func _check_dash() -> bool:
	if Input.is_action_just_pressed("dash") and dash_cooldown_timer <= 0.0:
		_change_state(State.DASH)
		return true
	return false

func _set_facing(dir: int) -> void:
	if dir == 0 or dir == facing_direction:
		return
	facing_direction = dir
	if sprite_root:
		sprite_root.scale.x = float(facing_direction)

func _get_game_ui() -> GameUI:
	var scene = get_tree().current_scene
	if scene:
		return scene.get_node_or_null("GameUI") as GameUI
	return null

func _get_state_name(state: State) -> String:
	match state:
		State.IDLE: return "IDLE"
		State.RUN: return "RUN"
		State.JUMP: return "JUMP"
		State.FALL: return "FALL"
		State.DASH: return "DASH"
		State.DEAD: return "DEAD"
		_: return "UNKNOWN"


===================================================

===================================================

===================================================
FILE: src\boss\boss_lunareth.gd

class_name BossLunareth
extends CharacterBody2D

## Boss Lunareth — Linh Lộc Nguyệt Thực (M4)
## Linh thú bảo vệ Rừng Noctis bị ma lực hắc nguyệt tha hóa, cần được thanh tẩy.
## Quản lý State Machine FSM, hai đòn đánh, chuyển giai đoạn Phase 2, và cơ chế hồi sinh/reset.

const BossVisualScript = preload("res://src/boss/boss_visual.gd")

signal boss_state_changed(new_state_name: String)
signal boss_phase_changed(new_phase: int)
signal boss_health_changed(current: float, max_val: float)
signal boss_telegraph_started(skill_name: String, duration: float)
signal boss_defeated()

enum State {
	DORMANT,           # Chưa kích hoạt
	INTRO,             # Thức tỉnh / Thị uy khi Player bước vào đấu trường
	APPROACH,          # Tiếp cận Player
	TELEGRAPH,         # Cảnh báo trước đòn đánh (ít nhất 0.6s - 0.8s)
	ATTACK,            # Thi triển đòn đánh (Húc hoặc Sóng xung kích)
	RECOVERY,          # Thời gian nghỉ / Cơ hội phản công cho Player
	PHASE_TRANSITION,  # Chuyển đổi sang Giai đoạn 2 (khi HP <= 160)
	DEFEATED           # Đã thanh tẩy thành công
}

enum AttackType {
	HORN_CHARGE,       # Đòn 1: Nguyệt Giác Xung Kích (20 DMG, telegraph 0.7s)
	MOON_SHOCKWAVE     # Đòn 2: Hắc Nguyệt Chấn Ba (25 DMG, telegraph 0.9s)
}

@export var max_hp: float = 320.0
@export var move_speed_p1: float = 160.0
@export var move_speed_p2: float = 195.0
@export var charge_speed_p1: float = 550.0
@export var charge_speed_p2: float = 620.0
@export var recovery_time_p1: float = 1.4
@export var recovery_time_p2: float = 1.1 # Giảm ~21% thời gian nghỉ ở Phase 2
@export var gravity: float = 1100.0

var move_speed: float:
	get:
		return move_speed_p2 if is_phase_2 else move_speed_p1

@onready var health_component: HealthComponent = $HealthComponent
@onready var hurtbox: Hurtbox = $Hurtbox
@onready var charge_hitbox: Hitbox = $ChargeHitbox
@onready var boss_visual: Node2D = $BossVisual
@onready var collision_shape: CollisionShape2D = $CollisionShape2D

var current_state: State = State.DORMANT
var current_attack: AttackType = AttackType.HORN_CHARGE
var is_phase_2: bool = false
var facing_direction: int = -1
var target_player: Player = null

var _state_timer: float = 0.0
var _charge_timer: float = 0.0
var _phase_2_triggered: bool = false
var _is_defeated: bool = false
var _attack_alternate: bool = false
var _initial_spawn_position: Vector2 = Vector2.ZERO

var shockwave_scene: PackedScene = preload("res://src/boss/boss_shockwave.tscn")

func _ready() -> void:
	_initial_spawn_position = global_position
	if charge_hitbox:
		charge_hitbox.monitoring = false
		charge_hitbox.visible = false
		charge_hitbox.damage = 20.0
		charge_hitbox.owner_node = self

	if health_component:
		health_component.max_health = max_hp
		health_component.current_health = max_hp
		health_component.health_changed.connect(_on_health_changed)
		health_component.died.connect(_on_died)

	if hurtbox:
		hurtbox.hit_received.connect(_on_hit_received)

	_change_state(State.DORMANT)

func _physics_process(delta: float) -> void:
	if not is_on_floor():
		velocity.y += gravity * delta

	# Kiểm tra người chơi
	if target_player == null or not is_instance_valid(target_player):
		_find_player()

	match current_state:
		State.DORMANT:
			velocity.x = move_toward(velocity.x, 0.0, 600.0 * delta)
			move_and_slide()

		State.INTRO:
			velocity.x = move_toward(velocity.x, 0.0, 600.0 * delta)
			_state_timer -= delta
			if _state_timer <= 0.0:
				_change_state(State.APPROACH)
			move_and_slide()

		State.APPROACH:
			_process_approach(delta)

		State.TELEGRAPH:
			velocity.x = move_toward(velocity.x, 0.0, 600.0 * delta)
			_state_timer -= delta
			if _state_timer <= 0.0:
				_change_state(State.ATTACK)
			move_and_slide()

		State.ATTACK:
			_process_attack(delta)

		State.RECOVERY:
			velocity.x = move_toward(velocity.x, 0.0, 800.0 * delta)
			_state_timer -= delta
			# Boss không tấn công khi Player đã chết
			if _is_player_dead():
				_state_timer = max(_state_timer, 0.5)
			elif _state_timer <= 0.0:
				_change_state(State.APPROACH)
			move_and_slide()

		State.PHASE_TRANSITION:
			velocity.x = move_toward(velocity.x, 0.0, 800.0 * delta)
			_state_timer -= delta
			# Đảm bảo duy trì trạng thái bất tử trong suốt 1.3s chuyển phase
			if health_component:
				health_component.is_invulnerable = true
				if health_component.has_method("set_invulnerable_duration"):
					health_component.set_invulnerable_duration(_state_timer)
				else:
					health_component.set("_invuln_timer", _state_timer)
			if _state_timer <= 0.0:
				# Kết thúc chuyển giai đoạn: tắt bất tử ngay lập tức, Boss nhận sát thương bình thường
				if health_component:
					if health_component.has_method("cancel_invulnerability"):
						health_component.cancel_invulnerability()
					else:
						health_component.is_invulnerable = false
						health_component.set("_invuln_timer", 0.0)
				_change_state(State.RECOVERY)
			move_and_slide()

		State.DEFEATED:
			velocity.x = move_toward(velocity.x, 0.0, 900.0 * delta)
			move_and_slide()

func _find_player() -> void:
	var players = get_tree().get_nodes_in_group("players")
	if players.size() > 0 and players[0] is Player:
		target_player = players[0] as Player

func _is_player_dead() -> bool:
	if target_player == null or not is_instance_valid(target_player):
		return true
	var p_health: HealthComponent = target_player.get_node_or_null("HealthComponent")
	return p_health == null or not p_health.is_alive()

func _update_facing() -> void:
	if target_player and is_instance_valid(target_player):
		var dx: float = target_player.global_position.x - global_position.x
		if abs(dx) > 15.0:
			facing_direction = 1 if dx > 0 else -1
			if boss_visual and boss_visual.has_method("set_facing"):
				boss_visual.set_facing(facing_direction)
			if charge_hitbox:
				charge_hitbox.position.x = 40.0 * facing_direction

func _process_approach(delta: float) -> void:
	if _is_player_dead():
		velocity.x = move_toward(velocity.x, 0.0, 600.0 * delta)
		move_and_slide()
		return

	_update_facing()
	var spd: float = move_speed_p2 if is_phase_2 else move_speed_p1
	velocity.x = facing_direction * spd

	var dist: float = abs(target_player.global_position.x - global_position.x)
	_state_timer -= delta

	# Quyết định tấn công nếu đã tiếp cận đủ gần hoặc timer tiếp cận hết hạn (~2.0s)
	if dist <= 320.0 or _state_timer <= 0.0:
		_decide_next_attack(dist)
		_change_state(State.TELEGRAPH)

	move_and_slide()

func _decide_next_attack(dist: float) -> void:
	# Luân phiên đòn đánh để tạo nhịp điệu chiến đấu công bằng
	_attack_alternate = not _attack_alternate
	if dist > 380.0:
		# Khoảng cách xa: Ưu tiên Nguyệt Giác Xung Kích
		current_attack = AttackType.HORN_CHARGE
	elif dist < 180.0:
		# Khoảng cách gần: Ưu tiên Hắc Nguyệt Chấn Ba
		current_attack = AttackType.MOON_SHOCKWAVE
	else:
		current_attack = AttackType.HORN_CHARGE if _attack_alternate else AttackType.MOON_SHOCKWAVE

func _process_attack(delta: float) -> void:
	match current_attack:
		AttackType.HORN_CHARGE:
			var charge_spd: float = charge_speed_p2 if is_phase_2 else charge_speed_p1
			velocity.x = facing_direction * charge_spd
			_charge_timer -= delta
			if _charge_timer <= 0.0:
				_finish_attack()
			move_and_slide()
		AttackType.MOON_SHOCKWAVE:
			# Đòn chấn ba đã spawn khi vào state ATTACK
			velocity.x = move_toward(velocity.x, 0.0, 800.0 * delta)
			_state_timer -= delta
			if _state_timer <= 0.0:
				_finish_attack()
			move_and_slide()

func _finish_attack() -> void:
	if charge_hitbox:
		charge_hitbox.monitoring = false
		charge_hitbox.visible = false
	_change_state(State.RECOVERY)

func _change_state(new_state: State) -> void:
	current_state = new_state
	var state_name: String = _get_state_name(current_state)
	emit_signal("boss_state_changed", state_name)

	match current_state:
		State.DORMANT:
			if boss_visual and boss_visual.has_method("set_state"):
				boss_visual.set_state(BossVisualScript.VisualState.IDLE)
			if hurtbox:
				hurtbox.set_deferred("monitoring", false)
				hurtbox.set_deferred("monitorable", false)

		State.INTRO:
			_state_timer = 1.2
			if boss_visual and boss_visual.has_method("set_state"):
				boss_visual.set_state(BossVisualScript.VisualState.IDLE)
			if hurtbox:
				hurtbox.set_deferred("monitoring", true)
				hurtbox.set_deferred("monitorable", true)

		State.APPROACH:
			_state_timer = 2.2
			if boss_visual and boss_visual.has_method("set_state"):
				boss_visual.set_state(BossVisualScript.VisualState.MOVE)

		State.TELEGRAPH:
			_update_facing()
			if current_attack == AttackType.HORN_CHARGE:
				_state_timer = 0.7 # Đạt chuẩn tối thiểu 0.6s
				if boss_visual and boss_visual.has_method("set_state"):
					boss_visual.set_state(BossVisualScript.VisualState.TELEGRAPH_CHARGE)
				emit_signal("boss_telegraph_started", "Nguyệt Giác Xung Kích", _state_timer)
			else:
				_state_timer = 0.9 # Đạt chuẩn tối thiểu 0.8s
				if boss_visual and boss_visual.has_method("set_state"):
					boss_visual.set_state(BossVisualScript.VisualState.TELEGRAPH_SHOCKWAVE)
				emit_signal("boss_telegraph_started", "Hắc Nguyệt Chấn Ba", _state_timer)

		State.ATTACK:
			if current_attack == AttackType.HORN_CHARGE:
				_charge_timer = 0.45
				if charge_hitbox:
					charge_hitbox.reset_hits()
					charge_hitbox.damage = 20.0
					charge_hitbox.monitoring = true
					charge_hitbox.visible = true
				if boss_visual and boss_visual.has_method("set_state"):
					boss_visual.set_state(BossVisualScript.VisualState.ATTACK_CHARGE)
			else:
				_state_timer = 0.35
				_spawn_shockwaves()
				if boss_visual and boss_visual.has_method("set_state"):
					boss_visual.set_state(BossVisualScript.VisualState.ATTACK_STOMP)

		State.RECOVERY:
			_state_timer = recovery_time_p2 if is_phase_2 else recovery_time_p1
			if boss_visual and boss_visual.has_method("set_state"):
				boss_visual.set_state(BossVisualScript.VisualState.RECOVERY)

		State.PHASE_TRANSITION:
			_state_timer = 1.3
			is_phase_2 = true
			if health_component:
				if health_component.has_method("set_invulnerable_duration"):
					health_component.set_invulnerable_duration(1.3)
				else:
					health_component.is_invulnerable = true
					health_component.set("_invuln_timer", 1.3)
			if boss_visual and boss_visual.has_method("set_state"):
				boss_visual.set_state(BossVisualScript.VisualState.PHASE_TRANSITION)
			emit_signal("boss_phase_changed", 2)

		State.DEFEATED:
			_is_defeated = true
			velocity = Vector2.ZERO
			if charge_hitbox:
				charge_hitbox.monitoring = false
				charge_hitbox.visible = false
			if hurtbox:
				hurtbox.monitoring = false
				hurtbox.monitorable = false
				var h_col := hurtbox.get_node_or_null("CollisionShape2D") as CollisionShape2D
				if h_col:
					h_col.disabled = true
			if health_component:
				health_component.is_invulnerable = true
			if boss_visual and boss_visual.has_method("set_state"):
				boss_visual.set_state(BossVisualScript.VisualState.DEFEATED)
			emit_signal("boss_defeated")

func _spawn_shockwaves() -> void:
	if shockwave_scene == null:
		return
	var wave_dir: float = float(facing_direction)
	var sw = shockwave_scene.instantiate()
	if sw is Node2D:
		var parent_node: Node = get_parent()
		if parent_node:
			parent_node.add_child(sw)
		sw.global_position = Vector2(global_position.x + wave_dir * 30.0, global_position.y)
		if sw.has_method("setup"):
			sw.setup(wave_dir, is_phase_2)

func _on_hit_received(damage_info: DamageInfo) -> void:
	if current_state == State.DORMANT or current_state == State.DEFEATED or current_state == State.PHASE_TRANSITION:
		return
	if boss_visual and boss_visual.has_method("flash_hit"):
		boss_visual.flash_hit()

func _on_health_changed(cur: float, max_v: float) -> void:
	emit_signal("boss_health_changed", cur, max_v)

	# Kích hoạt Phase 2 khi HP <= 50% (<= 160) - Chỉ kích hoạt duy nhất một lần
	if not _phase_2_triggered and cur <= (max_v * 0.5) and cur > 0.0:
		_phase_2_triggered = true
		_change_state(State.PHASE_TRANSITION)

func _on_died() -> void:
	if not _is_defeated:
		_change_state(State.DEFEATED)

func activate_boss() -> void:
	if current_state == State.DORMANT and not _is_defeated:
		_change_state(State.INTRO)

func apply_slow(_ratio: float, _duration: float) -> void:
	# Boss Lunareth là linh thú ma pháp cổ xưa, miễn nhiễm hoàn toàn với hiệu ứng làm chậm
	pass

func reset_boss() -> void:
	global_position = _initial_spawn_position
	velocity = Vector2.ZERO
	_phase_2_triggered = false
	_is_defeated = false
	is_phase_2 = false
	_state_timer = 0.0
	_charge_timer = 0.0
	_attack_alternate = false

	if health_component:
		health_component.current_health = max_hp
		if health_component.has_method("cancel_invulnerability"):
			health_component.cancel_invulnerability()
		else:
			health_component.is_invulnerable = false
			health_component.set("_invuln_timer", 0.0)
		emit_signal("boss_health_changed", max_hp, max_hp)

	if charge_hitbox:
		charge_hitbox.monitoring = false
		charge_hitbox.visible = false
		charge_hitbox.reset_hits()

	if hurtbox:
		hurtbox.set_deferred("monitoring", true)
		hurtbox.set_deferred("monitorable", true)
		var h_col := hurtbox.get_node_or_null("CollisionShape2D") as CollisionShape2D
		if h_col:
			h_col.disabled = false

	if boss_visual:
		if boss_visual.has_method("reset_visual"):
			boss_visual.reset_visual()
		elif boss_visual.has_method("set_state"):
			boss_visual.set("is_phase_2", false)
			boss_visual.set_state(BossVisualScript.VisualState.IDLE)

	emit_signal("boss_phase_changed", 1)
	_change_state(State.DORMANT)

func purify_boss() -> void:
	_is_defeated = true
	_phase_2_triggered = true
	current_state = State.DEFEATED
	velocity = Vector2.ZERO
	if health_component:
		health_component.current_health = 0.0
		health_component.is_invulnerable = true
	if charge_hitbox:
		charge_hitbox.monitoring = false
		charge_hitbox.visible = false
	if hurtbox:
		hurtbox.monitoring = false
		hurtbox.monitorable = false
		var h_col := hurtbox.get_node_or_null("CollisionShape2D") as CollisionShape2D
		if h_col:
			h_col.disabled = true
	if boss_visual and boss_visual.has_method("set_state"):
		boss_visual.set_state(BossVisualScript.VisualState.DEFEATED)
	emit_signal("boss_state_changed", "DEFEATED")

func _get_state_name(st: State) -> String:
	match st:
		State.DORMANT: return "DORMANT"
		State.INTRO: return "INTRO"
		State.APPROACH: return "APPROACH"
		State.TELEGRAPH: return "TELEGRAPH"
		State.ATTACK: return "ATTACK"
		State.RECOVERY: return "RECOVERY"
		State.PHASE_TRANSITION: return "PHASE_TRANSITION"
		State.DEFEATED: return "DEFEATED"
		_: return "UNKNOWN"

===================================================
FILE: src\boss\boss_shockwave.gd

class_name BossShockwave
extends Area2D

## Sóng ma lực Hắc Nguyệt Chấn Ba của Boss Lunareth (M4)
## Lan truyền trên mặt đất, gây 25 sát thương, có thể nhảy qua để né.

@export var speed: float = 380.0
@export var damage: float = 25.0
@export var knockback_force: float = 240.0
@export var direction: float = -1.0
@export var max_lifetime: float = 2.8

var is_phase_2: bool = false
var _time_alive: float = 0.0
var _hit_nodes: Array[Node] = []

@onready var visual_wave: ColorRect = $VisualWave
@onready var core_glow: ColorRect = $VisualWave/CoreGlow

func _ready() -> void:
	add_to_group("boss_projectiles")
	collision_layer = 0
	collision_mask = 2 # Player Hurtbox nằm trên Layer 2
	area_entered.connect(_on_area_entered)
	_apply_visuals()

func setup(p_direction: float, p_is_phase_2: bool = false) -> void:
	direction = sign(p_direction)
	if direction == 0.0:
		direction = -1.0
	is_phase_2 = p_is_phase_2
	speed = 440.0 if is_phase_2 else 380.0
	_apply_visuals()

func _apply_visuals() -> void:
	var wave_node: ColorRect = visual_wave if visual_wave else get_node_or_null("VisualWave") as ColorRect
	var glow_node: ColorRect = core_glow if core_glow else get_node_or_null("VisualWave/CoreGlow") as ColorRect

	if wave_node:
		if is_phase_2:
			wave_node.color = Color(0.95, 0.15, 0.85, 0.9)
		else:
			wave_node.color = Color(0.3, 0.75, 1.0, 0.9)

	if glow_node:
		if is_phase_2:
			glow_node.color = Color(1.0, 0.7, 1.0, 1.0)
		else:
			glow_node.color = Color(0.8, 0.95, 1.0, 1.0)

func _physics_process(delta: float) -> void:
	_time_alive += delta
	position.x += direction * speed * delta

	# Hiệu ứng gợn sóng nhấp nhô
	var wave_node: ColorRect = visual_wave if visual_wave else get_node_or_null("VisualWave") as ColorRect
	if wave_node:
		wave_node.scale.y = 1.0 + sin(_time_alive * 25.0) * 0.15

	if _time_alive >= max_lifetime:
		queue_free()

func _on_area_entered(area: Area2D) -> void:
	if area is Hurtbox:
		var hurtbox := area as Hurtbox
		if hurtbox.owner_node in _hit_nodes:
			return # Đã trúng rồi, không gây sát thương trùng

		var knock_dir := Vector2(direction, -0.3).normalized()
		var dmg_info := DamageInfo.new(damage, self, knock_dir, knockback_force)
		var success := hurtbox.apply_damage(dmg_info)
		if success:
			_hit_nodes.append(hurtbox.owner_node)
			# Biến mất sau khi đâm trúng
			queue_free()

===================================================
FILE: src\boss\boss_visual.gd
===================================================

class_name BossVisual
extends Node2D

## Bộ điều khiển hiển thị độc lập cho Boss Lunareth (M4)
## Cho phép phân tách hoàn toàn giữa logic FSM, Hitbox và Đồ họa.
## Dễ dàng thay thế bằng SpriteSheet trong tương lai mà không ảnh hưởng AI.

enum VisualState {
	IDLE,
	MOVE,
	TELEGRAPH_CHARGE,
	TELEGRAPH_SHOCKWAVE,
	ATTACK_CHARGE,
	ATTACK_STOMP,
	RECOVERY,
	PHASE_TRANSITION,
	DEFEATED
}

@onready var visual_root: Node2D = $VisualRoot
@onready var body_parts: Node2D = $VisualRoot/BodyParts
@onready var antlers_root: Node2D = $VisualRoot/BodyParts/Head/Antlers
@onready var eye: ColorRect = $VisualRoot/BodyParts/Head/Eye
@onready var lunar_mark: ColorRect = $VisualRoot/BodyParts/Head/LunarMark
@onready var telegraph_charge_rect: ColorRect = $TelegraphCharge
@onready var telegraph_shockwave_ring: ColorRect = $TelegraphShockwave
@onready var aura_rect: ColorRect = $VisualRoot/AuraRect

var current_visual_state: VisualState = VisualState.IDLE
var is_phase_2: bool = false
var _anim_timer: float = 0.0
var _hit_flash_timer: float = 0.0

# Bảng màu Phase 1 (Lam Nguyệt / Cyan Indigo)
const P1_BODY_COLOR: Color = Color(0.12, 0.18, 0.32, 1.0)
const P1_ANTLER_COLOR: Color = Color(0.25, 0.85, 0.95, 1.0)
const P1_AURA_COLOR: Color = Color(0.2, 0.6, 0.9, 0.25)

# Bảng màu Phase 2 (Hắc Nguyệt / Dark Eclipse Violet)
const P2_BODY_COLOR: Color = Color(0.22, 0.08, 0.28, 1.0)
const P2_ANTLER_COLOR: Color = Color(0.95, 0.2, 0.85, 1.0)
const P2_AURA_COLOR: Color = Color(0.8, 0.1, 0.6, 0.35)

# Bảng màu Thanh tẩy thành công (Moonlight Serene)
const CLEANSED_BODY_COLOR: Color = Color(0.88, 0.95, 1.0, 1.0)
const CLEANSED_ANTLER_COLOR: Color = Color(0.5, 0.9, 1.0, 0.9)
const CLEANSED_AURA_COLOR: Color = Color(1.0, 0.9, 0.6, 0.3)

func _ready() -> void:
	if telegraph_charge_rect:
		telegraph_charge_rect.visible = false
	if telegraph_shockwave_ring:
		telegraph_shockwave_ring.visible = false
	_apply_palette()

func reset_visual() -> void:
	is_phase_2 = false
	current_visual_state = VisualState.IDLE
	_anim_timer = 0.0
	_hit_flash_timer = 0.0
	if telegraph_charge_rect:
		telegraph_charge_rect.visible = false
	if telegraph_shockwave_ring:
		telegraph_shockwave_ring.visible = false
	if body_parts:
		body_parts.position = Vector2.ZERO
		body_parts.rotation = 0.0
		body_parts.modulate = Color(1.0, 1.0, 1.0, 1.0)
	if antlers_root:
		antlers_root.rotation = 0.0
	_apply_palette()

func _process(delta: float) -> void:
	_anim_timer += delta

	# Xử lý chớp trắng khi nhận đòn (Hit Flash)
	if _hit_flash_timer > 0.0:
		_hit_flash_timer -= delta
		if _hit_flash_timer <= 0.0:
			_apply_palette()

	# Hoạt ảnh thở / di chuyển / trạng thái
	match current_visual_state:
		VisualState.IDLE, VisualState.RECOVERY:
			var bob: float = sin(_anim_timer * 3.0) * 3.0
			body_parts.position.y = bob
			if antlers_root:
				antlers_root.rotation = sin(_anim_timer * 2.0) * 0.05
		VisualState.MOVE:
			var trot: float = sin(_anim_timer * 10.0) * 5.0
			body_parts.position.y = trot
			if antlers_root:
				antlers_root.rotation = sin(_anim_timer * 10.0) * 0.1
		VisualState.TELEGRAPH_CHARGE, VisualState.TELEGRAPH_SHOCKWAVE:
			# Chớp đỏ cảnh báo
			var pulse: float = (sin(_anim_timer * 16.0) + 1.0) * 0.5
			if telegraph_charge_rect and telegraph_charge_rect.visible:
				telegraph_charge_rect.modulate.a = 0.3 + pulse * 0.4
			if telegraph_shockwave_ring and telegraph_shockwave_ring.visible:
				telegraph_shockwave_ring.modulate.a = 0.3 + pulse * 0.4
		VisualState.PHASE_TRANSITION:
			# Rung lắc giải phóng ma lực
			body_parts.position.x = randf_range(-4.0, 4.0)
			body_parts.position.y = randf_range(-4.0, 4.0)
			if aura_rect:
				aura_rect.modulate.a = 0.5 + sin(_anim_timer * 20.0) * 0.3
		VisualState.DEFEATED:
			# Thả lỏng nằm nghỉ ngơi an lành
			body_parts.position.y = 20.0
			body_parts.position.x = 0.0
			if antlers_root:
				antlers_root.rotation = -0.2

func set_facing(dir: int) -> void:
	if visual_root:
		visual_root.scale.x = 1.0 if dir <= 0 else -1.0
	if telegraph_charge_rect:
		telegraph_charge_rect.scale.x = 1.0 if dir <= 0 else -1.0

func set_state(state: VisualState) -> void:
	current_visual_state = state
	if telegraph_charge_rect:
		telegraph_charge_rect.visible = (state == VisualState.TELEGRAPH_CHARGE)
	if telegraph_shockwave_ring:
		telegraph_shockwave_ring.visible = (state == VisualState.TELEGRAPH_SHOCKWAVE)

	if state == VisualState.PHASE_TRANSITION:
		is_phase_2 = true
		_apply_palette()
	elif state == VisualState.DEFEATED:
		_apply_defeated_palette()

func flash_hit() -> void:
	_hit_flash_timer = 0.15
	if body_parts:
		body_parts.modulate = Color(2.5, 2.5, 2.5, 1.0)

func _apply_palette() -> void:
	var body_col: Color = P2_BODY_COLOR if is_phase_2 else P1_BODY_COLOR
	var antler_col: Color = P2_ANTLER_COLOR if is_phase_2 else P1_ANTLER_COLOR
	var aura_col: Color = P2_AURA_COLOR if is_phase_2 else P1_AURA_COLOR

	if body_parts:
		body_parts.modulate = Color(1.0, 1.0, 1.0, 1.0)
		for child in body_parts.get_children():
			if child is ColorRect:
				child.color = body_col
	if antlers_root:
		for child in antlers_root.get_children():
			if child is ColorRect:
				child.color = antler_col
	if lunar_mark:
		lunar_mark.color = antler_col
	if aura_rect:
		aura_rect.color = aura_col

func _apply_defeated_palette() -> void:
	if body_parts:
		body_parts.modulate = Color(1.0, 1.0, 1.0, 1.0)
		for child in body_parts.get_children():
			if child is ColorRect:
				child.color = CLEANSED_BODY_COLOR
	if antlers_root:
		for child in antlers_root.get_children():
			if child is ColorRect:
				child.color = CLEANSED_ANTLER_COLOR
	if lunar_mark:
		lunar_mark.color = CLEANSED_ANTLER_COLOR
	if aura_rect:
		aura_rect.color = CLEANSED_AURA_COLOR


===================================================
FILE: src\combat\combat_component.gd
===================================================

class_name CombatComponent
extends Node

signal attack_started()
signal attack_finished()
signal spell_cast()

@export var attack_cooldown: float = 0.35
@export var spell_cooldown: float = 0.6
@export var spell_mana_cost: float = 15.0
@export var melee_hitbox: Hitbox
@export var projectile_scene: PackedScene = preload("res://src/combat/magic_projectile.tscn")
@export var projectile_spawn_offset: Vector2 = Vector2(30, -40)

var _attack_timer: float = 0.0
var _spell_timer: float = 0.0
var _is_attacking: bool = false
var _attack_anim_duration: float = 0.25

func _ready() -> void:
	if melee_hitbox:
		melee_hitbox.monitoring = false
		melee_hitbox.visible = false

func _process(delta: float) -> void:
	if _attack_timer > 0.0:
		_attack_timer -= delta
	if _spell_timer > 0.0:
		_spell_timer -= delta

	if _is_attacking:
		_attack_anim_duration -= delta
		if _attack_anim_duration <= 0.0:
			end_attack()

func can_attack() -> bool:
	return _attack_timer <= 0.0 and not _is_attacking

func can_cast_spell(health_comp: HealthComponent) -> bool:
	if _is_attacking:
		return false
	var owner_node := get_parent() as Node2D
	var spellbook: SpellbookComponent = null
	if owner_node:
		spellbook = owner_node.get_node_or_null("SpellbookComponent") as SpellbookComponent
	if spellbook:
		var check := spellbook.can_cast_selected(health_comp)
		return bool(check.get("can_cast", false))
	if _spell_timer > 0.0:
		return false
	if health_comp and health_comp.current_mana < spell_mana_cost:
		return false
	return true

func perform_attack(facing_dir: int) -> bool:
	if not can_attack():
		return false

	_is_attacking = true
	_attack_timer = attack_cooldown
	_attack_anim_duration = 0.22

	if melee_hitbox:
		melee_hitbox.reset_hits()
		melee_hitbox.monitoring = true
		melee_hitbox.visible = true
		# Căn chỉnh hitbox theo hướng mặt
		melee_hitbox.position.x = abs(melee_hitbox.position.x) * float(facing_dir)

	emit_signal("attack_started")
	return true

func end_attack() -> void:
	_is_attacking = false
	if melee_hitbox:
		melee_hitbox.monitoring = false
		melee_hitbox.visible = false
	emit_signal("attack_finished")

func cast_spell(owner_node: Node2D, facing_dir: int, health_comp: HealthComponent) -> bool:
	if _is_attacking:
		return false

	var spellbook: SpellbookComponent = null
	if owner_node:
		spellbook = owner_node.get_node_or_null("SpellbookComponent") as SpellbookComponent

	if spellbook:
		var success := spellbook.cast_selected_spell(owner_node, facing_dir, health_comp)
		if success:
			emit_signal("spell_cast")
		return success

	if not can_cast_spell(health_comp):
		return false

	if health_comp:
		var success := health_comp.consume_mana(spell_mana_cost)
		if not success:
			return false

	_spell_timer = spell_cooldown

	if projectile_scene and owner_node:
		var proj := projectile_scene.instantiate() as MagicProjectile
		var spawn_pos := owner_node.global_position + Vector2(projectile_spawn_offset.x * float(facing_dir), projectile_spawn_offset.y)
		proj.global_position = spawn_pos
		proj.setup(Vector2(float(facing_dir), 0.0), owner_node)
		var spawn_root: Node = owner_node.get_parent()
		if owner_node.get_tree() and owner_node.get_tree().current_scene:
			spawn_root = owner_node.get_tree().current_scene
		if spawn_root:
			spawn_root.add_child.call_deferred(proj)

	emit_signal("spell_cast")
	return true

func is_attacking() -> bool:
	return _is_attacking

class_name DamageInfo
extends RefCounted

var knockback_force: float = 0.0

func _init(p_amount: float = 10.0, p_source: Node = null, p_knockback_dir: Vector2 = Vector2.ZERO, p_knockback_force: float = 0.0) -> void:
	amount = p_amount
	source = p_source
	knockback_dir = p_knockback_dir
	knockback_force = p_knockback_force

FILE: src\combat\health_component.gd
===================================================

class_name HealthComponent
extends Node

signal health_changed(current: float, max_val: float)
signal mana_changed(current: float, max_val: float)
signal damaged(amount: float, source: Node)
signal died()
signal invulnerability_started()
signal invulnerability_ended()

@export var max_health: float = 100.0
@export var current_health: float = 100.0
@export var max_mana: float = 50.0
@export var current_mana: float = 50.0
@export var mana_regen_rate: float = 5.0 # MP hồi phục mỗi giây
@export var invulnerability_duration: float = 0.6 # Thời gian miễn nhiễm sát thương sau khi trúng đòn

var is_invulnerable: bool = false
var _invuln_timer: float = 0.0

func _ready() -> void:
	current_health = clamp(current_health, 0.0, max_health)
	current_mana = clamp(current_mana, 0.0, max_mana)
	emit_signal("health_changed", current_health, max_health)
	emit_signal("mana_changed", current_mana, max_mana)

func _process(delta: float) -> void:
	# Cập nhật thời gian miễn nhiễm sát thương
	if is_invulnerable:
		_invuln_timer -= delta
		if _invuln_timer <= 0.0:
			is_invulnerable = false
			emit_signal("invulnerability_ended")

	# Tự động hồi MP dần theo thời gian nếu chưa đầy
	if mana_regen_rate > 0.0 and current_mana < max_mana:
		current_mana = min(max_mana, current_mana + mana_regen_rate * delta)
		emit_signal("mana_changed", current_mana, max_mana)

func set_invulnerable_duration(duration: float) -> void:
	is_invulnerable = true
	_invuln_timer = max(_invuln_timer, duration)
	emit_signal("invulnerability_started")

func cancel_invulnerability() -> void:
	is_invulnerable = false
	_invuln_timer = 0.0
	emit_signal("invulnerability_ended")

func take_damage(damage: DamageInfo) -> bool:
	if is_invulnerable or current_health <= 0.0:
		return false

	var final_dmg := damage.amount
	current_health = max(0.0, current_health - final_dmg)
	emit_signal("health_changed", current_health, max_health)
	emit_signal("damaged", final_dmg, damage.source)

	if invulnerability_duration > 0.0:
		is_invulnerable = true
		_invuln_timer = invulnerability_duration
		emit_signal("invulnerability_started")

	if current_health <= 0.0:
		emit_signal("died")

	return true

func consume_mana(amount: float) -> bool:
	if current_mana >= amount:
		current_mana -= amount
		emit_signal("mana_changed", current_mana, max_mana)
		return true
	return false

func is_alive() -> bool:
	return current_health > 0.0


===================================================
FILE: src\combat\hitbox.gd
===================================================

class_name Hitbox
extends Area2D

signal hit_landed(target_hurtbox: Hurtbox)

@export var damage: float = 15.0
@export var knockback_force: float = 200.0
@export var owner_node: Node

var _already_hit_nodes: Array[Node] = []

func _ready() -> void:
	if owner_node == null:
		owner_node = get_parent()
	area_entered.connect(_on_area_entered)

func reset_hits() -> void:
	_already_hit_nodes.clear()

func _on_area_entered(area: Area2D) -> void:
	if area is Hurtbox:
		var hurtbox := area as Hurtbox
		if hurtbox.owner_node == owner_node:
			return # Không tự đánh trúng chính mình
		if hurtbox.owner_node in _already_hit_nodes:
			return # Đã trúng trong lượt đánh này rồi

		var knock_dir := Vector2.RIGHT
		if owner_node is Node2D and hurtbox.owner_node is Node2D:
			knock_dir = (hurtbox.owner_node.global_position - owner_node.global_position).normalized()
			if knock_dir == Vector2.ZERO:
				knock_dir = Vector2.RIGHT

		var dmg_info := DamageInfo.new(damage, owner_node, knock_dir, knockback_force)
		var success := hurtbox.apply_damage(dmg_info)
		if success:
			_already_hit_nodes.append(hurtbox.owner_node)
			emit_signal("hit_landed", hurtbox)

class_name Hurtbox
extends Area2D

signal hit_received(damage: DamageInfo)

@export var health_component: HealthComponent
@export var owner_node: Node

func _ready() -> void:
	if owner_node == null:
		owner_node = get_parent()

func apply_damage(damage: DamageInfo) -> bool:
	if health_component and health_component.is_alive():
		var taken = health_component.take_damage(damage)
		if taken:
			emit_signal("hit_received", damage)
		return taken
	return false

class_name MagicProjectile
extends Area2D

@export var speed: float = 550.0
@export var damage_amount: float = 30.0
@export var lifetime: float = 2.5
@export var knockback_force: float = 250.0

var direction: Vector2 = Vector2.RIGHT
var shooter: Node = null
var _timer: float = 0.0

func setup(p_direction: Vector2, p_shooter: Node, p_damage: float = 30.0) -> void:
	direction = p_direction.normalized()
	shooter = p_shooter
	damage_amount = p_damage
	rotation = direction.angle()

func _ready() -> void:
	area_entered.connect(_on_area_entered)
	body_entered.connect(_on_body_entered)

func _physics_process(delta: float) -> void:
	global_position += direction * speed * delta
	_timer += delta
	if _timer >= lifetime:
		queue_free()

func _on_area_entered(area: Area2D) -> void:
	if area is Hurtbox:
		var hurtbox := area as Hurtbox
		if hurtbox.owner_node == shooter:
			return
		var dmg_info := DamageInfo.new(damage_amount, shooter, direction, knockback_force)
		hurtbox.apply_damage(dmg_info)
		queue_free()

func _on_body_entered(body: Node) -> void:
	if body == shooter:
		return
	if body is TileMap or body is StaticBody2D:
		queue_free()

class_name InteractionComponent
extends Area2D

signal interaction_available(interactable: Node)
signal interaction_unavailable(interactable: Node)

@export var prompt_text: String = "Nhấn E để nói chuyện"
var is_player_nearby: bool = false
var _nearby_players: Array[Node] = []

func _ready() -> void:
	collision_layer = 0
	collision_mask = 2 # Chỉ nhận Player (Layer 2)
	body_entered.connect(_on_body_entered)
	body_exited.connect(_on_body_exited)

func _on_body_entered(body: Node) -> void:
	if body is Player:
		if not body in _nearby_players:
			_nearby_players.append(body)
		is_player_nearby = true
		emit_signal("interaction_available", get_parent())

func _on_body_exited(body: Node) -> void:
	if body in _nearby_players:
		_nearby_players.erase(body)
	if _nearby_players.size() == 0:
		is_player_nearby = false
		emit_signal("interaction_unavailable", get_parent())

FILE: src\interaction\interaction_coordinator.gd
===================================================

class_name InteractionCoordinator
extends Node

## Bộ điều phối input tập trung cho hệ thống tương tác và hội thoại (M2.1)
## Ngăn chặn race condition giữa NPC và GameUI do thứ tự node trong Scene Tree.

@export var game_ui: GameUI

func _ready() -> void:
	add_to_group("interaction_coordinator")
	_ensure_game_ui()

func _ensure_game_ui() -> void:
	if game_ui == null:
		var cur = get_tree().current_scene if get_tree() else null
		if cur:
			game_ui = cur.get_node_or_null("GameUI") as GameUI

func _unhandled_input(event: InputEvent) -> void:
	if not event.is_action_pressed("interact"):
		return

	_ensure_game_ui()

	# 1. Khi đang mở hội thoại: E chỉ chuyển sang câu tiếp theo hoặc đóng hội thoại
	if game_ui and game_ui.is_dialogue_open():
		game_ui.advance_dialogue()
		get_viewport().set_input_as_handled()
		return

	# 2. Khi hội thoại vừa đóng: grace cooldown ngăn chặn nhấn đúp re-open tức thì
	if game_ui and game_ui.is_dialogue_cooling_down():
		get_viewport().set_input_as_handled()
		return

	# 3. Khi chưa mở hội thoại: E dùng để kích hoạt tương tác với NPC gần nhất
	var target_npc := _find_nearby_interactable_npc()
	if target_npc:
		target_npc.trigger_interaction()
		get_viewport().set_input_as_handled()
		return

	# 4. Khi đứng gần Portal: kích hoạt dịch chuyển khu vực
	var target_portal := _find_nearby_portal()
	if target_portal:
		target_portal.trigger_interaction()
		get_viewport().set_input_as_handled()
		return

	# 5. Khi đứng gần vật thể tương tác khác (Tinh thạch cổ, bia đá,...):
	var target_interactable := _find_nearby_interactable()
	if target_interactable:
		target_interactable.trigger_interaction()
		get_viewport().set_input_as_handled()
		return

func _find_nearby_interactable_npc() -> NPC:
	var npcs = get_tree().get_nodes_in_group("npcs")
	for n in npcs:
		if n is NPC and n.is_interactable():
			return n
	return null

func _find_nearby_portal() -> ZonePortal:
	var portals = get_tree().get_nodes_in_group("zone_portals")
	for p in portals:
		if p is ZonePortal and p.is_interactable():
			return p
	return null

func _find_nearby_interactable() -> Node:
	var objs = get_tree().get_nodes_in_group("interactables")
	for obj in objs:
		if obj.has_method("is_interactable") and obj.is_interactable() and obj.has_method("trigger_interaction"):
			return obj
	return null
