class_name SaveManager
extends RefCounted

## Hệ thống Lưu & Tải Tiến trình (Save/Load System) cho NOCTELYRA (M3.2 FINAL)
## Hỗ trợ ghi tạm nguyên tử (Atomic write), backup và rollback khi lỗi,
## nạp trang bị trước để tính toán chính xác Max MP/Current MP,
## thẩm định (validation) chặt chẽ và cách ly dữ liệu kiểm thử an toàn 100%.

const DEFAULT_SAVE_PATH: String = "user://noctelyra_slot1.json"
const CURRENT_VERSION: String = "1.5.0"
const SUPPORTED_VERSIONS: Array[String] = ["1.0.0", "1.1.0", "1.2.0", "1.3.0", "1.4.0", "1.5.0"]

static var custom_save_path: String = ""
static var should_load_on_start: bool = false

# Các cờ mô phỏng lỗi dùng riêng cho kiểm thử an toàn ghi file (Fault Injection)
static var simulate_tmp_write_error: bool = false
static var simulate_backup_error: bool = false
static var simulate_replace_error: bool = false

static func get_save_path() -> String:
	if not custom_save_path.is_empty():
		return custom_save_path
	return DEFAULT_SAVE_PATH

static func get_tmp_path() -> String:
	return get_save_path() + ".tmp"

static func get_bak_path() -> String:
	return get_save_path() + ".bak"

static func has_save() -> bool:
	var path := get_save_path()
	if not FileAccess.file_exists(path):
		return false
	var file := FileAccess.open(path, FileAccess.READ)
	if file == null:
		return false
	var text := file.get_as_text()
	file.close()

	if text.strip_edges().is_empty():
		return false

	var test_json := JSON.new()
	var err := test_json.parse(text)
	if err != OK or not (test_json.data is Dictionary):
		return false

	var dict: Dictionary = test_json.data

	# Thẩm định phiên bản save_version
	if not dict.has("save_version") or not (dict["save_version"] is String):
		return false
	var ver: String = dict["save_version"]
	if not ver in SUPPORTED_VERSIONS:
		return false

	# Thẩm định cấu trúc dữ liệu cốt lõi bắt buộc phải tồn tại
	if not dict.has("player") or not (dict["player"] is Dictionary):
		return false
	if not dict.has("quest") or not (dict["quest"] is Dictionary):
		return false

	return true

static func delete_save() -> bool:
	var path := get_save_path()
	var success := true
	if FileAccess.file_exists(path):
		var err := DirAccess.remove_absolute(path)
		if err != OK: success = false
	var tmp_path := get_tmp_path()
	if FileAccess.file_exists(tmp_path):
		DirAccess.remove_absolute(tmp_path)
	var bak_path := get_bak_path()
	if FileAccess.file_exists(bak_path):
		DirAccess.remove_absolute(bak_path)
	return success

static func is_boss_combat_active(world: Node) -> bool:
	if world == null:
		return false
	if "boss_battle_started" in world and world.get("boss_battle_started") == true:
		return true
	var boss = world.get_node_or_null("BossLunareth")
	if boss and is_instance_valid(boss):
		if "current_state" in boss:
			var st: int = int(boss.get("current_state"))
			# 0 là DORMANT, 7 là DEFEATED
			if st != 0 and st != 7:
				return true
	return false

static func save_game(world: Node) -> bool:
	if world == null:
		return false

	# Kiểm tra bảo vệ: Không cho phép lưu khi đang chuyển khu vực (M6)
	if ZoneManager.is_transitioning:
		push_warning("SaveManager: Không thể lưu game trong khi đang chuyển khu vực!")
		return false

	# Kiểm tra bảo vệ: Không cho phép lưu khi đang giao tranh Boss
	if is_boss_combat_active(world):
		push_warning("SaveManager: Không thể lưu game trong khi trận đánh Boss đang diễn ra!")
		return false

	var player := world.get_node_or_null("Player") as Player
	var quest_mgr := world.get_node_or_null("QuestManager") as QuestManager
	var ui := world.get_node_or_null("GameUI") as GameUI

	if player == null or quest_mgr == null:
		return false

	var p_health := player.get_node_or_null("HealthComponent") as HealthComponent
	var p_inv := player.get_node_or_null("InventoryComponent") as InventoryComponent
	var p_eq := player.get_node_or_null("EquipmentComponent") as EquipmentComponent
	var p_spellbook := player.get_node_or_null("SpellbookComponent") as SpellbookComponent
	var p_journal := player.get_node_or_null("QuestJournalComponent") as QuestJournalComponent
	var p_stats := player.get_node_or_null("PlayerStatsComponent") as PlayerStatsComponent
	var p_prog := player.get_node_or_null("PlayerProgressionComponent") as PlayerProgressionComponent

	var save_path := get_save_path()
	var tmp_path := get_tmp_path()
	var bak_path := get_bak_path()

	var cur_zone: String = ZoneManager.get_current_zone_id(world)
	ZoneManager.save_zone_state(cur_zone, world)

	var save_data: Dictionary = {
		"save_version": CURRENT_VERSION,
		"timestamp": Time.get_unix_time_from_system(),
		"current_zone_id": cur_zone,
		"progression": {
			"race_id": p_stats.race_id if p_stats else "human",
			"level": p_prog.level if p_prog else 1,
			"current_exp": p_prog.current_exp if p_prog else 0,
			"claimed_exp_sources": p_prog.claimed_exp_sources.duplicate() if p_prog else []
		},
		"player": {
			"race_id": p_stats.race_id if p_stats else "human",
			"progression": {
				"level": p_prog.level if p_prog else 1,
				"current_exp": p_prog.current_exp if p_prog else 0,
				"claimed_exp_sources": p_prog.claimed_exp_sources.duplicate() if p_prog else []
			},
			"position_x": player.global_position.x,
			"position_y": player.global_position.y,
			"current_health": clamp(p_health.current_health, 0.0, p_health.max_health) if p_health else 100.0,
			"current_mana": clamp(p_health.current_mana, 0.0, p_health.max_mana) if p_health else 60.0
		},
		"gold": max(0, quest_mgr.gold),
		"inventory": p_inv.to_dict() if p_inv else [],
		"equipment": p_eq.to_dict() if p_eq else {},
		"spellbook": p_spellbook.to_dict() if p_spellbook else {
			"selected_spell": "moon_bolt",
			"known_spells": ["moon_bolt", "ember_bloom", "frost_shard"],
			"spell_levels": {"moon_bolt": 1, "ember_bloom": 1, "frost_shard": 1}
		},
		"quest_journal": p_journal.to_dict() if p_journal else {},
		"quest": {
			"state": int(quest_mgr.current_quest.current_state) if quest_mgr.current_quest else 0,
			"current_amount": int(quest_mgr.current_quest.current_amount) if quest_mgr.current_quest else 0
		},
		"zone_states": ZoneManager.zone_states.duplicate(true),
		"defeated_enemy_ids": world.get("defeated_enemy_ids") if "defeated_enemy_ids" in world else [],
		"collected_pickup_ids": world.get("collected_pickup_ids") if "collected_pickup_ids" in world else [],
		"dropped_pickups": world.get_dropped_pickups_data() if world.has_method("get_dropped_pickups_data") else [],
		"boss": {
			"activated": world.get("boss_battle_started") if "boss_battle_started" in world else false,
			"defeated": world.get("boss_defeated") if "boss_defeated" in world else false,
			"reward_claimed": world.get("boss_reward_claimed") if "boss_reward_claimed" in world else false
		}
	}

	var json_str := JSON.stringify(save_data, "\t")

	# 1. Ghi an toàn vào file tạm .tmp (Kiểm tra fault injection)
	if simulate_tmp_write_error:
		push_error("[Mô phỏng lỗi]: Thất bại khi ghi file tạm!")
		return false

	var tmp_file := FileAccess.open(tmp_path, FileAccess.WRITE)
	if tmp_file == null:
		push_error("Không thể mở file tạm để ghi: " + tmp_path)
		return false

	tmp_file.store_string(json_str)
	tmp_file.flush()
	tmp_file.close()

	# 2. Xác minh dữ liệu trong file tạm trước khi chạm vào file cũ
	var verify_file := FileAccess.open(tmp_path, FileAccess.READ)
	if verify_file == null:
		DirAccess.remove_absolute(tmp_path)
		return false
	var verify_text := verify_file.get_as_text()
	verify_file.close()

	var verify_json := JSON.new()
	if verify_json.parse(verify_text) != OK or not (verify_json.data is Dictionary):
		push_error("Xác minh file tạm thất bại! Giữ nguyên bản lưu cũ.")
		DirAccess.remove_absolute(tmp_path)
		return false

	# 3. Tạo bản sao lưu dự phòng .bak từ file hiện tại (nếu đã có file save cũ)
	var has_old := FileAccess.file_exists(save_path)
	if has_old:
		if FileAccess.file_exists(bak_path):
			DirAccess.remove_absolute(bak_path)

		if simulate_backup_error:
			push_error("[Mô phỏng lỗi]: Thất bại ở bước tạo bản sao lưu .bak!")
			DirAccess.remove_absolute(tmp_path)
			return false

		var copy_bak_err := DirAccess.copy_absolute(save_path, bak_path)
		if copy_bak_err != OK and not FileAccess.file_exists(bak_path):
			var bak_err := DirAccess.rename_absolute(save_path, bak_path)
			if bak_err != OK and not FileAccess.file_exists(bak_path):
				push_error("Không thể tạo bản sao lưu backup!")
				DirAccess.remove_absolute(tmp_path)
				return false

	# 4. Thay thế an toàn file chính bằng file tạm (.tmp -> save_path)
	var replace_err := DirAccess.rename_absolute(tmp_path, save_path)
	if replace_err != OK or simulate_replace_error:
		var copy_err := DirAccess.copy_absolute(tmp_path, save_path)
		if copy_err != OK or simulate_replace_error:
			push_error("Lỗi khi thay thế file save! Phục hồi bản lưu cũ từ backup.")
			if FileAccess.file_exists(bak_path):
				var restore_err := DirAccess.copy_absolute(bak_path, save_path)
				if restore_err != OK and not FileAccess.file_exists(save_path):
					DirAccess.rename_absolute(bak_path, save_path)
				if FileAccess.file_exists(save_path) and FileAccess.file_exists(bak_path):
					DirAccess.remove_absolute(bak_path)
			if FileAccess.file_exists(tmp_path):
				DirAccess.remove_absolute(tmp_path)
			return false
		DirAccess.remove_absolute(tmp_path)

	# 5. Hoàn tất thành công, dọn dẹp file backup
	if FileAccess.file_exists(bak_path):
		DirAccess.remove_absolute(bak_path)

	if ui:
		ui.show_notification("Đã lưu tiến trình game thành công!")

	return true

static func load_game(world: Node) -> bool:
	if world == null or not has_save():
		return false

	var player := world.get_node_or_null("Player") as Player
	var quest_mgr := world.get_node_or_null("QuestManager") as QuestManager
	var ui := world.get_node_or_null("GameUI") as GameUI

	if player == null or quest_mgr == null:
		push_error("load_game thất bại: World thiếu Player hoặc QuestManager.")
		return false

	var save_path := get_save_path()
	var file := FileAccess.open(save_path, FileAccess.READ)
	if file == null:
		return false
	var text := file.get_as_text()
	file.close()

	var test_json := JSON.new()
	var err := test_json.parse(text)
	if err != OK or not (test_json.data is Dictionary):
		push_warning("File save hỏng hoặc sai cú pháp JSON, bỏ qua để bảo vệ game.")
		return false

	var data: Dictionary = test_json.data
	var version: String = str(data.get("save_version", ""))
	if not version in SUPPORTED_VERSIONS:
		push_warning("Phiên bản save_version không được hỗ trợ: " + version)
		return false

	# Kiểm tra các khối dữ liệu cốt lõi
	if not data.has("player") or not (data["player"] is Dictionary):
		push_warning("Thiếu dữ liệu player hợp lệ trong bản lưu.")
		return false
	if not data.has("quest") or not (data["quest"] is Dictionary):
		push_warning("Thiếu dữ liệu quest hợp lệ trong bản lưu.")
		return false

	# Xác định khu vực hiện tại & Nạp zone_states (M6)
	var z_id: String = str(data.get("current_zone_id", "noctis_forest"))
	if not ZoneDefinition.is_valid_zone(z_id):
		z_id = "noctis_forest"
	ZoneManager.current_zone_id = z_id

	if data.has("zone_states") and data["zone_states"] is Dictionary:
		ZoneManager.zone_states = data["zone_states"].duplicate(true)
	else:
		# Tương thích ngược v1.0.0 - v1.2.0: map dữ liệu cũ vào noctis_forest
		var legacy_state: Dictionary = {
			"collected_pickup_ids": data.get("collected_pickup_ids", []),
			"dropped_pickups": data.get("dropped_pickups", []),
			"defeated_enemy_ids": data.get("defeated_enemy_ids", [])
		}
		if data.has("boss") and data["boss"] is Dictionary:
			legacy_state["boss"] = data["boss"]
		ZoneManager.zone_states["noctis_forest"] = legacy_state

	# BƯỚC 0 (Milestone M8): Khôi phục Chủng tộc & Cấp độ TRƯỚC TIÊN
	var p_stats := player.get_node_or_null("PlayerStatsComponent") as PlayerStatsComponent
	var p_prog := player.get_node_or_null("PlayerProgressionComponent") as PlayerProgressionComponent

	var prog_data: Dictionary = {}
	if data.has("progression") and data["progression"] is Dictionary:
		prog_data = data["progression"]
	elif data.has("player") and data["player"] is Dictionary and data["player"].has("progression") and data["player"]["progression"] is Dictionary:
		prog_data = data["player"]["progression"]

	var r_id: String = str(prog_data.get("race_id", ""))
	if r_id.is_empty() and data.has("player") and data["player"] is Dictionary:
		r_id = str(data["player"].get("race_id", "human"))
	if not RaceDatabase.has_race(r_id):
		r_id = "human"

	if p_stats:
		p_stats.set_race(r_id)

	if p_prog:
		p_prog.from_dict({
			"level": int(prog_data.get("level", 1)),
			"current_exp": int(prog_data.get("current_exp", 0)),
			"claimed_exp_sources": prog_data.get("claimed_exp_sources", [])
		})

	# BƯỚC 1 (QUAN TRỌNG): Khôi phục Trang bị & Túi đồ TRƯỚC để tính toán lại Max Stats (Max MP / Sát thương)
	var p_eq := player.get_node_or_null("EquipmentComponent") as EquipmentComponent
	if p_eq:
		if data.has("equipment") and (data["equipment"] is Dictionary):
			p_eq.load_from_dict(data["equipment"])
		else:
			p_eq.load_from_dict({})

	var p_inv := player.get_node_or_null("InventoryComponent") as InventoryComponent
	if p_inv:
		if data.has("inventory") and (data["inventory"] is Array):
			p_inv.load_from_dict(data["inventory"])
		else:
			p_inv._initialize_slots()

	# BƯỚC 1.2 (Milestone M8): Tính toán lại toàn bộ Max Stats thống nhất sau khi đã nạp Race + Level + Gear
	if p_stats:
		p_stats.recalculate_stats(false)

	# BƯỚC 1.5: Khôi phục Sách Phép (SpellbookComponent - M5)
	var p_spellbook := player.get_node_or_null("SpellbookComponent") as SpellbookComponent
	if p_spellbook:
		if data.has("spellbook") and (data["spellbook"] is Dictionary):
			p_spellbook.from_dict(data["spellbook"])
		else:
			# Tương thích ngược v1.0.0 và v1.1.0: mặc định mở khóa 3 phép cấp 1
			p_spellbook.from_dict({
				"selected_spell": "moon_bolt",
				"known_spells": ["moon_bolt", "ember_bloom", "frost_shard"],
				"spell_levels": {
					"moon_bolt": 1,
					"ember_bloom": 1,
					"frost_shard": 1
				}
			})

	# BƯỚC 2: Khôi phục Player (Tọa độ & HP/MP)
	# Nhờ Bước 1 đã chạy, p_health.max_mana đã được cập nhật chính xác (80 nếu đeo moon_amulet, 60 nếu không)
	var p_data: Dictionary = data["player"]
	var raw_x = p_data.get("position_x", player.global_position.x)
	var raw_y = p_data.get("position_y", player.global_position.y)

	var px: float = float(raw_x) if typeof(raw_x) in [TYPE_FLOAT, TYPE_INT] else player.global_position.x
	var py: float = float(raw_y) if typeof(raw_y) in [TYPE_FLOAT, TYPE_INT] else player.global_position.y
	if not is_finite(px): px = 180.0
	if not is_finite(py): py = 630.0

	# Giới hạn tọa độ trong thế giới an toàn (clamp mở rộng cho Đấu trường Boss M4)
	px = clamp(px, 50.0, 6200.0)
	py = clamp(py, 100.0, 700.0)
	player.global_position = Vector2(px, py)

	var p_health := player.get_node_or_null("HealthComponent") as HealthComponent
	if p_health:
		var raw_hp = p_data.get("current_health", p_health.max_health)
		var hp_val: float = float(raw_hp) if typeof(raw_hp) in [TYPE_FLOAT, TYPE_INT] else p_health.max_health
		p_health.current_health = clamp(hp_val, 0.0, p_health.max_health)

		var raw_mp = p_data.get("current_mana", p_health.max_mana)
		var mp_val: float = float(raw_mp) if typeof(raw_mp) in [TYPE_FLOAT, TYPE_INT] else p_health.max_mana
		p_health.current_mana = clamp(mp_val, 0.0, p_health.max_mana)

		p_health.emit_signal("health_changed", p_health.current_health, p_health.max_health)
		p_health.emit_signal("mana_changed", p_health.current_mana, p_health.max_mana)

	# BƯỚC 3: Khôi phục & Validate Vàng & Nhiệm vụ
	var raw_gold = data.get("gold", 0)
	var val_gold: int = int(raw_gold) if typeof(raw_gold) in [TYPE_INT, TYPE_FLOAT] else 0
	quest_mgr.gold = clamp(val_gold, 0, 999999)
	quest_mgr.emit_signal("gold_changed", quest_mgr.gold)

	var q_data: Dictionary = data["quest"]
	var raw_state = q_data.get("state", 0)
	var q_state: int = int(raw_state) if typeof(raw_state) in [TYPE_INT, TYPE_FLOAT] else 0
	q_state = clamp(q_state, 0, 3)

	var raw_amt = q_data.get("current_amount", 0)
	var q_amt: int = int(raw_amt) if typeof(raw_amt) in [TYPE_INT, TYPE_FLOAT] else 0

	if quest_mgr.current_quest:
		quest_mgr.current_quest.current_state = q_state as QuestData.State
		quest_mgr.current_quest.current_amount = clamp(q_amt, 0, quest_mgr.current_quest.target_amount)
		if q_state == QuestData.State.COMPLETED:
			quest_mgr.current_quest.current_amount = quest_mgr.current_quest.target_amount

	# BƯỚC 3b: Khôi phục Sổ Nhiệm Vụ (M7)
	var p_journal := player.get_node_or_null("QuestJournalComponent") as QuestJournalComponent
	if p_journal and data.has("quest_journal") and (data["quest_journal"] is Dictionary):
		p_journal.from_dict(data["quest_journal"])

	if ui:
		if quest_mgr.current_quest:
			ui.update_quest_info(quest_mgr.current_quest)
		ui.update_gold(quest_mgr.gold)
		if ui.has_method("update_quest_journal_ui"):
			ui.update_quest_journal_ui()

	# BƯỚC 4: Khôi phục trạng thái vật phẩm thế giới và quái vật cho khu vực hiện tại
	var cur_zone_state: Dictionary = ZoneManager.zone_states.get(z_id, {})
	var raw_collected = cur_zone_state.get("collected_pickup_ids", data.get("collected_pickup_ids", []))
	var collected_ids: Array = raw_collected if raw_collected is Array else []

	var raw_dropped = cur_zone_state.get("dropped_pickups", data.get("dropped_pickups", []))
	var dropped_pickups: Array = raw_dropped if raw_dropped is Array else []

	var raw_defeated = cur_zone_state.get("defeated_enemy_ids", data.get("defeated_enemy_ids", []))
	var defeated_ids: Array = raw_defeated if raw_defeated is Array else []

	if world.has_method("restore_world_state"):
		world.restore_world_state(collected_ids, dropped_pickups, defeated_ids)

	# BƯỚC 5: Khôi phục trạng thái Boss Lunareth (M4) nếu ở Rừng Noctis
	var boss_dict: Dictionary = {}
	if cur_zone_state.has("boss") and cur_zone_state["boss"] is Dictionary:
		boss_dict = cur_zone_state["boss"]
	elif data.has("boss") and data["boss"] is Dictionary:
		boss_dict = data["boss"]

	var b_activated: bool = bool(boss_dict.get("activated", false))
	var b_defeated: bool = bool(boss_dict.get("defeated", false))
	var b_reward: bool = bool(boss_dict.get("reward_claimed", false))

	if world.has_method("restore_boss_state"):
		world.restore_boss_state(b_activated, b_defeated, b_reward)

	# Đồng bộ danh sách quái đã chết vào QuestManager để chống đếm trùng
	for did in defeated_ids:
		var sid := str(did)
		if not sid in quest_mgr._killed_enemy_ids:
			quest_mgr._killed_enemy_ids.append(sid)

	if ui:
		if ui.has_method("set_current_zone"):
			ui.set_current_zone(z_id)
		ui.show_notification("Đã tải bản lưu thành công!")

	return true

static func get_save_zone_id() -> String:
	if not has_save():
		return "noctis_forest"
	var path := get_save_path()
	var file := FileAccess.open(path, FileAccess.READ)
	if file == null:
		return "noctis_forest"
	var text := file.get_as_text()
	file.close()
	var test_json := JSON.new()
	if test_json.parse(text) == OK and test_json.data is Dictionary:
		var d: Dictionary = test_json.data
		var z: String = str(d.get("current_zone_id", "noctis_forest"))
		if ZoneDefinition.is_valid_zone(z):
			return z
	return "noctis_forest"

static func get_save_zone_scene_path() -> String:
	return ZoneDefinition.get_zone_scene_path(get_save_zone_id())


===================================================
FILE: src\stats\player_stats_component.gd
===================================================

class_name PlayerStatsComponent
extends Node

## Hệ Thống Tính Chỉ Số Thống Nhất (Unified Stats System) cho NOCTELYRA (Milestone M8)
## Là NGUỒN DUY NHẤT tính toán chỉ số cuối cùng:
## Final Stats = Race Base Stats + Level Growth + Equipment Bonuses
## Đồng bộ và áp dụng an toàn sang HealthComponent, MeleeHitbox, SpellbookComponent, MovementData.

signal stats_updated()
signal race_changed(new_race_id: String)

@export var player: Player

# Thuộc tính chủng tộc hiện tại
var _race_id: String = "human"
var race_id: String:
	get:
		return _race_id
	set(val):
		if _race_id != val or base_health == 0.0:
			set_race(val)
		else:
			_race_id = val

# Chỉ số cơ bản của tộc (được nạp từ RaceDatabase)
var base_health: float = 100.0
var base_mana: float = 60.0
var base_attack: float = 25.0
var base_mana_regen: float = 5.0
var base_move_speed: float = 270.0
var magic_damage_multiplier: float = 1.0

# Chỉ số cuối cùng sau khi cộng cấp độ & trang bị
var final_max_health: float = 100.0
var final_max_mana: float = 60.0
var final_attack: float = 25.0
var final_mana_regen: float = 5.0
var final_move_speed: float = 270.0
var final_magic_multiplier: float = 1.0

# Hệ số tăng trưởng theo mỗi cấp độ (từ cấp 2 trở đi)
const GROWTH_PER_LEVEL_HP: float = 10.0
const GROWTH_PER_LEVEL_MP: float = 5.0
const GROWTH_PER_LEVEL_ATK: float = 2.0

func _ready() -> void:
	if player == null and get_parent() is Player:
		player = get_parent() as Player

	_bind_components()
	set_race(race_id)

func _bind_components() -> void:
	if player == null:
		return

	# Kết nối EquipmentComponent
	var eq := player.get_node_or_null("EquipmentComponent") as EquipmentComponent
	if eq and not eq.equipment_changed.is_connected(_on_equipment_changed):
		eq.equipment_changed.connect(_on_equipment_changed)

	# Kết nối PlayerProgressionComponent
	var prog := player.get_node_or_null("PlayerProgressionComponent") as PlayerProgressionComponent
	if prog and not prog.level_up.is_connected(_on_level_up):
		prog.level_up.connect(_on_level_up)

func set_race(new_race_id: String) -> void:
	var def := RaceDatabase.get_race(new_race_id)
	if def == null:
		def = RaceDatabase.get_race("human")

	_race_id = def.race_id
	base_health = def.base_health
	base_mana = def.base_mana
	base_attack = def.base_attack
	base_mana_regen = def.base_mana_regen
	base_move_speed = def.base_move_speed
	magic_damage_multiplier = def.magic_damage_multiplier

	# Cập nhật ngoại hình visual của Player nếu có
	if player and player.has_method("apply_race_visual"):
		player.apply_race_visual(race_id)

	recalculate_stats(false)
	emit_signal("race_changed", race_id)

func _on_equipment_changed(_slot_type: String, _item_id: String) -> void:
	recalculate_stats(false)

func _on_level_up(_new_level: int) -> void:
	# Khi lên cấp: tăng HP/MP hiện tại theo phần vừa được cộng thêm (không hồi đầy)
	recalculate_stats(true)

func recalculate_stats(is_level_up: bool = false) -> void:
	if player == null and get_parent() is Player:
		player = get_parent() as Player

	# 1. Lấy cấp độ hiện tại từ PlayerProgressionComponent
	var level: int = 1
	var prog := player.get_node_or_null("PlayerProgressionComponent") as PlayerProgressionComponent if player else null
	if prog:
		level = prog.level

	var lvl_diff: int = max(0, level - 1)
	var growth_hp: float = float(lvl_diff) * GROWTH_PER_LEVEL_HP
	var growth_mp: float = float(lvl_diff) * GROWTH_PER_LEVEL_MP
	var growth_atk: float = float(lvl_diff) * GROWTH_PER_LEVEL_ATK

	# 2. Lấy chỉ số thưởng từ trang bị hiện tại (EquipmentComponent)
	var eq_hp: float = 0.0
	var eq_mp: float = 0.0
	var eq_atk: float = 0.0

	var eq := player.get_node_or_null("EquipmentComponent") as EquipmentComponent if player else null
	if eq:
		var w_id := eq.get_equipped("weapon")
		if not w_id.is_empty() and ItemDatabase.has_item(w_id):
			var w_def := ItemDatabase.get_item(w_id)
			eq_atk += float(w_def.stat_modifiers.get("attack_bonus", 0.0))
			eq_hp += float(w_def.stat_modifiers.get("max_health_bonus", 0.0))
			eq_mp += float(w_def.stat_modifiers.get("max_mana_bonus", 0.0))

		var a_id := eq.get_equipped("accessory")
		if not a_id.is_empty() and ItemDatabase.has_item(a_id):
			var a_def := ItemDatabase.get_item(a_id)
			eq_atk += float(a_def.stat_modifiers.get("attack_bonus", 0.0))
			eq_hp += float(a_def.stat_modifiers.get("max_health_bonus", 0.0))
			eq_mp += float(a_def.stat_modifiers.get("max_mana_bonus", 0.0))

	# 3. Tính toán Final Stats thống nhất
	final_max_health = base_health + growth_hp + eq_hp
	final_max_mana = base_mana + growth_mp + eq_mp
	final_attack = base_attack + growth_atk + eq_atk
	final_mana_regen = base_mana_regen
	final_move_speed = base_move_speed
	final_magic_multiplier = magic_damage_multiplier

	# 4. Áp dụng đồng bộ vào các component tương ứng của Player
	if player:
		# HealthComponent
		var p_health := player.get_node_or_null("HealthComponent") as HealthComponent
		if p_health:
			var prev_max_hp := p_health.max_health
			var prev_max_mp := p_health.max_mana

			p_health.max_health = final_max_health
			p_health.max_mana = final_max_mana
			p_health.mana_regen_rate = final_mana_regen

			if is_level_up:
				# Khi lên cấp: cộng thêm lượng Max HP/MP tăng thêm vào Current HP/MP (không hồi đầy)
				var hp_gain: float = max(0.0, final_max_health - prev_max_hp)
				var mp_gain: float = max(0.0, final_max_mana - prev_max_mp)
				p_health.current_health = min(final_max_health, p_health.current_health + hp_gain)
				p_health.current_mana = min(final_max_mana, p_health.current_mana + mp_gain)
			else:
				# Khi thay đồ/tháo đồ: giữ HP/MP trong giới hạn mới
				p_health.current_health = min(final_max_health, p_health.current_health)
				p_health.current_mana = min(final_max_mana, p_health.current_mana)

			p_health.emit_signal("health_changed", p_health.current_health, p_health.max_health)
			p_health.emit_signal("mana_changed", p_health.current_mana, p_health.max_mana)

		# Combat Hitbox
		if player.melee_hitbox:
			player.melee_hitbox.damage = final_attack

		# SpellbookComponent
		var p_sb := player.get_node_or_null("SpellbookComponent") as SpellbookComponent
		if p_sb:
			p_sb.magic_damage_multiplier = final_magic_multiplier

		# MovementData
		if player.movement_data:
			if player.has_method("_ensure_isolated_movement_data"):
				player._ensure_isolated_movement_data()
			player.movement_data.move_speed = final_move_speed

	emit_signal("stats_updated")

func get_race_definition() -> RaceDefinition:
	return RaceDatabase.get_race(race_id)

func to_dict() -> Dictionary:
	return {
		"race_id": race_id
	}

func from_dict(data: Dictionary) -> void:
	if data == null or data.is_empty():
		return
	var r_id := str(data.get("race_id", "human"))
	set_race(r_id)

FILE: src\ui\character_creation_ui.gd
===================================================

class_name CharacterCreationUI
extends Control

## Giao diện Chọn Chủng Tộc & Khởi Tạo Nhân Vật (Character Creation UI) cho NOCTELYRA (M8)
## Luồng New Game: Main Menu -> Chọn tộc (Human / Elf / Demon Beast) -> Xác nhận -> Noctis Forest.
## Tuyệt đối không xóa save cũ nếu người chơi nhấn Quay Lại (Cancel).

signal race_selected(race_id: String)
signal creation_confirmed(race_id: String)
signal creation_cancelled()
signal cancelled()
signal character_confirmed(race_id: String)

const GAME_SCENE_PATH: String = "res://src/world/noctis_forest.tscn"
@export var auto_change_scene: bool = true

# UI Elements
@onready var race_title_label: Label = $Panel/Content/Header/RaceTitleLabel
@onready var race_motto_label: Label = $Panel/Content/Header/RaceMottoLabel
@onready var desc_label: Label = $Panel/Content/DescLabel
@onready var pros_label: Label = $Panel/Content/ProsLabel
@onready var cons_label: Label = $Panel/Content/ConsLabel

# Stats Display
@onready var hp_label: Label = $Panel/Content/StatsGrid/HPLabel
@onready var mp_label: Label = $Panel/Content/StatsGrid/MPLabel
@onready var atk_label: Label = $Panel/Content/StatsGrid/ATKLabel
@onready var magic_label: Label = $Panel/Content/StatsGrid/MagicLabel
@onready var regen_label: Label = $Panel/Content/StatsGrid/RegenLabel
@onready var speed_label: Label = $Panel/Content/StatsGrid/SpeedLabel

# Race Tab Buttons
@onready var human_btn: Button = $Panel/RaceTabs/HumanBtn
@onready var elf_btn: Button = $Panel/RaceTabs/ElfBtn
@onready var beast_btn: Button = $Panel/RaceTabs/BeastBtn

# Action Buttons
@onready var confirm_btn: Button = $Panel/Actions/ConfirmBtn
@onready var back_btn: Button = $Panel/Actions/BackBtn

var selected_race_id: String = "human"

func _ready() -> void:
	if human_btn:
		human_btn.pressed.connect(func(): select_race("human"))
	if elf_btn:
		elf_btn.pressed.connect(func(): select_race("elf"))
	if beast_btn:
		beast_btn.pressed.connect(func(): select_race("demon_beast"))

	if confirm_btn:
		confirm_btn.pressed.connect(_on_confirm_pressed)
	if back_btn:
		back_btn.pressed.connect(_on_back_pressed)

	select_race(selected_race_id)

func select_race(r_id: String) -> void:
	if not RaceDatabase.has_race(r_id):
		r_id = "human"
	selected_race_id = r_id

	var def := RaceDatabase.get_race(r_id)
	if def == null:
		return

	if race_title_label:
		race_title_label.text = def.display_name.to_upper()
		race_title_label.modulate = def.theme_color
	if race_motto_label:
		race_motto_label.text = def.title
	if desc_label:
		desc_label.text = def.description
	if pros_label:
		pros_label.text = "Ưu điểm: " + def.pros
	if cons_label:
		cons_label.text = "Nhược điểm: " + def.cons

	if hp_label:
		hp_label.text = "Sinh Lực: %d HP" % int(def.base_health)
	if mp_label:
		mp_label.text = "Ma Lực: %d MP" % int(def.base_mana)
	if atk_label:
		atk_label.text = "Tấn Công: %d ATK" % int(def.base_attack)
	if magic_label:
		magic_label.text = "Hệ Số Phép: x%.1f" % def.magic_damage_multiplier
	if regen_label:
		regen_label.text = "Hồi MP: %.1f MP/s" % def.base_mana_regen
	if speed_label:
		speed_label.text = "Tốc Độ: %d px/s" % int(def.base_move_speed)

	# Cập nhật style nút đang chọn
	_update_tab_highlights()
	emit_signal("race_selected", selected_race_id)

func _update_tab_highlights() -> void:
	if human_btn:
		human_btn.theme_type_variation = "FlatButton" if selected_race_id != "human" else ""
	if elf_btn:
		elf_btn.theme_type_variation = "FlatButton" if selected_race_id != "elf" else ""
	if beast_btn:
		beast_btn.theme_type_variation = "FlatButton" if selected_race_id != "demon_beast" else ""

func _select_race(r_id: String) -> void:
	select_race(r_id)

func _on_cancel_pressed() -> void:
	_on_back_pressed()

func _on_back_pressed() -> void:
	# Tuyệt đối không xóa bản lưu khi người chơi nhấn Quay Lại
	creation_cancelled.emit()
	cancelled.emit()
	visible = false

func _on_confirm_pressed() -> void:
	# Chỉ khi người chơi xác nhận bắt đầu New Game mới xóa bản lưu cũ và khởi tạo
	SaveManager.delete_save()
	SaveManager.should_load_on_start = false
	ZoneManager.reset_session()
	ZoneManager.initial_race_id = selected_race_id

	creation_confirmed.emit(selected_race_id)
	character_confirmed.emit(selected_race_id)

	if auto_change_scene and get_tree():
		get_tree().change_scene_to_file(GAME_SCENE_PATH)

[node name="HumanBtn" type="Button" parent="Panel/RaceTabs"]
layout_mode = 2
size_flags_horizontal = 3
theme_override_font_sizes/font_size = 16
text = "Nhân tộc"

[node name="ElfBtn" type="Button" parent="Panel/RaceTabs"]
layout_mode = 2

class_name CharacterSheetUI
extends Control

## Giao diện Bảng Thông Tin Nhân Vật (Character Sheet UI) cho NOCTELYRA (Milestone M8)
## Phím tắt C bật/tắt, hiển thị chủng tộc, cấp độ, thanh tiến trình EXP, chỉ số HP/MP,
## sát thương cận chiến, hệ số ma pháp, hồi MP, tốc độ di chuyển và trang bị hiện tại.

signal sheet_opened()
signal sheet_closed()

@onready var close_btn: Button = $Panel/Header/CloseBtn

# Header / Profile
@onready var race_label: Label = $Panel/Content/ProfileBox/RaceLabel
@onready var race_desc_label: Label = $Panel/Content/ProfileBox/RaceDescLabel
@onready var level_label: Label = $Panel/Content/ProgressionBox/LevelLabel
@onready var exp_bar: ProgressBar = $Panel/Content/ProgressionBox/ExpBar
@onready var exp_label: Label = $Panel/Content/ProgressionBox/ExpLabel

# Stats
@onready var hp_stat_label: Label = $Panel/Content/StatsGrid/HPLabel
@onready var mp_stat_label: Label = $Panel/Content/StatsGrid/MPLabel
@onready var atk_stat_label: Label = $Panel/Content/StatsGrid/ATKLabel
@onready var magic_stat_label: Label = $Panel/Content/StatsGrid/MagicLabel
@onready var regen_stat_label: Label = $Panel/Content/StatsGrid/RegenLabel
@onready var speed_stat_label: Label = $Panel/Content/StatsGrid/SpeedLabel

# Equipment
@onready var weapon_label: Label = $Panel/Content/EquipBox/WeaponLabel
@onready var accessory_label: Label = $Panel/Content/EquipBox/AccessoryLabel

var player: Player = null

func _ready() -> void:
	visible = false
	if close_btn:
		close_btn.pressed.connect(close)

func setup_player(p: Player) -> void:
	player = p
	if player:
		if player.player_stats_component:
			if not player.player_stats_component.stats_updated.is_connected(refresh):
				player.player_stats_component.stats_updated.connect(refresh)
		if player.progression_component:
			if not player.progression_component.progression_updated.is_connected(refresh):
				player.progression_component.progression_updated.connect(refresh)
	refresh()

func _ensure_player() -> void:
	if player == null and get_tree() and get_tree().current_scene:
		var p := get_tree().current_scene.get_node_or_null("Player") as Player
		if p:
			setup_player(p)

func open(p: Player = null) -> void:
	if p != null:
		setup_player(p)
	else:
		_ensure_player()
	visible = true
	refresh()
	emit_signal("sheet_opened")

func close() -> void:
	visible = false
	emit_signal("sheet_closed")

func toggle() -> void:
	if visible:
		close()
	else:
		open()

func is_open() -> bool:
	return visible

func refresh() -> void:
	_ensure_player()
	if player == null:
		return

	var stats := player.player_stats_component
	var prog := player.progression_component
	var health := player.health_component
	var eq := player.equipment_component

	# 1. Chủng tộc
	if stats:
		var r_def := stats.get_race_definition()
		if r_def:
			if race_label:
				race_label.text = "Chủng tộc: %s (%s)" % [r_def.display_name, r_def.title]
				race_label.modulate = r_def.theme_color
			if race_desc_label:
				race_desc_label.text = "%s\nƯu điểm: %s | Nhược điểm: %s" % [r_def.description, r_def.pros, r_def.cons]

	# 2. Cấp độ & EXP
	if prog:
		var cur_lvl := prog.level
		var cur_exp := prog.current_exp
		var req_exp := prog.get_current_target_exp()

		if level_label:
			level_label.text = "Cấp độ: Lv.%d%s" % [cur_lvl, " (Tối đa)" if prog.is_max_level() else ""]

		if exp_bar:
			if prog.is_max_level():
				exp_bar.max_value = 100
				exp_bar.value = 100
			else:
				exp_bar.max_value = max(1, req_exp)
				exp_bar.value = cur_exp

		if exp_label:
			if prog.is_max_level():
				exp_label.text = "EXP: ĐÃ ĐẠT CẤP ĐỘ TỐI ĐA (MAX)"
			else:
				exp_label.text = "EXP: %d / %d (%d%%)" % [cur_exp, req_exp, int(float(cur_exp) / float(max(1, req_exp)) * 100.0)]

	# 3. Chỉ số chiến đấu thống nhất
	if stats and health:
		if hp_stat_label:
			hp_stat_label.text = "Sinh Lực (HP): %d / %d" % [int(health.current_health), int(stats.final_max_health)]
		if mp_stat_label:
			mp_stat_label.text = "Ma Lực (MP): %d / %d" % [int(health.current_mana), int(stats.final_max_mana)]
		if atk_stat_label:
			atk_stat_label.text = "Tấn Công Cận Chiến: %d ATK" % [int(stats.final_attack)]
		if magic_stat_label:
			magic_stat_label.text = "Hệ Số Phép Thuật: x%.1f" % [stats.final_magic_multiplier]
		if regen_stat_label:
			regen_stat_label.text = "Hồi Phục Ma Lực: %.1f MP/s" % [stats.final_mana_regen]
		if speed_stat_label:
			speed_stat_label.text = "Tốc Độ Di Chuyển: %d px/s" % [int(stats.final_move_speed)]

	# 4. Trang bị
	if eq:
		var w_id := eq.get_equipped("weapon")
		var a_id := eq.get_equipped("accessory")

		if weapon_label:
			if not w_id.is_empty() and ItemDatabase.has_item(w_id):
				var w_def := ItemDatabase.get_item(w_id)
				weapon_label.text = "Vũ khí: %s (+%d ATK)" % [w_def.display_name, int(w_def.stat_modifiers.get("attack_bonus", 0))]
			else:
				weapon_label.text = "Vũ khí: [Trống]"

		if accessory_label:
			if not a_id.is_empty() and ItemDatabase.has_item(a_id):
				var a_def := ItemDatabase.get_item(a_id)
				accessory_label.text = "Phụ kiện: %s (+%d Max MP)" % [a_def.display_name, int(a_def.stat_modifiers.get("max_mana_bonus", 0))]
			else:
				accessory_label.text = "Phụ kiện: [Trống]"

class_name GameUI
extends CanvasLayer

## Giao diện người dùng chính (HUD, Dialogue, GameOver, PauseMenu, BossHUD, Victory) cho NOCTELYRA (M4)

signal retry_requested()
signal victory_dismissed()

@onready var hud: Control = $HUD
@onready var hp_bar: ProgressBar = $HUD/TopLeftBars/HPBar
@onready var mp_bar: ProgressBar = $HUD/TopLeftBars/MPBar
@onready var hp_label: Label = $HUD/TopLeftBars/HPBar/HPLabel
@onready var mp_label: Label = $HUD/TopLeftBars/MPBar/MPLabel
@onready var state_label: Label = $HUD/StateLabel
@onready var gold_label: Label = $HUD/GoldPanel/GoldLabel
@onready var quest_title_label: Label = $HUD/QuestPanel/VBox/QuestTitle
@onready var quest_progress_label: Label = $HUD/QuestPanel/VBox/QuestProgress
@onready var notification_label: Label = $HUD/NotificationLabel

# Boss UI (M4)
@onready var boss_hud: Control = $HUD/BossBarPanel
@onready var boss_name_label: Label = $HUD/BossBarPanel/BossNameLabel
@onready var boss_phase_label: Label = $HUD/BossBarPanel/BossPhaseLabel
@onready var boss_hp_bar: ProgressBar = $HUD/BossBarPanel/BossHPBar
@onready var boss_hp_label: Label = $HUD/BossBarPanel/BossHPBar/BossHPLabel
@onready var boss_warning_label: Label = $HUD/BossWarningBanner
@onready var boss_announcement_label: Label = $HUD/BossAnnouncementBanner
@onready var victory_panel: Control = $VictoryPanel
@onready var victory_title: Label = $VictoryPanel/Panel/Title
@onready var victory_subtitle: Label = $VictoryPanel/Panel/Subtitle
@onready var victory_continue_btn: Button = $VictoryPanel/Panel/ContinueBtn

@onready var dialogue_box: Control = $DialogueBox
@onready var dialogue_speaker: Label = $DialogueBox/Panel/SpeakerLabel
@onready var dialogue_text: Label = $DialogueBox/Panel/DialogueText
@onready var dialogue_hint: Label = $DialogueBox/Panel/NextHint

@onready var game_over_panel: Control = $GameOverPanel
@onready var retry_button: Button = $GameOverPanel/Panel/RetryBtn

@onready var pause_menu: Control = $PauseMenu
@onready var save_btn: Button = $PauseMenu/Panel/SaveBtn
@onready var inventory_ui: InventoryUI = $InventoryUI
@onready var quest_journal_ui: QuestJournalUI = $QuestJournalUI
@onready var character_sheet_ui: CharacterSheetUI = $CharacterSheetUI
@onready var spell_hotbar: SpellHotbar = $HUD/SpellHotbar

@onready var zone_label: Label = $HUD/ZoneLabel
@onready var zone_banner: Control = $ZoneBanner
@onready var zone_banner_title: Label = $ZoneBanner/Panel/TitleLabel
@onready var zone_banner_subtitle: Label = $ZoneBanner/Panel/SubtitleLabel

var _dialogue_lines: Array[String] = []
var _current_dialogue_idx: int = 0
var _current_quest_action: String = "none"
var _notif_timer: float = 0.0
var _dialogue_cooldown: float = 0.0
var _ui_close_grace_cooldown: float = 0.0
var _warning_timer: float = 0.0
var _announcement_timer: float = 0.0
var _zone_banner_timer: float = 0.0

func _ready() -> void:
	if character_sheet_ui == null:
		character_sheet_ui = get_node_or_null("CharacterSheetUI") as CharacterSheetUI
	if character_sheet_ui:
		character_sheet_ui.visible = false
	if dialogue_box:
		dialogue_box.visible = false
	if game_over_panel:
		game_over_panel.visible = false
	if notification_label:
		notification_label.visible = false
	if boss_hud:
		boss_hud.visible = false
	if boss_warning_label:
		boss_warning_label.visible = false
	if boss_announcement_label:
		boss_announcement_label.visible = false
	if victory_panel:
		victory_panel.visible = false
	if zone_banner:
		zone_banner.visible = false
	if retry_button:
		retry_button.pressed.connect(_on_retry_pressed)
	if save_btn:
		save_btn.pressed.connect(_on_save_pressed)
	if victory_continue_btn:
		victory_continue_btn.pressed.connect(_on_victory_continue_pressed)

func _process(delta: float) -> void:
	if _notif_timer > 0.0:
		_notif_timer -= delta
		if _notif_timer <= 0.0 and notification_label:
			notification_label.visible = false

	if _dialogue_cooldown > 0.0:
		_dialogue_cooldown -= delta

	if _ui_close_grace_cooldown > 0.0:
		_ui_close_grace_cooldown -= delta

	if _warning_timer > 0.0:
		_warning_timer -= delta
		if _warning_timer <= 0.0 and boss_warning_label:
			boss_warning_label.visible = false

	if _announcement_timer > 0.0:
		_announcement_timer -= delta
		if _announcement_timer <= 0.0 and boss_announcement_label:
			boss_announcement_label.visible = false

	if _zone_banner_timer > 0.0:
		_zone_banner_timer -= delta
		if _zone_banner_timer <= 0.0 and zone_banner:
			zone_banner.visible = false

func _unhandled_input(event: InputEvent) -> void:
	# Bật/tắt Sổ Nhiệm Vụ bằng phím Q
	if event.is_action_pressed("quest_journal"):
		if not is_dialogue_open():
			toggle_quest_journal()
			get_viewport().set_input_as_handled()
			return

	# Mở/đóng Túi đồ bằng phím I
	if event.is_action_pressed("inventory"):
		if not is_dialogue_open():
			toggle_inventory()
			get_viewport().set_input_as_handled()
			return

	# Bật/tắt Bảng Thông Tin Nhân Vật bằng phím C
	if event.is_action_pressed("character_sheet"):
		if not is_dialogue_open():
			toggle_character_sheet()
			get_viewport().set_input_as_handled()
			return

	# Phím ESC đóng CharacterSheet, Journal hoặc Inventory nếu đang mở
	if event.is_action_pressed("ui_cancel"):
		if is_character_sheet_open():
			close_character_sheet()
			get_viewport().set_input_as_handled()
			return
		if is_quest_journal_open():
			close_quest_journal()
			get_viewport().set_input_as_handled()
			return
		if is_inventory_open():
			close_inventory()
			get_viewport().set_input_as_handled()
			return

	# Đóng Journal hoặc Character Sheet nếu đang mở bằng phím J (attack)
	if is_quest_journal_open() and event.is_action_pressed("attack"):
		close_quest_journal()
		get_viewport().set_input_as_handled()
		return
	if is_character_sheet_open() and event.is_action_pressed("attack"):
		close_character_sheet()
		get_viewport().set_input_as_handled()
		return

	# Chặn các phím hành động khi đang mở hội thoại, trong thời gian hồi, hoặc đang mở túi đồ / sổ nhiệm vụ / bảng nhân vật
	if is_dialogue_open() or is_dialogue_cooling_down() or is_ui_cooling_down() or is_inventory_open() or is_quest_journal_open() or is_character_sheet_open():
		if event.is_action_pressed("attack") or event.is_action_pressed("jump") or event.is_action_pressed("cast_spell") or event.is_action_pressed("dash") or event.is_action_pressed("spell_1") or event.is_action_pressed("spell_2") or event.is_action_pressed("spell_3") or event.is_action_pressed("spell_upgrade"):
			get_viewport().set_input_as_handled()
			return

	# Dự phòng nếu không có InteractionCoordinator trong scene
	if is_dialogue_open() and event.is_action_pressed("interact"):
		if get_tree() and get_tree().get_nodes_in_group("interaction_coordinator").size() == 0:
			advance_dialogue()
			get_viewport().set_input_as_handled()

func show_dialogue(speaker: String, lines: Array[String], action: String = "none") -> void:
	_dialogue_lines = lines
	_current_dialogue_idx = 0
	_current_quest_action = action
	dialogue_speaker.text = speaker
	_display_current_line()
	dialogue_box.visible = true

func _display_current_line() -> void:
	if _current_dialogue_idx < _dialogue_lines.size():
		dialogue_text.text = _dialogue_lines[_current_dialogue_idx]
		if _current_dialogue_idx == _dialogue_lines.size() - 1:
			dialogue_hint.text = "[E để đóng]"
		else:
			dialogue_hint.text = "[E để tiếp tục]"

func advance_dialogue() -> void:
	_current_dialogue_idx += 1
	if _current_dialogue_idx < _dialogue_lines.size():
		_display_current_line()
	else:
		close_dialogue()

func close_dialogue() -> void:
	dialogue_box.visible = false
	_dialogue_cooldown = 0.25 # Grace cooldown 0.25s
	_ui_close_grace_cooldown = 0.25
	var scene: Node = _get_active_scene()
	var quest_mgr: QuestManager = scene.get_node_or_null("QuestManager") as QuestManager if scene else null
	var player: Player = scene.get_node_or_null("Player") as Player if scene else null
	if player == null and get_tree():
		var players = get_tree().get_nodes_in_group("player")
		if players.size() > 0:
			player = players[0] as Player
	var p_health: HealthComponent = player.get_node_or_null("HealthComponent") as HealthComponent if player else null

	if _current_quest_action.begins_with("start_quest:"):
		var q_id: String = _current_quest_action.substr(12)
		if player and player.quest_journal_component:
			player.quest_journal_component.start_quest(q_id)
			show_notification("Đã nhận nhiệm vụ mới!")
			update_quest_journal_ui()
	elif _current_quest_action.begins_with("complete_quest:"):
		var q_id: String = _current_quest_action.substr(15)
		if player and player.quest_journal_component:
			var res: Dictionary = player.quest_journal_component.turn_in_quest(q_id, player.inventory_component, quest_mgr)
			if bool(res.get("success", false)):
				show_notification("Đã hoàn thành nhiệm vụ và nhận thưởng!")
				update_quest_journal_ui()
				# Tự động lưu sau khi hoàn thành nhiệm vụ
				SaveManager.save_game(scene)
			else:
				var fail_reason: String = str(res.get("reason", "Túi đồ đã đầy! Hãy dọn chỗ trống trước khi trả nhiệm vụ."))
				show_notification(fail_reason)
	elif quest_mgr:
		if _current_quest_action == "start_quest":
			quest_mgr.start_quest()
		elif _current_quest_action == "complete_quest":
			quest_mgr.complete_quest(p_health)
			# M8: Thưởng EXP nhiệm vụ chính Elyra (chống nhận trùng)
			if player and player.progression_component:
				player.progression_component.add_exp(ExpDatabase.get_quest_exp("quest_tutorial"), "quest:quest_tutorial")
			# Tự động lưu sau khi hoàn thành nhiệm vụ và nhận thưởng
			SaveManager.save_game(scene)
	_current_quest_action = "none"

func _get_active_scene() -> Node:
	if get_tree() and get_tree().current_scene:
		return get_tree().current_scene
	return get_parent()

func is_dialogue_open() -> bool:
	return dialogue_box and dialogue_box.visible

func is_dialogue_cooling_down() -> bool:
	return _dialogue_cooldown > 0.0

func is_ui_cooling_down() -> bool:
	return _ui_close_grace_cooldown > 0.0

func toggle_inventory() -> void:
	if is_inventory_open():
		close_inventory()
	else:
		if is_quest_journal_open():
			close_quest_journal()
		if is_character_sheet_open():
			close_character_sheet()
		open_inventory()

func open_inventory() -> void:
	if is_quest_journal_open():
		close_quest_journal()
	if is_character_sheet_open():
		close_character_sheet()
	if inventory_ui:
		inventory_ui.open()

func close_inventory() -> void:
	if inventory_ui:
		inventory_ui.close()
	_ui_close_grace_cooldown = 0.15

func is_inventory_open() -> bool:
	return inventory_ui != null and inventory_ui.is_open()

func toggle_quest_journal() -> void:
	if is_quest_journal_open():
		close_quest_journal()
	else:
		if is_inventory_open():
			close_inventory()
		if is_character_sheet_open():
			close_character_sheet()
		open_quest_journal()

func open_quest_journal() -> void:
	if is_inventory_open():
		close_inventory()
	if is_character_sheet_open():
		close_character_sheet()
	if quest_journal_ui:
		quest_journal_ui.open()

func close_quest_journal() -> void:
	if quest_journal_ui:
		quest_journal_ui.close()
	_ui_close_grace_cooldown = 0.15

func is_quest_journal_open() -> bool:
	return quest_journal_ui != null and quest_journal_ui.is_open()

func update_quest_journal_ui() -> void:
	if quest_journal_ui and quest_journal_ui.is_open():
		quest_journal_ui.refresh()

func toggle_character_sheet() -> void:
	if is_character_sheet_open():
		close_character_sheet()
	else:
		if is_inventory_open():
			close_inventory()
		if is_quest_journal_open():
			close_quest_journal()
		open_character_sheet()

func open_character_sheet() -> void:
	if is_inventory_open():
		close_inventory()
	if is_quest_journal_open():
		close_quest_journal()
	if character_sheet_ui:
		var scene := _get_active_scene()
		var player: Player = scene.get_node_or_null("Player") as Player if scene else null
		if player == null and get_tree():
			var players = get_tree().get_nodes_in_group("player")
			if players.size() > 0:
				player = players[0] as Player
		character_sheet_ui.open(player)

func close_character_sheet() -> void:
	if character_sheet_ui:
		character_sheet_ui.close()
	_ui_close_grace_cooldown = 0.15

func is_character_sheet_open() -> bool:
	return character_sheet_ui != null and character_sheet_ui.is_open()

func update_character_sheet_ui() -> void:
	if character_sheet_ui and character_sheet_ui.is_open():
		character_sheet_ui.refresh()

func _on_save_pressed() -> void:
	var scene := _get_active_scene()
	# Chặn lưu game thủ công khi Boss đang giao chiến
	if scene and scene.has_method("is_boss_battle_active") and scene.is_boss_battle_active():
		show_notification("Không thể lưu game trong khi đang giao tranh với Boss!")
		return
	if SaveManager.is_boss_combat_active(scene):
		show_notification("Không thể lưu game trong khi đang giao tranh với Boss!")
		return
	var ok: bool = SaveManager.save_game(scene)
	if ok:
		show_notification("Đã lưu tiến trình thành công!")
	else:
		show_notification("Không thể lưu game lúc này!")

func update_player_hp(cur: float, max_v: float) -> void:
	if hp_bar:
		hp_bar.max_value = max_v
		hp_bar.value = cur
	if hp_label:
		hp_label.text = "HP: %d/%d" % [int(cur), int(max_v)]

func update_player_mp(cur: float, max_v: float) -> void:
	if mp_bar:
		mp_bar.max_value = max_v
		mp_bar.value = cur
	if mp_label:
		mp_label.text = "MP: %d/%d" % [int(cur), int(max_v)]

func update_player_state(st_name: String) -> void:
	if state_label:
		state_label.text = "State: %s" % st_name

func update_gold(amount: int) -> void:
	if gold_label:
		gold_label.text = "Vàng: %d G" % amount

func update_quest_info(quest: QuestData) -> void:
	if quest == null:
		return
	if quest_title_label:
		quest_title_label.text = "Nhiệm vụ: %s" % quest.title
	if quest_progress_label:
		match quest.current_state:
			QuestData.State.NOT_STARTED:
				quest_progress_label.text = "Tiến độ: Chưa nhận (Gặp Elyra)"
			QuestData.State.ACTIVE:
				quest_progress_label.text = "Tiêu diệt sinh vật: %d/%d" % [quest.current_amount, quest.target_amount]
			QuestData.State.READY_TO_TURN_IN:
				quest_progress_label.text = "Tiến độ: 3/3 (Về gặp Elyra nhận thưởng!)"
			QuestData.State.COMPLETED:
				quest_progress_label.text = "Trạng thái: Hoàn thành! (Cổng Đấu trường đã mở)"
	update_quest_journal_ui()

func show_notification(msg: String) -> void:
	if notification_label:
		notification_label.text = msg
		notification_label.visible = true
		_notif_timer = 3.5

func show_game_over() -> void:
	if game_over_panel:
		game_over_panel.visible = true

func hide_game_over() -> void:
	if game_over_panel:
		game_over_panel.visible = false

func _on_retry_pressed() -> void:
	hide_game_over()
	emit_signal("retry_requested")

# --- HỆ THỐNG GIAO DIỆN BOSS (M4) ---

func show_boss_hud() -> void:
	if boss_hud:
		boss_hud.visible = true

func hide_boss_hud() -> void:
	if boss_hud:
		boss_hud.visible = false
	if boss_warning_label:
		boss_warning_label.visible = false
	if boss_announcement_label:
		boss_announcement_label.visible = false

func update_boss_hp(cur: float, max_v: float) -> void:
	if boss_hp_bar:
		boss_hp_bar.max_value = max_v
		boss_hp_bar.value = cur
	if boss_hp_label:
		boss_hp_label.text = "HP: %d/%d" % [int(max(0, cur)), int(max_v)]

func update_boss_phase(phase: int) -> void:
	if boss_phase_label:
		if phase == 2:
			boss_phase_label.text = "[GIAI ĐOẠN 2 - HẮC NGUYỆT]"
			boss_phase_label.modulate = Color(1.0, 0.4, 0.9, 1.0)
			show_boss_announcement("LUNARETH GIẢI PHÓNG HẮC NGUYỆT MA LỰC!")
		else:
			boss_phase_label.text = "[GIAI ĐOẠN 1]"
			boss_phase_label.modulate = Color(0.4, 0.9, 1.0, 1.0)

func show_boss_warning(skill_name: String, duration: float) -> void:
	if boss_warning_label:
		boss_warning_label.text = "⚠️ CẢNH BÁO: %s!" % skill_name.to_upper()
		boss_warning_label.visible = true
		_warning_timer = max(0.6, duration)

func show_boss_announcement(msg: String, duration: float = 2.5) -> void:
	if boss_announcement_label:
		boss_announcement_label.text = msg
		boss_announcement_label.visible = true
		_announcement_timer = duration

func show_victory_banner(title: String, subtitle: String) -> void:
	hide_boss_hud()
	if victory_panel:
		if victory_title: victory_title.text = title
		if victory_subtitle: victory_subtitle.text = subtitle
		victory_panel.visible = true

func hide_victory_banner() -> void:
	if victory_panel:
		victory_panel.visible = false

func _on_victory_continue_pressed() -> void:
	hide_victory_banner()
	emit_signal("victory_dismissed")

# --- HỆ THỐNG GIAO DIỆN KHU VỰC & THẾ GIỚI (M6) ---

func set_current_zone(zone_id: String) -> void:
	if zone_label == null:
		zone_label = get_node_or_null("HUD/ZoneLabel") as Label
	if zone_label:
		zone_label.text = "📍 %s" % ZoneDefinition.get_zone_display_name(zone_id)

func show_zone_banner(zone_name: String, subtitle: String = "") -> void:
	if zone_banner == null:
		zone_banner = get_node_or_null("ZoneBanner") as Control
	if zone_banner_title == null:
		zone_banner_title = get_node_or_null("ZoneBanner/Panel/TitleLabel") as Label
	if zone_banner_subtitle == null:
		zone_banner_subtitle = get_node_or_null("ZoneBanner/Panel/SubtitleLabel") as Label

	if zone_banner:
		if zone_banner_title:
			zone_banner_title.text = zone_name
		if zone_banner_subtitle:
			if subtitle.is_empty():
				subtitle = ZoneDefinition.get_zone_sub_title(ZoneManager.current_zone_id)
			zone_banner_subtitle.text = subtitle
		zone_banner.visible = true
		_zone_banner_timer = 2.0

FILE: src\ui\main_menu.gd

class_name MainMenu
extends Control

## Màn hình chính (Main Menu) cho NOCTELYRA (M3 - M8)
## Cung cấp Continue (nếu có save), New Game -> Chọn Tộc (Character Creation), và Quit.

const GAME_SCENE_PATH: String = "res://src/world/noctis_forest.tscn"
const CharacterCreationUIScene = preload("res://src/ui/character_creation_ui.tscn")

@onready var continue_btn: Button = $Panel/VBox/ContinueBtn
@onready var new_game_btn: Button = $Panel/VBox/NewGameBtn
@onready var quit_btn: Button = $Panel/VBox/QuitBtn

@onready var confirm_dialog: Control = $ConfirmDialog
@onready var confirm_yes_btn: Button = $ConfirmDialog/Panel/HBox/YesBtn
@onready var confirm_no_btn: Button = $ConfirmDialog/Panel/HBox/NoBtn

var character_creation_ui: CharacterCreationUI = null

func _ready() -> void:
	if confirm_dialog:
		confirm_dialog.visible = false

	_ensure_character_creation_ui()
	_update_buttons()

	if continue_btn:
		continue_btn.pressed.connect(_on_continue_pressed)
	if new_game_btn:
		new_game_btn.pressed.connect(_on_new_game_pressed)
	if quit_btn:
		quit_btn.pressed.connect(_on_quit_pressed)

	if confirm_yes_btn:
		confirm_yes_btn.pressed.connect(_on_confirm_new_game)
	if confirm_no_btn:
		confirm_no_btn.pressed.connect(func(): confirm_dialog.visible = false)

func _ensure_character_creation_ui() -> void:
	if character_creation_ui == null:
		character_creation_ui = get_node_or_null("CharacterCreationUI") as CharacterCreationUI
	if character_creation_ui == null:
		character_creation_ui = CharacterCreationUIScene.instantiate() as CharacterCreationUI
		character_creation_ui.name = "CharacterCreationUI"
		add_child(character_creation_ui)
	character_creation_ui.visible = false
	if not character_creation_ui.creation_cancelled.is_connected(_on_creation_cancelled):
		character_creation_ui.creation_cancelled.connect(_on_creation_cancelled)

func _on_creation_cancelled() -> void:
	_update_buttons()

func _update_buttons() -> void:
	var has_valid_save := SaveManager.has_save()
	if continue_btn:
		continue_btn.disabled = not has_valid_save

func _on_continue_pressed() -> void:
	if SaveManager.has_save():
		SaveManager.should_load_on_start = true
		var target_scene := SaveManager.get_save_zone_scene_path()
		get_tree().change_scene_to_file(target_scene)

func _on_new_game_pressed() -> void:
	if SaveManager.has_save():
		if confirm_dialog:
			confirm_dialog.visible = true
	else:
		_open_character_creation()

func _on_confirm_new_game() -> void:
	if confirm_dialog:
		confirm_dialog.visible = false
	_open_character_creation()

func _open_character_creation() -> void:
	_ensure_character_creation_ui()
	if character_creation_ui:
		character_creation_ui.select_race("human")
		character_creation_ui.visible = true

func _on_quit_pressed() -> void:
	get_tree().quit()

FILE: src\ui\pause_controller.gd
===================================================

class_name PauseController
extends Node

@export var pause_menu: Control

func _ready() -> void:
	# PauseController luôn luôn xử lý input bất kể game đang pause hay không
	process_mode = Node.PROCESS_MODE_ALWAYS
	if pause_menu:
		pause_menu.visible = false

func _unhandled_input(event: InputEvent) -> void:
	if event.is_action_pressed("pause"):
		# Nếu túi đồ đang mở thì Esc đóng túi đồ trước thay vì mở PauseMenu
		var cur_scene = get_tree().current_scene if get_tree() else null
		if cur_scene:
			var ui := cur_scene.get_node_or_null("GameUI") as GameUI
			if ui and ui.is_inventory_open():
				ui.close_inventory()
				get_viewport().set_input_as_handled()
				return

		toggle_pause()
		get_viewport().set_input_as_handled()

func toggle_pause() -> void:
	var tree := get_tree()
	if tree == null:
		return
	if tree.paused:
		resume()
	else:
		pause()

func pause() -> void:
	var tree := get_tree()
	if tree == null:
		return
	tree.paused = true
	if pause_menu:
		pause_menu.visible = true

func resume() -> void:
	var tree := get_tree()
	if tree == null:
		return
	tree.paused = false
	if pause_menu:
		pause_menu.visible = false

FILE: src\ui\quest_journal_ui.gd

class_name QuestJournalUI
extends Control

## Giao diện Sổ Nhiệm Vụ (Quest Journal) cho NOCTELYRA (M7)
## Phím tắt Q bật/tắt, hiển thị Nhiệm vụ Chính và các Nhiệm vụ Phụ từng vùng.

signal journal_opened()
signal journal_closed()

@onready var close_btn: Button = $Panel/Header/CloseBtn
@onready var quest_list_container: VBoxContainer = $Panel/ScrollContainer/QuestList

# Card references
@onready var main_quest_card: PanelContainer = $Panel/ScrollContainer/QuestList/MainQuestCard
@onready var main_title_label: Label = $Panel/ScrollContainer/QuestList/MainQuestCard/VBox/HeaderHBox/TitleLabel
@onready var main_badge_label: Label = $Panel/ScrollContainer/QuestList/MainQuestCard/VBox/HeaderHBox/BadgeLabel
@onready var main_npc_label: Label = $Panel/ScrollContainer/QuestList/MainQuestCard/VBox/NpcLabel
@onready var main_desc_label: Label = $Panel/ScrollContainer/QuestList/MainQuestCard/VBox/DescLabel
@onready var main_progress_label: Label = $Panel/ScrollContainer/QuestList/MainQuestCard/VBox/ProgressLabel
@onready var main_reward_label: Label = $Panel/ScrollContainer/QuestList/MainQuestCard/VBox/RewardLabel

@onready var kaelis_quest_card: PanelContainer = $Panel/ScrollContainer/QuestList/KaelisQuestCard
@onready var kaelis_title_label: Label = $Panel/ScrollContainer/QuestList/KaelisQuestCard/VBox/HeaderHBox/TitleLabel
@onready var kaelis_badge_label: Label = $Panel/ScrollContainer/QuestList/KaelisQuestCard/VBox/HeaderHBox/BadgeLabel
@onready var kaelis_npc_label: Label = $Panel/ScrollContainer/QuestList/KaelisQuestCard/VBox/NpcLabel
@onready var kaelis_desc_label: Label = $Panel/ScrollContainer/QuestList/KaelisQuestCard/VBox/DescLabel
@onready var kaelis_progress_label: Label = $Panel/ScrollContainer/QuestList/KaelisQuestCard/VBox/ProgressLabel
@onready var kaelis_reward_label: Label = $Panel/ScrollContainer/QuestList/KaelisQuestCard/VBox/RewardLabel

@onready var rhaek_quest_card: PanelContainer = $Panel/ScrollContainer/QuestList/RhaekQuestCard
@onready var rhaek_title_label: Label = $Panel/ScrollContainer/QuestList/RhaekQuestCard/VBox/HeaderHBox/TitleLabel
@onready var rhaek_badge_label: Label = $Panel/ScrollContainer/QuestList/RhaekQuestCard/VBox/HeaderHBox/BadgeLabel
@onready var rhaek_npc_label: Label = $Panel/ScrollContainer/QuestList/RhaekQuestCard/VBox/NpcLabel
@onready var rhaek_desc_label: Label = $Panel/ScrollContainer/QuestList/RhaekQuestCard/VBox/DescLabel
@onready var rhaek_progress_label: Label = $Panel/ScrollContainer/QuestList/RhaekQuestCard/VBox/ProgressLabel
@onready var rhaek_reward_label: Label = $Panel/ScrollContainer/QuestList/RhaekQuestCard/VBox/RewardLabel

func _ready() -> void:
	visible = false
	if close_btn:
		close_btn.pressed.connect(close)

func open() -> void:
	visible = true
	refresh()
	emit_signal("journal_opened")

func close() -> void:
	visible = false
	emit_signal("journal_closed")

func toggle() -> void:
	if visible:
		close()
	else:
		open()

func is_open() -> bool:
	return visible

func refresh() -> void:
	var player: Player = _get_player()
	var quest_mgr: QuestManager = _get_quest_manager()
	
	if player and player.quest_journal_component and player.inventory_component:
		player.quest_journal_component.update_item_collection(player.inventory_component)

	_refresh_main_quest(quest_mgr)
	_refresh_side_quests(player)

func _refresh_main_quest(quest_mgr: QuestManager) -> void:
	if not main_quest_card:
		return
	if quest_mgr == null or quest_mgr.current_quest == null:
		main_title_label.text = "Nhiệm Vụ Chính: Thanh Tẩy Rừng Noctis"
		main_badge_label.text = "[Chưa nhận]"
		main_badge_label.modulate = Color(0.7, 0.7, 0.7)
		main_npc_label.text = "Người giao: Elyra | Khu vực: Rừng Hắc Nguyệt (Noctis Forest)"
		main_desc_label.text = "Tiêu diệt sinh vật ma hóa để bảo vệ Làng Ánh Nguyệt và mở Cổng Ma Thuật."
		main_progress_label.text = "Mục tiêu: Tiêu diệt 3 sinh vật ma hóa (0/3)"
		main_reward_label.text = "Phần thưởng: 50 Vàng, Hồi phục toàn bộ HP & MP"
		return

	var q = quest_mgr.current_quest
	main_title_label.text = "Nhiệm Vụ Chính: " + q.title
	main_npc_label.text = "Người giao: Elyra | Khu vực: Rừng Hắc Nguyệt (Noctis Forest)"
	main_desc_label.text = q.description
	main_reward_label.text = "Phần thưởng: %d Vàng, Hồi phục toàn bộ HP & MP" % q.reward_gold

	match q.current_state:
		QuestData.State.NOT_STARTED:
			main_badge_label.text = "[Chưa nhận]"
			main_badge_label.modulate = Color(0.7, 0.7, 0.7)
			main_progress_label.text = "Mục tiêu: Tiêu diệt 3 sinh vật ma hóa (Chưa nhận - gặp Elyra)"
		QuestData.State.ACTIVE:
			main_badge_label.text = "[Đang thực hiện]"
			main_badge_label.modulate = Color(1.0, 0.85, 0.3)
			main_progress_label.text = "Mục tiêu: Tiêu diệt %d/%d sinh vật ma hóa" % [q.current_amount, q.target_amount]
		QuestData.State.READY_TO_TURN_IN:
			main_badge_label.text = "[Sẵn sàng trả]"
			main_badge_label.modulate = Color(0.3, 0.9, 1.0)
			main_progress_label.text = "Mục tiêu: %d/%d sinh vật ma hóa (Gặp Elyra nhận thưởng!)" % [q.current_amount, q.target_amount]
		QuestData.State.COMPLETED:
			main_badge_label.text = "[Đã hoàn thành]"
			main_badge_label.modulate = Color(0.4, 1.0, 0.4)
			main_progress_label.text = "Mục tiêu: Đã hoàn thành (Cổng Ma Thuật Đấu Trường Đã Mở)"

func _refresh_side_quests(player: Player) -> void:
	var journal = player.quest_journal_component if player else null

	# Quest 1: Elyndra Cleansing (Kaelis)
	if kaelis_quest_card:
		var q1_state = journal.get_quest_state("quest_elyndra_cleansing") if journal else QuestDefinition.QuestState.NOT_STARTED
		var q1_prog = journal.get_quest_progress("quest_elyndra_cleansing") if journal else 0
		kaelis_title_label.text = "Nhiệm Vụ Phụ: Thanh Tẩy Thánh Lâm"
		kaelis_npc_label.text = "Người giao: Kaelis | Khu vực: Thánh Lâm Elyndra (Elyndra Glade)"
		kaelis_desc_label.text = "Tiêu diệt các cây Nấm Ma Hóa đang ô nhiễm dòng chảy Tinh Mạch của Thánh Lâm."
		kaelis_reward_label.text = "Phần thưởng: 80 Vàng, 2 Bình Máu (health_potion)"
		_apply_badge_and_progress(kaelis_badge_label, kaelis_progress_label, q1_state, q1_prog, 3, "Nấm Ma Hóa (Corrupted Mooncap)", "Kaelis")

	# Quest 2: Vharok Embers (Rhaek)
	if rhaek_quest_card:
		var q2_state = journal.get_quest_state("quest_vharok_embers") if journal else QuestDefinition.QuestState.NOT_STARTED
		var q2_prog = journal.get_quest_progress("quest_vharok_embers") if journal else 0
		rhaek_title_label.text = "Nhiệm Vụ Phụ: Lửa Rèn Tiền Đồn"
		rhaek_npc_label.text = "Người giao: Rhaek | Khu vực: Tiền Đồn Vharok (Vharok Outpost)"
		rhaek_desc_label.text = "Thu thập Lõi Tàn Hỏa từ vách đá hoặc Thằn Lằn Tro Tàn để duy trì lò rèn Quỷ tộc."
		rhaek_reward_label.text = "Phần thưởng: 100 Vàng, 3 Mảnh Chân Ma (mana_shard)"
		_apply_badge_and_progress(rhaek_badge_label, rhaek_progress_label, q2_state, q2_prog, 3, "Lõi Tàn Hỏa (Ember Core)", "Rhaek")

func _apply_badge_and_progress(badge_lbl: Label, prog_lbl: Label, state: int, cur: int, target: int, target_name: String, npc_name: String) -> void:
	match state:
		QuestDefinition.QuestState.NOT_STARTED:
			badge_lbl.text = "[Chưa nhận]"
			badge_lbl.modulate = Color(0.7, 0.7, 0.7)
			prog_lbl.text = "Mục tiêu: %s (Chưa nhận - gặp %s)" % [target_name, npc_name]
		QuestDefinition.QuestState.ACTIVE:
			badge_lbl.text = "[Đang thực hiện]"
			badge_lbl.modulate = Color(1.0, 0.85, 0.3)
			prog_lbl.text = "Mục tiêu: %d/%d %s" % [cur, target, target_name]
		QuestDefinition.QuestState.READY_TO_TURN_IN:
			badge_lbl.text = "[Sẵn sàng trả]"
			badge_lbl.modulate = Color(0.3, 0.9, 1.0)
			prog_lbl.text = "Mục tiêu: %d/%d %s (Gặp %s nhận thưởng!)" % [cur, target, target_name, npc_name]
		QuestDefinition.QuestState.COMPLETED:
			badge_lbl.text = "[Đã hoàn thành]"
			badge_lbl.modulate = Color(0.4, 1.0, 0.4)
			prog_lbl.text = "Mục tiêu: Đã hoàn thành và nhận thưởng"

func _get_player() -> Player:
	if get_tree() and get_tree().current_scene:
		var p = get_tree().current_scene.get_node_or_null("Player") as Player
		if p:
			return p
	var par = get_parent()
	if par:
		var p = par.get_node_or_null("Player") as Player
		if p:
			return p
	if get_tree():
		var players = get_tree().get_nodes_in_group("player")
		if players.size() > 0:
			return players[0] as Player
	return null

func _get_quest_manager() -> QuestManager:
	if get_tree() and get_tree().current_scene:
		var qm = get_tree().current_scene.get_node_or_null("QuestManager") as QuestManager
		if qm:
			return qm
	var par = get_parent()
	if par:
		var qm = par.get_node_or_null("QuestManager") as QuestManager
		if qm:
			return qm
	return null

FILE: src\ui\spell_hotbar.gd

class_name SpellHotbar
extends Control

## Thanh hiển thị & chọn phép thuật (Spell Hotbar UI) cho NOCTELYRA (Milestone M5)
## Hiển thị 3 ô phép [1], [2], [3], cấp độ, MP tiêu hao, viền highlight và thời gian hồi độc lập.
## Hỗ trợ chọn phép bằng phím hoặc nhấp chuột, kèm nút/phím tắt [U] để nâng cấp.

signal spell_selected_from_ui(spell_id: String)
signal spell_upgrade_requested()

@onready var slot1: Control = $Slots/Slot1
@onready var slot2: Control = $Slots/Slot2
@onready var slot3: Control = $Slots/Slot3
@onready var upgrade_btn: Button = $UpgradeBtn

var _spellbook: SpellbookComponent
var _slot_nodes: Array[Control] = []
var _spell_ids: Array[String] = ["moon_bolt", "ember_bloom", "frost_shard"]

func _ready() -> void:
	_slot_nodes = [slot1, slot2, slot3]
	_setup_slots()
	if upgrade_btn:
		upgrade_btn.pressed.connect(_on_upgrade_btn_pressed)

func _process(_delta: float) -> void:
	var sb := _get_spellbook()
	if sb == null:
		return

	_update_hotbar_display(sb)

func _get_spellbook() -> SpellbookComponent:
	if _spellbook and is_instance_valid(_spellbook):
		return _spellbook
	var scene := get_tree().current_scene
	if scene:
		var p = scene.get_node_or_null("Player")
		if p:
			_spellbook = p.get_node_or_null("SpellbookComponent") as SpellbookComponent
			if _spellbook:
				_connect_spellbook()
	return _spellbook

func set_spellbook(sb: SpellbookComponent) -> void:
	_spellbook = sb
	_connect_spellbook()

func _connect_spellbook() -> void:
	if _spellbook:
		if not _spellbook.spell_selected.is_connected(_on_spell_selected):
			_spellbook.spell_selected.connect(_on_spell_selected)
		if not _spellbook.spell_upgraded.is_connected(_on_spell_upgraded):
			_spellbook.spell_upgraded.connect(_on_spell_upgraded)

func _setup_slots() -> void:
	for i in range(_slot_nodes.size()):
		var slot_node := _slot_nodes[i]
		if slot_node:
			var idx := i
			slot_node.gui_input.connect(func(event: InputEvent):
				if event is InputEventMouseButton and event.pressed and event.button_index == MOUSE_BUTTON_LEFT:
					_on_slot_clicked(idx)
			)

func _on_slot_clicked(idx: int) -> void:
	var sb := _get_spellbook()
	if sb and idx >= 0 and idx < _spell_ids.size():
		var s_id := _spell_ids[idx]
		sb.select_spell(s_id)
		emit_signal("spell_selected_from_ui", s_id)

func _on_upgrade_btn_pressed() -> void:
	emit_signal("spell_upgrade_requested")
	var scene := get_tree().current_scene
	if scene:
		var p = scene.get_node_or_null("Player")
		if p and p.has_method("_upgrade_current_spell"):
			p._upgrade_current_spell()

func _on_spell_selected(_spell_id: String) -> void:
	var sb := _get_spellbook()
	if sb:
		_update_hotbar_display(sb)

func _on_spell_upgraded(_spell_id: String, _new_level: int) -> void:
	var sb := _get_spellbook()
	if sb:
		_update_hotbar_display(sb)

func _update_hotbar_display(sb: SpellbookComponent) -> void:
	for i in range(_spell_ids.size()):
		if i >= _slot_nodes.size():
			continue
		var s_id := _spell_ids[i]
		var slot_node := _slot_nodes[i]
		if slot_node == null:
			continue

		var spell := SpellDatabase.get_spell(s_id)
		if spell == null:
			continue

		var is_selected := (sb.selected_spell_id == s_id)
		var lvl := sb.get_spell_level(s_id)
		var cd := sb.get_cooldown(s_id)

		# 1. Viền sáng (Highlight)
		var highlight := slot_node.get_node_or_null("Highlight") as Control
		if highlight:
			highlight.visible = is_selected

		# 2. Cấp độ (Lv.1 - Lv.3)
		var level_label := slot_node.get_node_or_null("LevelLabel") as Label
		if level_label:
			level_label.text = "Lv.%d" % lvl

		# 3. Tiêu hao MP
		var mana_label := slot_node.get_node_or_null("ManaLabel") as Label
		if mana_label:
			mana_label.text = "%d MP" % int(spell.mana_cost)

		# 4. Cooldown Overlay & Số đếm
		var cd_overlay := slot_node.get_node_or_null("CooldownOverlay") as Control
		var cd_label := slot_node.get_node_or_null("CooldownOverlay/CooldownLabel") as Label
		if cd > 0.0:
			if cd_overlay:
				cd_overlay.visible = true
			if cd_label:
				cd_label.text = "%.1fs" % cd
		else:
			if cd_overlay:
				cd_overlay.visible = false

	# Cập nhật tooltip / text của nút nâng cấp
	if upgrade_btn:
		var cur_spell := sb.get_selected_spell()
		if cur_spell:
			var cur_lvl := sb.get_spell_level(cur_spell.spell_id)
			if cur_lvl >= 3:
				upgrade_btn.text = "[U] Max Lv.3"
				upgrade_btn.disabled = true
			else:
				var cost := cur_spell.get_upgrade_cost(cur_lvl)
				upgrade_btn.text = "[U] Nâng cấp (%d Shard)" % cost
				upgrade_btn.disabled = false

===================================================
FILE: src\world\ancient_crystal.gd

class_name AncientCrystal
extends Area2D

## Tinh Thạch Cổ (Ancient Crystal) trong Thánh Lâm Elyndra (Milestone M6)
## Cho phép người chơi tương tác bằng phím E để tìm hiểu cốt truyện và nguồn gốc ma lực.

@onready var prompt_label: Label = $PromptLabel
@onready var visual_crystal: Node2D = $VisualCrystal

var _player_in_range: bool = false
var _anim_time: float = 0.0

func _ready() -> void:
	add_to_group("interactables")
	collision_layer = 0
	collision_mask = 2 # Player (Layer 2)

	body_entered.connect(_on_body_entered)
	body_exited.connect(_on_body_exited)

	if prompt_label:
		prompt_label.text = "[E] Đọc văn tự Cổ Tinh Thạch"
		prompt_label.visible = false

func _process(delta: float) -> void:
	_anim_time += delta * 2.0
	if visual_crystal:
		var float_offset: float = sin(_anim_time * 2.0) * 4.0
		visual_crystal.position.y = -40.0 + float_offset

func is_interactable() -> bool:
	return _player_in_range

func trigger_interaction() -> void:
	var ui := _get_game_ui()
	if ui == null:
		return

	if ui.is_dialogue_open() or ui.is_dialogue_cooling_down():
		return

	var lore_lines: Array[String] = [
		"[Văn tự cổ phát sáng]: 'Từ thuở vạn vật phân chia, thế giới NOCTELYRA được bảo hộ bởi ba trụ cột ma mạch.'",
		"'Nhân tộc canh giữ Bìa Rừng và các cổ tự. Tinh Linh gìn giữ Thánh Lâm và sinh khí đất trời.'",
		"'Quỷ tộc bảo vệ Cổng Vực Sâu và sức mạnh địa nhiệt. Ba tộc hòa hợp thì ma triều êm ả, phân tranh thì bóng tối trỗi dậy.'",
		"'Kẻ mang dấu ấn Nguyệt Thực sẽ mở lối qua các vùng đất, kết nối lại những gì đã rạn nứt.'"
	]

	ui.show_dialogue("Cổ Tinh Thạch", lore_lines, "none")

func _on_body_entered(body: Node) -> void:
	if body is Player:
		_player_in_range = true
		if prompt_label:
			prompt_label.visible = true

func _on_body_exited(body: Node) -> void:
	if body is Player:
		_player_in_range = false
		if prompt_label:
			prompt_label.visible = false

func _get_game_ui() -> GameUI:
	var cur = get_tree().current_scene if get_tree() else null
	if cur:
		var ui = cur.get_node_or_null("GameUI") as GameUI
		if ui: return ui
	var p = get_parent()
	if p:
		var ui = p.get_node_or_null("GameUI") as GameUI
		if ui: return ui
	return null

===================================================
FILE: src\world\base_zone.gd
