class_name BaseZone
extends Node2D

## Lớp cơ sở (Base Zone) cho tất cả các vùng đất trong NOCTELYRA (Milestone M6, M6.1)
## Cung cấp kiến trúc mở rộng thế giới, vòng lặp tham chiếu chung,
## đồng bộ trạng thái người chơi, camera bám sát player,
## và khởi tạo UI ổn định (tách biệt setup_signals và setup_ui).

@export var zone_id: String = "noctis_forest"
@export var zone_display_name: String = "Rừng Noctis"

var player: Player
var game_ui: GameUI
var quest_manager: QuestManager
var pause_controller: PauseController
var interaction_coordinator: InteractionCoordinator

var defeated_enemy_ids: Array = []
var collected_pickup_ids: Array = []

func _enter_tree() -> void:
	init_base_references()

func _ready() -> void:
	process_mode = Node.PROCESS_MODE_PAUSABLE
	init_base_references()
	setup_signals()
	setup_camera()
	setup_ui()

	# 1. Tải Save nếu người chơi nhấn Continue từ Main Menu
	if SaveManager.should_load_on_start:
		SaveManager.should_load_on_start = false
		SaveManager.load_game(self)
	elif ZoneManager.session_player_data.is_empty():
		# Khởi tạo game mới với chủng tộc đã chọn (Milestone M8)
		if player and player.player_stats_component:
			var init_race := ZoneManager.initial_race_id if not ZoneManager.initial_race_id.is_empty() else "human"
			player.player_stats_component.set_race(init_race)
			var p_h := player.health_component
			if p_h:
				p_h.current_health = p_h.max_health
				p_h.current_mana = p_h.max_mana
				p_h.emit_signal("health_changed", p_h.current_health, p_h.max_health)
				p_h.emit_signal("mana_changed", p_h.current_mana, p_h.max_mana)

	# 2. Xử lý chuyển cảnh hoặc áp dụng trạng thái từ ZoneManager
	ZoneManager.on_zone_entered(self)

	# 3. Đồng bộ lại UI & Camera ngay sau khi nạp Save hoặc chuyển vùng
	setup_ui()
	snap_camera_to_player()

func _process(delta: float) -> void:
	ZoneManager.update_cooldown(delta)

func init_base_references() -> void:
	if player == null:
		player = get_node_or_null("Player") as Player
	if game_ui == null:
		game_ui = get_node_or_null("GameUI") as GameUI
	if quest_manager == null:
		quest_manager = get_node_or_null("QuestManager") as QuestManager
	if pause_controller == null:
		pause_controller = get_node_or_null("PauseController") as PauseController
	if interaction_coordinator == null:
		interaction_coordinator = get_node_or_null("InteractionCoordinator") as InteractionCoordinator

func setup_signals() -> void:
	# Kết nối Player
	if player:
		if not player.is_in_group("players"):
			player.add_to_group("players")
		if not player.state_changed.is_connected(_on_player_state_changed):
			player.state_changed.connect(_on_player_state_changed)

		var p_health: HealthComponent = player.get_node_or_null("HealthComponent")
		if p_health:
			if not p_health.health_changed.is_connected(_on_player_health_changed):
				p_health.health_changed.connect(_on_player_health_changed)
			if not p_health.mana_changed.is_connected(_on_player_mana_changed):
				p_health.mana_changed.connect(_on_player_mana_changed)

	# Kết nối GameUI
	if game_ui:
		if not game_ui.retry_requested.is_connected(_on_retry_requested):
			game_ui.retry_requested.connect(_on_retry_requested)

	# Kết nối QuestManager
	if quest_manager:
		if not quest_manager.quest_started.is_connected(_on_quest_updated):
			quest_manager.quest_started.connect(_on_quest_updated)
		if not quest_manager.quest_progress_updated.is_connected(_on_quest_updated):
			quest_manager.quest_progress_updated.connect(_on_quest_updated)
		if not quest_manager.quest_ready_to_turn_in.is_connected(_on_quest_updated):
			quest_manager.quest_ready_to_turn_in.connect(_on_quest_updated)
		if not quest_manager.quest_completed.is_connected(_on_quest_updated):
			quest_manager.quest_completed.connect(_on_quest_updated)
		if not quest_manager.gold_changed.is_connected(_on_gold_changed):
			quest_manager.gold_changed.connect(_on_gold_changed)
		if not quest_manager.notification_posted.is_connected(_on_notification_posted):
			quest_manager.notification_posted.connect(_on_notification_posted)

	# Kết nối tất cả NPC trong khu vực
	if get_tree():
		for n in get_tree().get_nodes_in_group("npcs"):
			if n is NPC and not n.dialogue_requested.is_connected(_on_npc_dialogue_requested):
				n.dialogue_requested.connect(_on_npc_dialogue_requested)

	# Kết nối tất cả Enemy trong khu vực
	if get_tree():
		for e in get_tree().get_nodes_in_group("enemies"):
			if e is Enemy:
				if not e.enemy_defeated.is_connected(_on_enemy_defeated):
					e.enemy_defeated.connect(_on_enemy_defeated)
				if player and player.quest_journal_component:
					if not e.enemy_defeated.is_connected(player.quest_journal_component.register_enemy_kill):
						e.enemy_defeated.connect(player.quest_journal_component.register_enemy_kill)

func setup_camera() -> void:
	if player == null:
		player = get_node_or_null("Player") as Player
	if player == null:
		return

	var cam: Camera2D = get_node_or_null("Camera2D") as Camera2D
	if cam:
		var remote: RemoteTransform2D = player.get_node_or_null("RemoteTransform2D") as RemoteTransform2D
		if remote == null:
			remote = RemoteTransform2D.new()
			remote.name = "RemoteTransform2D"
			remote.update_scale = false
			player.add_child(remote)
		remote.remote_path = cam.get_path()

func snap_camera_to_player() -> void:
	if player == null:
		player = get_node_or_null("Player") as Player
	if player == null:
		return

	var cam: Camera2D = get_node_or_null("Camera2D") as Camera2D
	if cam:
		cam.global_position = player.global_position
		cam.reset_smoothing()

func setup_ui() -> void:
	if game_ui == null:
		game_ui = get_node_or_null("GameUI") as GameUI
	if game_ui == null:
		return

	if player == null:
		player = get_node_or_null("Player") as Player

	if player:
		# 1. Gắn Player vào InventoryUI
		if game_ui.inventory_ui:
			game_ui.inventory_ui.setup_player(player)

		# 2. Gắn SpellbookComponent vào SpellHotbar
		if game_ui.spell_hotbar and player.spellbook_component:
			game_ui.spell_hotbar.set_spellbook(player.spellbook_component)

		# 3. Cập nhật thanh HP và MP
		var p_health: HealthComponent = player.get_node_or_null("HealthComponent")
		if p_health:
			game_ui.update_player_hp(p_health.current_health, p_health.max_health)
			game_ui.update_player_mp(p_health.current_mana, p_health.max_mana)

		# 4. Cập nhật trạng thái Player
		_on_player_state_changed("IDLE")

		# 5. Kết nối sự kiện thăng cấp
		if player.progression_component and not player.progression_component.level_up.is_connected(_on_player_level_up):
			player.progression_component.level_up.connect(_on_player_level_up)

	# 6. Cập nhật Vàng và Nhiệm vụ
	if quest_manager:
		game_ui.update_gold(quest_manager.gold)
		if quest_manager.current_quest:
			game_ui.update_quest_info(quest_manager.current_quest)

	# 7. Cập nhật thông tin vùng đất (HUD zone label)
	if game_ui.has_method("set_current_zone"):
		game_ui.set_current_zone(zone_id)

func _on_player_level_up(new_level: int) -> void:
	if game_ui:
		game_ui.show_notification("✨ Thăng cấp! Bạn đã đạt Cấp %d!" % new_level)
		if player:
			var p_health: HealthComponent = player.get_node_or_null("HealthComponent")
			if p_health:
				game_ui.update_player_hp(p_health.current_health, p_health.max_health)
				game_ui.update_player_mp(p_health.current_mana, p_health.max_mana)
			if game_ui.character_sheet_ui and game_ui.character_sheet_ui.is_open():
				game_ui.character_sheet_ui.refresh()

func _on_npc_dialogue_requested(npc: NPC, lines: Array[String], action: String) -> void:
	if game_ui:
		game_ui.show_dialogue(npc.npc_name, lines, action)

func register_collected_pickup(p_id: String) -> void:
	if not p_id.is_empty() and not p_id in collected_pickup_ids:
		collected_pickup_ids.append(p_id)

func _on_enemy_defeated(enemy_node: Node) -> void:
	if enemy_node is Enemy and not enemy_node.enemy_id.is_empty():
		if not enemy_node.enemy_id in defeated_enemy_ids:
			defeated_enemy_ids.append(enemy_node.enemy_id)

	# M8: Trao EXP từ quái vật (chống nhận trùng bằng enemy_id duy nhất)
	if player == null:
		player = get_node_or_null("Player") as Player
	if player and player.progression_component:
		var e_type: String = str(enemy_node.enemy_type_id) if "enemy_type_id" in enemy_node else "corrupted_wolf"
		var exp_val: int = ExpDatabase.get_enemy_exp(e_type)
		var e_id_str: String = str(enemy_node.enemy_id) if ("enemy_id" in enemy_node and not str(enemy_node.enemy_id).is_empty()) else str(enemy_node.get_instance_id())
		var source_id: String = "enemy:" + e_id_str
		player.progression_component.add_exp(exp_val, source_id)

func get_dropped_pickups_data() -> Array[Dictionary]:
	var result: Array[Dictionary] = []
	var dyn_pickups = get_tree().get_nodes_in_group("dynamic_pickups")
	for p in dyn_pickups:
		if p is ItemPickup and is_instance_valid(p) and not p.is_picked_up and not p.is_queued_for_deletion():
			# Chỉ lấy các pickup thuộc khu vực hiện tại
			if is_ancestor_of(p) or p.get_parent() == self:
				result.append({
					"pickup_id": p.pickup_id,
					"item_id": p.item_id,
					"amount": p.amount,
					"x": p.global_position.x,
					"y": p.global_position.y
				})
	return result

func restore_world_state(p_collected_ids: Array, p_dropped_pickups: Array, p_defeated_ids: Array) -> void:
	collected_pickup_ids.clear()
	for id in p_collected_ids:
		collected_pickup_ids.append(str(id))

	defeated_enemy_ids.clear()
	for id in p_defeated_ids:
		defeated_enemy_ids.append(str(id))

	# 1. Dọn các vật phẩm đặt sẵn đã từng nhặt
	var preplaced = get_tree().get_nodes_in_group("preplaced_pickups")
	for p in preplaced:
		if is_ancestor_of(p) and p is ItemPickup and not p.pickup_id.is_empty():
			if p.pickup_id in collected_pickup_ids:
				p.queue_free()

	# 2. Xóa các dynamic pickup cũ trước khi phục hồi danh sách lưu (tránh nhân bản)
	var existing_dyn = get_tree().get_nodes_in_group("dynamic_pickups")
	for p in existing_dyn:
		if is_ancestor_of(p) and is_instance_valid(p):
			p.queue_free()

	# 3. Phục hồi các vật phẩm quái rơi chưa nhặt
	var pickup_scene: PackedScene = preload("res://src/loot/item_pickup.tscn")
	for dp in p_dropped_pickups:
		if dp is Dictionary:
			var p := pickup_scene.instantiate() as ItemPickup
			p.pickup_id = str(dp.get("pickup_id", ""))
			p.item_id = str(dp.get("item_id", "mana_shard"))
			p.amount = max(1, int(dp.get("amount", 1)))
			p.is_dynamic = true
			var px: float = float(dp.get("x", 0.0))
			var py: float = float(dp.get("y", 0.0))
			p.global_position = Vector2(px, py)
			add_child(p)

	# 4. Xóa các quái đã chết
	var enemies = get_tree().get_nodes_in_group("enemies")
	for e in enemies:
		if is_ancestor_of(e) and e is Enemy and not e.enemy_id.is_empty():
			if e.enemy_id in defeated_enemy_ids:
				e.queue_free()

func clear_player_projectiles() -> void:
	if get_tree():
		var projectiles := get_tree().get_nodes_in_group("player_projectiles")
		for p in projectiles:
			if is_instance_valid(p) and not p.is_queued_for_deletion():
				p.queue_free()

func _on_retry_requested() -> void:
	clear_player_projectiles()
	if player:
		player.respawn()
	setup_ui()
	snap_camera_to_player()

func _on_player_state_changed(new_state_name: String) -> void:
	if game_ui:
		game_ui.update_player_state(new_state_name)

func _on_player_health_changed(current: float, maximum: float) -> void:
	if game_ui:
		game_ui.update_player_hp(current, maximum)

func _on_player_mana_changed(current: float, maximum: float) -> void:
	if game_ui:
		game_ui.update_player_mp(current, maximum)

func _on_quest_updated(_q: QuestData) -> void:
	if game_ui and quest_manager and quest_manager.current_quest:
		game_ui.update_quest_info(quest_manager.current_quest)
		game_ui.update_gold(quest_manager.gold)

func _on_gold_changed(new_gold: int) -> void:
	if game_ui:
		game_ui.update_gold(new_gold)

func _on_notification_posted(msg: String) -> void:
	if game_ui:
		game_ui.show_notification(msg)

func _on_resume_button_pressed() -> void:
	if pause_controller:
		pause_controller.resume()
	else:
		get_tree().paused = false
		if game_ui and game_ui.pause_menu:
			game_ui.pause_menu.visible = false

func _on_quit_button_pressed() -> void:
	get_tree().paused = false
	get_tree().change_scene_to_file("res://src/ui/main_menu.tscn")

class_name MagicGate
extends StaticBody2D

## Cổng Ma Pháp chặn lối vào Đấu trường Boss Lunareth (M4)
## Chỉ mở khóa sau khi hoàn thành và trả nhiệm vụ tiêu diệt quái vật cho Elyra.
## Ngăn cản tuyệt đối việc vượt qua bằng Dash hay Double Jump khi đang khóa.

signal gate_opened()

@export var is_open: bool = false

@onready var collision_shape: CollisionShape2D = $CollisionShape2D
@onready var visual_barrier: ColorRect = $VisualBarrier
@onready var runes: Label = $VisualBarrier/RuneLabel
@onready var interaction_area: Area2D = $InteractionArea

func _ready() -> void:
	collision_layer = 1
	collision_mask = 0
	if is_open:
		open_gate(false)
	else:
		close_gate()
	if interaction_area:
		interaction_area.body_entered.connect(_on_body_entered)

func open_gate(notify: bool = true) -> void:
	is_open = true
	if collision_shape:
		collision_shape.disabled = true
	if visual_barrier:
		visual_barrier.color = Color(0.2, 0.8, 1.0, 0.2)
	if runes:
		runes.text = "[PHONG ẤN ĐÃ GIẢI TRỪ]"
		runes.modulate = Color(0.4, 1.0, 0.8, 0.8)
	emit_signal("gate_opened")

func close_gate() -> void:
	is_open = false
	if collision_shape:
		collision_shape.disabled = false
	if visual_barrier:
		visual_barrier.color = Color(0.65, 0.15, 0.85, 0.85)
	if runes:
		runes.text = "[PHONG ẤN CỔ TỰ]\nCần hoàn thành nhiệm vụ Elyra!"
		runes.modulate = Color(1.0, 0.6, 0.9, 1.0)

func update_quest_status(quest: QuestData) -> void:
	if quest and quest.current_state == QuestData.State.COMPLETED:
		if not is_open:
			open_gate(true)
	else:
		if is_open:
			close_gate()

func _on_body_entered(body: Node) -> void:
	if body is Player and not is_open:
		var cur_scene = get_tree().current_scene if get_tree() else null
		if cur_scene and "game_ui" in cur_scene and cur_scene.game_ui:
			cur_scene.game_ui.show_notification("Cổng Ma Pháp bị phong ấn! Hãy hoàn thành nhiệm vụ của Elyra để giải trừ.")

FILE: src\world\noctis_forest.gd

class_name NoctisForest
extends BaseZone

## Thế giới Bìa Rừng Noctis & Đấu trường Boss Lunareth (M4, M6, M6.1)
## Quản lý vòng lặp nhiệm vụ, quái vật, cổng ma pháp, đấu trường và Boss Lunareth.

var npc_elyra: NPC

# Boss & Đấu trường (M4)
const BossLunarethScript = preload("res://src/boss/boss_lunareth.gd")
const MagicGateScript = preload("res://src/world/magic_gate.gd")

var boss_lunareth: CharacterBody2D
var magic_gate: StaticBody2D
var arena_barrier: StaticBody2D
var arena_trigger: Area2D
var arena_checkpoint: Vector2 = Vector2(4120.0, 630.0)

var boss_battle_started: bool = false
var boss_defeated: bool = false
var boss_reward_claimed: bool = false

func _init() -> void:
	zone_id = "noctis_forest"
	zone_display_name = "Rừng Noctis"

func _enter_tree() -> void:
	init_references()

func _ready() -> void:
	init_references()
	super._ready()

func init_references() -> void:
	init_base_references()
	if npc_elyra == null:
		npc_elyra = get_node_or_null("NPC_Elyra") as NPC
	if magic_gate == null:
		magic_gate = get_node_or_null("MagicGate") as StaticBody2D
	if boss_lunareth == null:
		boss_lunareth = get_node_or_null("BossLunareth") as CharacterBody2D
	if arena_barrier == null:
		arena_barrier = get_node_or_null("Geometry/ArenaBarrier") as StaticBody2D
	if arena_trigger == null:
		arena_trigger = get_node_or_null("ArenaTrigger") as Area2D

	# Khởi tạo trạng thái rào chắn đấu trường
	if arena_barrier:
		arena_barrier.visible = false
		var b_col := arena_barrier.get_node_or_null("CollisionShape2D") as CollisionShape2D
		if b_col:
			b_col.disabled = true

func setup_signals() -> void:
	super.setup_signals()

	# Kết nối NPC Elyra
	if npc_elyra and not npc_elyra.dialogue_requested.is_connected(_on_npc_dialogue_requested):
		npc_elyra.dialogue_requested.connect(_on_npc_dialogue_requested)

	# Kết nối các quái vật trên map
	var enemies = get_tree().get_nodes_in_group("enemies") if get_tree() else []
	for e in enemies:
		if e is Enemy:
			if quest_manager and not e.enemy_defeated.is_connected(quest_manager.register_enemy_kill):
				e.enemy_defeated.connect(quest_manager.register_enemy_kill)
			if not e.enemy_defeated.is_connected(_on_enemy_defeated):
				e.enemy_defeated.connect(_on_enemy_defeated)

	# Kết nối Boss Lunareth (M4)
	if boss_lunareth and not boss_lunareth.boss_health_changed.is_connected(_on_boss_health_changed):
		boss_lunareth.boss_health_changed.connect(_on_boss_health_changed)
		boss_lunareth.boss_phase_changed.connect(_on_boss_phase_changed)
		boss_lunareth.boss_telegraph_started.connect(_on_boss_telegraph_started)
		boss_lunareth.boss_defeated.connect(_on_boss_defeated)

	# Kết nối Arena Trigger
	if arena_trigger and not arena_trigger.body_entered.is_connected(_on_arena_trigger_entered):
		arena_trigger.body_entered.connect(_on_arena_trigger_entered)

func setup_ui() -> void:
	super.setup_ui()
	# Đồng bộ trạng thái mở cổng theo nhiệm vụ
	if magic_gate and quest_manager and quest_manager.current_quest:
		magic_gate.update_quest_status(quest_manager.current_quest)

func is_boss_battle_active() -> bool:
	return boss_battle_started and not boss_defeated and boss_lunareth != null and boss_lunareth.current_state != BossLunarethScript.State.DEFEATED

func _on_arena_trigger_entered(body: Node) -> void:
	if body is Player and not boss_defeated and not boss_battle_started:
		start_boss_battle()

func start_boss_battle() -> void:
	boss_battle_started = true

	# Kích hoạt rào chắn đấu trường (Arena Boundary) khóa chặt lối vào
	if arena_barrier:
		arena_barrier.visible = true
		var b_col := arena_barrier.get_node_or_null("CollisionShape2D") as CollisionShape2D
		if b_col:
			b_col.disabled = false

	# Cập nhật checkpoint hồi sinh ngoài cổng đấu trường
	if player:
		player.spawn_position = arena_checkpoint

	# Đánh thức Boss
	if boss_lunareth:
		boss_lunareth.activate_boss()

	# Hiển thị HUD Boss
	if game_ui and boss_lunareth:
		game_ui.show_boss_hud()
		if boss_lunareth.health_component:
			game_ui.update_boss_hp(boss_lunareth.health_component.current_health, boss_lunareth.health_component.max_health)
		game_ui.update_boss_phase(1)
		game_ui.show_notification("Linh Lộc Lunareth xuất hiện!")

func _on_boss_health_changed(cur: float, max_v: float) -> void:
	if game_ui:
		game_ui.update_boss_hp(cur, max_v)

func _on_boss_phase_changed(new_phase: int) -> void:
	if game_ui:
		game_ui.update_boss_phase(new_phase)

func _on_boss_telegraph_started(skill_name: String, duration: float) -> void:
	if game_ui:
		game_ui.show_boss_warning(skill_name, duration)

func _on_boss_defeated() -> void:
	boss_battle_started = false
	boss_defeated = true
	clear_boss_projectiles()

	# Mở khóa rào chắn đấu trường
	if arena_barrier:
		arena_barrier.visible = false
		var b_col := arena_barrier.get_node_or_null("CollisionShape2D") as CollisionShape2D
		if b_col:
			b_col.disabled = true

	grant_boss_reward()

func grant_boss_reward() -> void:
	if boss_reward_claimed:
		return
	boss_reward_claimed = true

	# 1. Thưởng 100 Vàng
	if quest_manager:
		quest_manager.gold += 100
		quest_manager.emit_signal("gold_changed", quest_manager.gold)

	# 1b. Thưởng 150 EXP từ Boss Lunareth (M8)
	if player and player.progression_component:
		player.progression_component.add_exp(ExpDatabase.get_boss_exp(), "boss:lunareth")

	# 2. Thưởng vật phẩm Mảnh Nguyệt Thực (eclipse_fragment)
	if player and player.inventory_component:
		var inv: InventoryComponent = player.inventory_component
		if inv.can_add_item("eclipse_fragment", 1):
			inv.add_item("eclipse_fragment", 1)
			if game_ui:
				game_ui.show_notification("Đã nhận Mảnh Nguyệt Thực & 100 Vàng!")
		else:
			# Túi đầy: rơi đồ dưới dạng ItemPickup trên mặt đất tại vị trí Boss
			_spawn_boss_reward_pickup()
			if game_ui:
				game_ui.show_notification("Túi đồ đầy! Mảnh Nguyệt Thực đã rơi trên mặt đất.")

	# 3. Hiển thị thông báo chiến thắng & thanh tẩy thành công
	if game_ui:
		game_ui.show_victory_banner(
			"THANH TẨY THÀNH CÔNG!",
			"Linh Lộc Lunareth đã hồi phục tâm trí!\nRừng Noctis đã được thanh tẩy.\nNhận thưởng: 100 Vàng & Mảnh Nguyệt Thực."
		)

	# 4. Tự động lưu tiến trình game sau chiến thắng
	SaveManager.save_game(self)

func _spawn_boss_reward_pickup() -> void:
	var pickup_scene: PackedScene = preload("res://src/loot/item_pickup.tscn")
	var p := pickup_scene.instantiate() as ItemPickup
	p.pickup_id = "drop_boss_eclipse_fragment"
	p.item_id = "eclipse_fragment"
	p.amount = 1
	p.is_dynamic = true
	var drop_x: float = boss_lunareth.global_position.x if boss_lunareth else 5300.0
	p.global_position = Vector2(drop_x, 630.0)
	add_child(p)

func restore_boss_state(p_activated: bool, p_defeated: bool, p_reward: bool) -> void:
	boss_defeated = p_defeated
	boss_reward_claimed = p_reward
	clear_boss_projectiles()

	if boss_defeated:
		boss_battle_started = false
		if arena_barrier:
			arena_barrier.visible = false
			var b_col := arena_barrier.get_node_or_null("CollisionShape2D") as CollisionShape2D
			if b_col:
				b_col.disabled = true
		if magic_gate:
			magic_gate.open_gate(false)
		if boss_lunareth:
			boss_lunareth.purify_boss()
		if game_ui:
			game_ui.hide_boss_hud()
	else:
		boss_battle_started = false
		if arena_barrier:
			arena_barrier.visible = false
			var b_col := arena_barrier.get_node_or_null("CollisionShape2D") as CollisionShape2D
			if b_col:
				b_col.disabled = true
		if boss_lunareth:
			boss_lunareth.reset_boss()
		if magic_gate and quest_manager and quest_manager.current_quest:
			magic_gate.update_quest_status(quest_manager.current_quest)

func clear_boss_projectiles() -> void:
	if get_tree():
		var projectiles := get_tree().get_nodes_in_group("boss_projectiles")
		for p in projectiles:
			if is_instance_valid(p) and not p.is_queued_for_deletion():
				p.queue_free()

func clear_all_projectiles() -> void:
	clear_boss_projectiles()
	clear_player_projectiles()

func _on_quest_updated(quest: QuestData) -> void:
	super._on_quest_updated(quest)
	# Mở cổng đấu trường ngay khi nhiệm vụ hoàn thành
	if magic_gate:
		magic_gate.update_quest_status(quest)

func _on_retry_requested() -> void:
	super._on_retry_requested()

	# Xóa toàn bộ projectile của Boss và Player khi Retry
	clear_all_projectiles()

	# Xử lý Retry khi tử trận trong trận Boss
	if boss_battle_started and not boss_defeated:
		boss_battle_started = false
		if arena_barrier:
			arena_barrier.visible = false
			var b_col := arena_barrier.get_node_or_null("CollisionShape2D") as CollisionShape2D
			if b_col:
				b_col.disabled = true
		if boss_lunareth:
			boss_lunareth.reset_boss()
		if game_ui:
			game_ui.hide_boss_hud()


===================================================

===================================================
FILE: src\world\test_level.gd
===================================================

extends Node2D

@onready var player: Player = $Player
@onready var state_label: Label = $CanvasLayer/HUD/StateLabel
@onready var hp_bar: ProgressBar = $CanvasLayer/HUD/VBoxBars/HPBar
@onready var mp_bar: ProgressBar = $CanvasLayer/HUD/VBoxBars/MPBar
@onready var pause_controller: PauseController = $PauseController

func _ready() -> void:
	# Gameplay level đặt PROCESS_MODE_PAUSABLE
	process_mode = Node.PROCESS_MODE_PAUSABLE

	if player:
		# Thêm Player vào group "players" để Enemy dễ nhận diện
		player.add_to_group("players")
		player.state_changed.connect(_on_player_state_changed)
		_on_player_state_changed("IDLE")

		var health_comp: HealthComponent = player.get_node_or_null("HealthComponent")
		if health_comp:
			health_comp.health_changed.connect(_on_player_health_changed)
			health_comp.mana_changed.connect(_on_player_mana_changed)
			_on_player_health_changed(health_comp.current_health, health_comp.max_health)
			_on_player_mana_changed(health_comp.current_mana, health_comp.max_mana)

func _on_player_state_changed(new_state: String) -> void:
	if state_label:
		state_label.text = "State: %s" % new_state

func _on_player_health_changed(cur: float, max_v: float) -> void:
	if hp_bar:
		hp_bar.max_value = max_v
		hp_bar.value = cur

func _on_player_mana_changed(cur: float, max_v: float) -> void:
	if mp_bar:
		mp_bar.max_value = max_v
		mp_bar.value = cur

func _on_resume_button_pressed() -> void:
	var ctrl = pause_controller if pause_controller else get_node_or_null("PauseController")
	if ctrl:
		ctrl.resume()
	else:
		get_tree().paused = false
		var menu = get_node_or_null("CanvasLayer/PauseMenu")
		if menu:
			menu.visible = false

func _on_quit_button_pressed() -> void:
	get_tree().quit()

class_name VharokOutpost
extends BaseZone

## Vharok — Tiền Đồn Quỷ tộc (Milestone M6)
## Vùng đất Quỷ tộc bán thú: Kiến trúc đá đen/tím/đỏ, NPC Rhaek,
## cổng ma pháp trở về Thánh Lâm Elyndra.

var npc_rhaek: NPC

func _init() -> void:
	zone_id = "vharok_outpost"
	zone_display_name = "Vharok — Tiền Đồn Quỷ tộc"

func _ready() -> void:
	init_zone_references()
	super._ready()

func init_zone_references() -> void:
	init_base_references()
	if npc_rhaek == null:
		npc_rhaek = get_node_or_null("NPC_Rhaek") as NPC

===================================================

class_name ZoneDefinition
extends RefCounted

## Định nghĩa danh mục và thông số các vùng đất trong NOCTELYRA (Milestone M6)
## Cung cấp metadata, định danh duy nhất và đường dẫn scene chuẩn.

const ZONE_NOCTIS_FOREST: String = "noctis_forest"
const ZONE_ELYNDRA_GLADE: String = "elyndra_glade"
const ZONE_VHAROK_OUTPOST: String = "vharok_outpost"

const ZONES: Dictionary = {
	ZONE_NOCTIS_FOREST: {
		"zone_id": ZONE_NOCTIS_FOREST,
		"display_name": "Rừng Noctis",
		"sub_title": "Bìa Rừng Hắc Nguyệt — Làng Ánh Nguyệt",
		"scene_path": "res://src/world/noctis_forest.tscn",
		"default_spawn": "spawn_default"
	},
	ZONE_ELYNDRA_GLADE: {
		"zone_id": ZONE_ELYNDRA_GLADE,
		"display_name": "Elyndra — Thánh Lâm Tinh Linh",
		"sub_title": "Vùng Đất Thánh Của Tinh Linh Tinh Mạch",
		"scene_path": "res://src/world/elyndra_glade.tscn",
		"default_spawn": "spawn_from_noctis"
	},
	ZONE_VHAROK_OUTPOST: {
		"zone_id": ZONE_VHAROK_OUTPOST,
		"display_name": "Vharok — Tiền Đồn Quỷ tộc",
		"sub_title": "Lãnh Địa Gia Tộc Hắc Nham — Quỷ Tộc Bán Thú",
		"scene_path": "res://src/world/vharok_outpost.tscn",
		"default_spawn": "spawn_from_elyndra"
	}
}

static func is_valid_zone(zone_id: String) -> bool:
	return ZONES.has(zone_id)

static func get_zone_display_name(zone_id: String) -> String:
	if ZONES.has(zone_id):
		return ZONES[zone_id].get("display_name", zone_id)
	return zone_id

static func get_zone_sub_title(zone_id: String) -> String:
	if ZONES.has(zone_id):
		return ZONES[zone_id].get("sub_title", "")
	return ""

static func get_zone_scene_path(zone_id: String) -> String:
	if ZONES.has(zone_id):
		return ZONES[zone_id].get("scene_path", "")
	return "res://src/world/noctis_forest.tscn"

static func get_default_spawn(zone_id: String) -> String:
	if ZONES.has(zone_id):
		return ZONES[zone_id].get("default_spawn", "spawn_default")
	return "spawn_default"


===================================================
FILE: src\world\zone_manager.gd
===================================================

class_name ZoneManager
extends RefCounted

## Trình điều phối Quản lý Khu vực & Dịch chuyển Thế giới (Milestone M6)
## Quản lý trạng thái chuyển scene, bảo toàn dữ liệu Player (HP/MP/Inv/Spellbook/Gold/Quest)
## và lưu trữ trạng thái cục bộ của từng vùng đất (pickups, quái đã diệt, boss).

static var current_zone_id: String = "noctis_forest"
static var current_spawn_id: String = "spawn_default"
static var initial_race_id: String = "human"
static var is_transitioning: bool = false
static var is_zone_traveling: bool = false
static var travel_cooldown: float = 0.0

static var session_player_data: Dictionary = {}
static var zone_states: Dictionary = {
	"noctis_forest": {},
	"elyndra_glade": {},
	"vharok_outpost": {}
}

static func is_cooling_down() -> bool:
	return travel_cooldown > 0.0

static func update_cooldown(delta: float) -> void:
	if travel_cooldown > 0.0:
		travel_cooldown = max(0.0, travel_cooldown - delta)

static func get_current_zone_id(world: Node = null) -> String:
	if world and "zone_id" in world and not str(world.get("zone_id")).is_empty():
		return str(world.get("zone_id"))
	return current_zone_id

static func get_zone_scene_path(zone_id: String) -> String:
	return ZoneDefinition.get_zone_scene_path(zone_id)

static func get_zone_display_name(zone_id: String) -> String:
	return ZoneDefinition.get_zone_display_name(zone_id)

static func reset_session() -> void:
	current_zone_id = "noctis_forest"
	current_spawn_id = "spawn_default"
	initial_race_id = "human"
	is_transitioning = false
	is_zone_traveling = false
	travel_cooldown = 0.0
	session_player_data.clear()
	zone_states = {
		"noctis_forest": {},
		"elyndra_glade": {},
		"vharok_outpost": {}
	}

static func extract_player_state(world: Node) -> Dictionary:
	if world == null:
		return {}

	var player: Player = world.get_node_or_null("Player") as Player
	var quest_mgr: QuestManager = world.get_node_or_null("QuestManager") as QuestManager
	if player == null:
		return {}

	var p_health := player.health_component
	var p_inv := player.inventory_component
	var p_eq := player.equipment_component
	var p_sb := player.spellbook_component
	var p_journal := player.quest_journal_component
	var p_stats := player.player_stats_component
	var p_prog := player.progression_component

	return {
		"race_id": p_stats.race_id if p_stats else "human",
		"level": p_prog.level if p_prog else 1,
		"current_exp": p_prog.current_exp if p_prog else 0,
		"claimed_exp_sources": p_prog.claimed_exp_sources.duplicate() if p_prog else [],
		"current_health": p_health.current_health if p_health else 100.0,
		"current_mana": p_health.current_mana if p_health else 60.0,
		"max_health": p_health.max_health if p_health else 100.0,
		"max_mana": p_health.max_mana if p_health else 60.0,
		"gold": quest_mgr.gold if quest_mgr else 0,
		"inventory": p_inv.to_dict() if p_inv else [],
		"equipment": p_eq.to_dict() if p_eq else {},
		"spellbook": p_sb.to_dict() if p_sb else {},
		"quest_journal": p_journal.to_dict() if p_journal else {},
		"quest": {
			"state": int(quest_mgr.current_quest.current_state) if quest_mgr and quest_mgr.current_quest else 0,
			"current_amount": int(quest_mgr.current_quest.current_amount) if quest_mgr and quest_mgr.current_quest else 0
		},
		"killed_enemy_ids": quest_mgr._killed_enemy_ids.duplicate() if quest_mgr else []
	}

static func apply_player_state(world: Node, data: Dictionary) -> void:
	if world == null or data.is_empty():
		return

	var player: Player = world.get_node_or_null("Player") as Player
	var quest_mgr: QuestManager = world.get_node_or_null("QuestManager") as QuestManager
	var ui: GameUI = world.get_node_or_null("GameUI") as GameUI
	if player == null:
		return

	# 0. Khôi phục Chủng tộc & Cấp độ / EXP TRƯỚC TIÊN (M8)
	var p_stats := player.player_stats_component
	var p_prog := player.progression_component

	if p_stats and data.has("race_id"):
		p_stats.set_race(str(data["race_id"]))

	if p_prog:
		p_prog.from_dict({
			"level": int(data.get("level", 1)),
			"current_exp": int(data.get("current_exp", 0)),
			"claimed_exp_sources": data.get("claimed_exp_sources", [])
		})

	# 1. Nạp Trang bị & Túi đồ
	var p_eq := player.equipment_component
	if p_eq and data.has("equipment") and (data["equipment"] is Dictionary):
		p_eq.load_from_dict(data["equipment"])

	var p_inv := player.inventory_component
	if p_inv and data.has("inventory") and (data["inventory"] is Array):
		p_inv.load_from_dict(data["inventory"])

	# Tính lại Max Stats thống nhất sau khi nạp Race, Level và Gear
	if p_stats:
		p_stats.recalculate_stats(false)

	# 2. Nạp Sách Phép
	var p_sb := player.spellbook_component
	if p_sb and data.has("spellbook") and (data["spellbook"] is Dictionary):
		p_sb.from_dict(data["spellbook"])

	# 2b. Nạp Sổ Nhiệm Vụ (M7)
	var p_journal := player.quest_journal_component
	if p_journal and data.has("quest_journal") and (data["quest_journal"] is Dictionary):
		p_journal.from_dict(data["quest_journal"])

	# 3. Nạp Máu và Mana hiện tại (GIỮ NGUYÊN CHÍNH XÁC, không tự hồi đầy!)
	var p_health := player.health_component
	if p_health:
		if data.has("current_health"):
			p_health.current_health = clamp(float(data["current_health"]), 0.0, p_health.max_health)
		if data.has("current_mana"):
			p_health.current_mana = clamp(float(data["current_mana"]), 0.0, p_health.max_mana)
		p_health.emit_signal("health_changed", p_health.current_health, p_health.max_health)
		p_health.emit_signal("mana_changed", p_health.current_mana, p_health.max_mana)

	# 4. Nạp Vàng và Nhiệm vụ
	if quest_mgr:
		if data.has("gold"):
			quest_mgr.gold = max(0, int(data["gold"]))
			quest_mgr.emit_signal("gold_changed", quest_mgr.gold)
		if data.has("quest") and (data["quest"] is Dictionary):
			var q_data: Dictionary = data["quest"]
			if quest_mgr.current_quest:
				quest_mgr.current_quest.current_state = int(q_data.get("state", 0)) as QuestData.State
				quest_mgr.current_quest.current_amount = int(q_data.get("current_amount", 0))
		if data.has("killed_enemy_ids") and (data["killed_enemy_ids"] is Array):
			quest_mgr._killed_enemy_ids = data["killed_enemy_ids"].duplicate()

	# 5. Cập nhật HUD UI
	if ui:
		if p_health:
			ui.update_player_hp(p_health.current_health, p_health.max_health)
			ui.update_player_mp(p_health.current_mana, p_health.max_mana)
		if quest_mgr:
			ui.update_gold(quest_mgr.gold)
			if quest_mgr.current_quest:
				ui.update_quest_info(quest_mgr.current_quest)
		if p_sb and ui.spell_hotbar:
			ui.spell_hotbar.set_spellbook(p_sb)
		if player and ui.inventory_ui:
			ui.inventory_ui.setup_player(player)

static func save_zone_state(zone_id: String, world: Node) -> void:
	if world == null or zone_id.is_empty():
		return

	var state: Dictionary = {
		"collected_pickup_ids": world.get("collected_pickup_ids") if "collected_pickup_ids" in world else [],
		"defeated_enemy_ids": world.get("defeated_enemy_ids") if "defeated_enemy_ids" in world else [],
		"dropped_pickups": world.get_dropped_pickups_data() if world.has_method("get_dropped_pickups_data") else []
	}

	if zone_id == "noctis_forest":
		state["boss"] = {
			"activated": world.get("boss_battle_started") if "boss_battle_started" in world else false,
			"defeated": world.get("boss_defeated") if "boss_defeated" in world else false,
			"reward_claimed": world.get("boss_reward_claimed") if "boss_reward_claimed" in world else false
		}

	zone_states[zone_id] = state

static func restore_zone_state(zone_id: String, world: Node) -> void:
	if world == null or not zone_states.has(zone_id):
		return

	var state: Dictionary = zone_states[zone_id]
	if state.is_empty():
		return

	var collected: Array = state.get("collected_pickup_ids", [])
	var dropped: Array = state.get("dropped_pickups", [])
	var defeated: Array = state.get("defeated_enemy_ids", [])

	if world.has_method("restore_world_state"):
		world.restore_world_state(collected, dropped, defeated)

	if zone_id == "noctis_forest" and world.has_method("restore_boss_state"):
		var b_dict: Dictionary = state.get("boss", {})
		var b_act: bool = bool(b_dict.get("activated", false))
		var b_def: bool = bool(b_dict.get("defeated", false))
		var b_rew: bool = bool(b_dict.get("reward_claimed", false))
		world.restore_boss_state(b_act, b_def, b_rew)

static func travel_to_zone(target_zone_id: String, target_spawn_id: String, current_world: Node) -> bool:
	if is_transitioning:
		return false
	if not ZoneDefinition.is_valid_zone(target_zone_id):
		push_error("ZoneManager: target_zone_id không hợp lệ: " + target_zone_id)
		return false

	var player: Player = null
	if current_world:
		player = current_world.get_node_or_null("Player") as Player
	if player and player.health_component and not player.health_component.is_alive():
		return false

	# 1. Lưu trạng thái thế giới hiện tại và trích xuất dữ liệu Player
	var cur_id := get_current_zone_id(current_world)
	save_zone_state(cur_id, current_world)
	session_player_data = extract_player_state(current_world)

	# 2. Thiết lập cờ trạng thái
	is_transitioning = true
	is_zone_traveling = true
	current_zone_id = target_zone_id
	current_spawn_id = target_spawn_id if not target_spawn_id.is_empty() else ZoneDefinition.get_default_spawn(target_zone_id)

	# 3. Tiến hành đổi scene
	var scene_path := get_zone_scene_path(target_zone_id)
	var tree: SceneTree = null
	if current_world and current_world.get_tree():
		tree = current_world.get_tree()

	if tree:
		tree.change_scene_to_file(scene_path)
		return true

	return false

static func on_zone_entered(world: Node) -> void:
	if world == null:
		return

	var z_id := get_current_zone_id(world)
	current_zone_id = z_id

	# 1. Nếu đang trong tiến trình dịch chuyển giữa các khu vực: Áp dụng dữ liệu bảo toàn
	if is_zone_traveling:
		apply_player_state(world, session_player_data)
		restore_zone_state(z_id, world)

		# Đặt Player vào đúng vị trí ZoneSpawnPoint
		_place_player_at_spawn(world, current_spawn_id)

		is_zone_traveling = false
		is_transitioning = false
		travel_cooldown = 1.5 # Grace cooldown chống ping-pong lặp tức thì
	else:
		# Vào khu vực thông thường: phục hồi trạng thái khu vực nếu có sẵn trong bộ nhớ
		if zone_states.has(z_id) and not zone_states[z_id].is_empty():
			restore_zone_state(z_id, world)

	# 2. Cập nhật UI thông tin khu vực
	var ui: GameUI = world.get_node_or_null("GameUI") as GameUI
	if ui:
		if ui.has_method("set_current_zone"):
			ui.set_current_zone(z_id)
		if ui.has_method("show_zone_banner"):
			ui.show_zone_banner(get_zone_display_name(z_id))

static func _place_player_at_spawn(world: Node, spawn_id: String) -> void:
	var player: Player = world.get_node_or_null("Player") as Player
	if player == null:
		return

	var target_pos := Vector2.ZERO
	var facing := 1
	var found := false

	# Tìm kiếm node ZoneSpawnPoint có spawn_point_id khớp
	var spawn_points = world.get_tree().get_nodes_in_group("zone_spawn_points")
	for sp in spawn_points:
		if sp is ZoneSpawnPoint and sp.spawn_point_id == spawn_id:
			target_pos = sp.global_position
			facing = sp.facing_direction
			found = true
			break

	# Nếu không tìm thấy spawn cụ thể, tìm spawn_default
	if not found:
		for sp in spawn_points:
			if sp is ZoneSpawnPoint and sp.spawn_point_id == "spawn_default":
				target_pos = sp.global_position
				facing = sp.facing_direction
				found = true
				break

	if found:
		player.global_position = target_pos
		player.spawn_position = target_pos
		player.facing_direction = facing
		if player.sprite_root:
			player.sprite_root.scale.x = float(facing)

		# Cập nhật camera ngay lập tức tới vị trí spawn mới (chống trượt hoặc nhìn ngoài biên)
		var cam: Camera2D = world.get_node_or_null("Camera2D") as Camera2D
		if cam:
			cam.global_position = target_pos
			cam.reset_smoothing()


===================================================
FILE: src\world\zone_portal.gd
===================================================

class_name ZonePortal
extends Area2D

## Cổng Dịch chuyển Khu vực (Zone Portal) cho NOCTELYRA (Milestone M6)
## Quản lý phát hiện người chơi, điều kiện mở khóa, hiển thị nhãn và chuyển cảnh qua ZoneManager.

@export var portal_id: String = ""
@export var portal_name: String = "Cổng Dịch Chuyển"
@export var target_zone_id: String = "elyndra_glade"
@export var target_spawn_id: String = "spawn_from_noctis"
@export var is_locked: bool = false
@export var lock_reason: String = "Cổng ma pháp chưa được khai mở."
@export var portal_color: Color = Color(0.3, 0.8, 1.0, 0.8)

@onready var prompt_label: Label = $PromptLabel
@onready var visual_vortex: Node2D = $VisualVortex

var _player_in_range: bool = false
var _anim_time: float = 0.0

func _ready() -> void:
	add_to_group("zone_portals")
	collision_layer = 0
	collision_mask = 2 # Chỉ tương tác với Player (Layer 2)

	body_entered.connect(_on_body_entered)
	body_exited.connect(_on_body_exited)

	_update_prompt_text()
	if prompt_label:
		prompt_label.visible = false

func _process(delta: float) -> void:
	# Hiệu ứng xoay / xung nhịp nhẹ của vòng ma pháp
	_anim_time += delta * 2.0
	if visual_vortex:
		visual_vortex.rotation += delta * 1.5
		var s: float = 1.0 + sin(_anim_time * 2.5) * 0.06
		visual_vortex.scale = Vector2(s, s)

func _update_prompt_text() -> void:
	if prompt_label:
		var dest_name := ZoneDefinition.get_zone_display_name(target_zone_id)
		prompt_label.text = "[E] Dịch chuyển đến %s" % dest_name

func is_interactable() -> bool:
	return _player_in_range and not ZoneManager.is_cooling_down()

func can_use(player: Player, world: Node) -> Dictionary:
	if player == null:
		return {"can_use": false, "reason": "Không tìm thấy người chơi!"}

	var p_health: HealthComponent = player.get_node_or_null("HealthComponent")
	if p_health and not p_health.is_alive():
		return {"can_use": false, "reason": "Người chơi đã tử vong!"}

	if ZoneManager.is_transitioning:
		return {"can_use": false, "reason": "Đang thực hiện dịch chuyển khu vực..."}

	if ZoneManager.is_cooling_down():
		return {"can_use": false, "reason": "Cổng đang hồi phục năng lượng..."}

	# Điều kiện đặc biệt: Cổng Moonveil Portal tại Rừng Noctis chỉ mở khi Boss Lunareth đã bị đánh bại
	if world:
		var cur_zone: String = ZoneManager.get_current_zone_id(world)
		if cur_zone == ZoneDefinition.ZONE_NOCTIS_FOREST and target_zone_id == ZoneDefinition.ZONE_ELYNDRA_GLADE:
			var b_def: bool = bool(world.get("boss_defeated")) if "boss_defeated" in world else false
			if not b_def:
				return {
					"can_use": false,
					"reason": "Cổng ma pháp chưa được khai mở. Hãy thanh tẩy Linh Lộc Lunareth trước!"
				}

	if is_locked:
		return {"can_use": false, "reason": lock_reason}

	return {"can_use": true, "reason": "OK"}

func trigger_interaction() -> void:
	var world := get_tree().current_scene if get_tree() else get_parent()
	var player: Player = null
	if world:
		player = world.get_node_or_null("Player") as Player
	if player == null and get_tree():
		var players := get_tree().get_nodes_in_group("players")
		if players.size() > 0:
			player = players[0] as Player

	var check := can_use(player, world)
	var ui := _get_game_ui()

	if not check.get("can_use", false):
		var reason_str: String = str(check.get("reason", lock_reason))
		if ui:
			ui.show_notification(reason_str)
		return

	# Thực hiện dịch chuyển qua ZoneManager
	ZoneManager.travel_to_zone(target_zone_id, target_spawn_id, world)

func _on_body_entered(body: Node) -> void:
	if body is Player:
		_player_in_range = true
		if prompt_label:
			_update_prompt_text()
			prompt_label.visible = true

func _on_body_exited(body: Node) -> void:
	if body is Player:
		_player_in_range = false
		if prompt_label:
			prompt_label.visible = false

func _get_game_ui() -> GameUI:
	var cur = get_tree().current_scene if get_tree() else null
	if cur:
		var ui = cur.get_node_or_null("GameUI") as GameUI
		if ui: return ui
	var p = get_parent()
	if p:
		var ui = p.get_node_or_null("GameUI") as GameUI
		if ui: return ui
	return null

extends Node2D

	add_to_group("zone_spawn_points")
